# Changelog

All notable changes to Tiger Studio Manager are documented here.
Versions follow [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

---

## v2.51.0 — 2026-11-05

### Added
- **Delete my account from Studio**: a "Delete my account" row at the bottom of the account window's Sign-in section opens an `openDialog` window (new art `tigersquad/squad-notice/tiger-goodbye`) listing what goes, then two steps: confirm it is you (password, or Google once more) and type the confirm word of the user's language; only then the red button arms, held 2.5 s. It calls the backend `deleteMyAccount` (same full wipe as the admin's, sign-in under 5 min, admin accounts refused), which now answers at once: the sign-in is disabled and the wipe runs in the background (`accountDeletionRun` on `accountDeletions/{uid}`, retried until every step passed; deployed). Studio removes the account from the computer right away (`_openDeleteAccount`, `_reauthForDelete`, `#eacDeleteAccountBtn`, `.eac-delete-*` in `60-modals.css`). i18n `eacDelete*` (22 keys, 11 locales).
- **Klipper: Happy Hare gates and AFC lanes editable** from the Filament card: the printers' filament sheet (material chips, colour palette, Apply) writes with the machine's own macro (`MMU_GATE_MAP` / `SET_MATERIAL` + `SET_COLOR`) then re-reads (`renderer/printers/klipper/filament-edit.js`, `cards.js`, `plugin.js`, `klipper.css`, `PROTOCOL.md`).
- **Demo printers**: the Klipper demo serves Moonraker's WebSocket `/websocket` (JSON-RPC 2.0: identify, objects list / query / subscribe, gcode script, `notify_proc_stat_update` every second) on its HTTP address, for the TigerSpool and the Tiger Touch; the Voron 2.4 plays Klipper + Happy Hare with a 4-gate ERCF (`services/demo/faces/klipper.js`, `shapes.js`). Demo printers of a model we filmed show that printer's camera while idle: ~3 s looped clips (A1, Ender-3 V4, Centauri Carbon 2, U1, Kobra X), stills for the X1C and AD5X (`services/demo/idle/`, `library.js` `IDLE_PHOTOS`; `package.json` `asarUnpack`).
- **Invite banner**: every envelope of the banner wiggles on click (`inviteScene`, `tapEgg`; referrals ctx gets `easterEgg` / `eggFound` / `toast`; rule `easter-eggs.md`; backend deploy pending).
- **Country picker window** (Account modal, Countries League): the dropdown becomes the current flag plus a "Change" button (hidden while locked) opening an `openDialog` window: the tiger holding a globe (`tiger-globe`, art ladder), the current country pinned, a search, every country as a round-flag tile in two columns, then "Join the <country> league" held 1.5 s (`holdToFire`) and "Keep my flag". Nothing is written before the hold completes (`_openSquadCountryPicker`, `_squadCountryOptions`, `#eacSquadCountryCur`, `#eacSquadCountryBtn`, `.eac-country`, `.eac-cpick-*`). i18n `squadCountry{Change,PickTitle,PickSub,Current,Search,Detected,LockTitle,PickWarn,Confirm,PickNone,Keep}`.
- **Countries League, 30-day flag lock**: the flag moves at most once every 30 days (first flag and first change free), enforced where the flag is published (backend `squadLock.js` `lockedCountry`, `publishSupporter`, `userProfiles.tigerSquad.countryAt`; deployed); the account window shows the date it opens again (`_fillSquadCountry`, `#eacSquadCountryHint`). i18n `squadCountryLockHint`, `squadCountryLockedUntil`.
- **Treasure hunt: ownership percentage** on every sticker (info bubble and card), including those still to win and the veiled ones (0 % when nobody holds it). A veiled slot of the catalogue carries an opaque key `o` (backend `tool/trophy-art.mjs` `slotKey`; catalogue rebuilt and deployed); Studio matches it (`slotKey`, `mapSlots`, `statId`).
- **Invite view networks**: WeChat, Messenger, LINE, KakaoTalk, VK and SMS added to the brand buttons (`NETS`, `COPY_NETS`, `_socialShareUrl`: line, vk, sms; logos `assets/svg/logos/logo_{wechat,messenger,line,kakaotalk,vk}.svg`, CC0). `main.js` `isSafeExternalUrl` allows `mailto:` and `sms:`.

### Changed
- **TigerSquad League**: a click on a country of "Ligue des pays" shows that league in the middle column (`squadBoard/nat_<CC>`, one read, kept 2 min; `pickLeague`, `ctx.natOf`, `_squadNatCache`, `.sqr-row--pick`); click on the own country goes back; Enter / Space too.
- **Invite view redone**: every network at once as icon-only buttons (name as aria-label; "Other networks" and the "Show the message" folds gone), "Copy my link" joined to the link field (`.rf-link-bar`), the message under the grid with a copy icon, the orange "My code" card beside the sharing (`.rf-share-body`, `.rf-mycode`: QR click = enlarge, code, Share link | Download, "New code" corner icon), "How it works" right under the banner with the steps drawn as a metro line (`.rf-stop`), "Copy my link" keeping its width when it turns into "Link copied!" (`.rf-copy-lbls`), "Your invitations" as a table (friend, joined, account created, e-mail confirmed, envelopes received; `inviteeRow`). Discord, WeChat, Messenger and KakaoTalk copy the message. The enlarged QR takes the card's dark rounded frame (`.rf-qr-xl`). Dead `.rf-qr*` / `.rf-code-*` rules removed.
- **"My code" card drawn by one helper**, `myCodeCardHtml` (`ui-kit.js`), in the Friends panel (`#fpMyCode`) and the Invite view; `regeneratePublicKey` shows its loading state on every card. Friends card compact: QR left, label and code right (`.fp-hero-top`), "New code" a round refresh icon (`.fp-hero-regen`, aria-label `regenLabel`), "Share link" and "Invite friends" side by side (container query under 300 px). "New code" is confirmed first (`_confirmRegenPublicKey`; `regenConfirmTitle`, `regenConfirmText`), then both cards update (`refreshReferrals`). The app behind the Friends cards is no longer darkened (`#friendsOverlay` transparent).
- **Invite links** are the referral tunnel `tigersystem.io/join/<code>` everywhere (Invite view `inviteUrl`, Friends "Share link" and QR, friend-view Share): `_joinShareUrl`; `_friendShareUrl` removed. The Hub page creates the account and records the referrer itself (`docs/REFERRALS.md`); backend `versionGate.js` lets `client=hub` through; `referrals` redeployed.
- **Spool details, Duplicate** refused on a spool with no container: the container picker opens and the red bubble (`_showErrorTip`, `#toolInfoPop`) points at its search (`toolDuplicateNeedContainer`).
- **Container picker and Spool details**: hovering a container photo shows it at 220 px beside it, whole on the card surface (`_showThumbZoom`, `data-zoom`, `.thumb-zoom--whole`).
- **Treasure hunt album**: the "Locked" tab splits into "With a hint" and "Hidden", each with its count (`hasHint`, `isHidden`; `trophyFilter_hint`, `trophyFilter_hidden` added, `trophyFilter_locked` removed). A sticker still to win that shows a hint opens its card (silhouette, mission, "Seen at <friend>'s" with the friend's `userAvatar`; no gloss, no download); hidden ones stay shut. Locked silhouettes are drawn as in a friend's album, as big as held stickers (`.is-locked`, `.trp-seen-av`).

### Fixed
- TigerSquad League "View all": the list scrolls inside its card, which keeps the other columns' height (`.is-open` + `--n`).
- Friends card back to 420 px (92 vw max): `.detail-panel { width: 300px }` won over `.friends-panel`; now a doubled class. On a short window the code card no longer shrinks (the column scrolls); its three buttons no longer spill out (`.fp-hero-btn`).
- Treasure hunt in a friend's view: a filter showed your own stickers; it re-rendered without the friend (`renderTrophiesView(host, friend)`).
- "Give an envelope": one window everywhere; the opening stage's older copy lacked the fanned hand; both build from `_giveOptions`.
- Demo printers take filament writes like the real machines and show them on the next read: FlashForge `/checkCode`, Snapmaker vendor + sub-type, Anycubic `…/<family>/report`, Creality vendor and external holder, Elegoo CC2 brand / name / temperatures and the canvas pushed to every client, Bambu `tray_info_idx` and temperatures; a slot a client wrote is no longer re-drawn (`services/demo/engine.js`, `faces/*.js`; tests `test-demo-klipper-ws.mjs`, `test-demo-filament-writes.mjs`; `docs/DEMO-MODE.md`).
- Backend (deployed): `streakFreeze` status reads friends in two field-masked `getAll`; `squadDeal` reads the profile and deals in parallel (deals cached 1 min per instance); `myReports` reads GitHub at most every 2 min (15 min once closed). Warm calls: requests ~430 → ~210 ms, freezes ~315 → ~250 ms, deals ~400 → ~360 ms.

### i18n
- Added `referralHowTitle`, `referralCol{Friend,Date,Account,Email,Envelopes}`, `referralCell{Yes,Waiting}`, `referralAppCopied`; reworded `referralStep1`, `referralStep1Sub`, `referralStep2Sub`; removed `referralShareVia`, `referralMore`, `referralLess`, `referralMsgShow`, `referralMsgHide`, `referralCodeInLink`, `referralDiscordCopy`, `referralDiscordCopied`, `referralRow{Validated,Pending,Expired}`, `referralOrCode`, `referralCopyCode`.

---

## v2.50.0 — 2026-11-02

### Added
- **Forced update for the TigerSquad**: when `config/studio.minSquadVersion` (world-readable) is newer than the build, the TigerSquad and Tiger Quests views and actions lock and open "The TigerSquad has evolved" (update / restart, or later). Every Cloud Functions call carries the build's version as `?v=` (a global `fetch` wrapper, `cloudfunctions.net` only) and a 426 `UPDATE_REQUIRED` locks it too (`_squadOutdated`, `_loadSquadMinVersion`, `_squadUpdateRequired`, `_openSquadUpdate`, gates in `_squadGate`, `_squadOnly`, `_enforceSquadGate`, `setViewMode`). Backend: `functions/versionGate.js` on tigerCoins, streakFreeze, squadDeal, dailyQuest, referrals. Art `tiger-update` (laddered). i18n `squadUpdate{Kicker,Title,Text,Btn}`.
- **Leaving the TigerSquad costs 30 days**: switching it off opens a window listing what goes (TigerCoins actions, streak freezes, +150 XP, badge and level, the TigerSquad space) and what stays — "I'm staying" or a 1.5 s hold to leave; a member back within the 30 days is in the squad but its views and actions open "Your access is back in N days" (date + day-of-30 bar) (`_openSquadLeaveWarning`, `_openSquadLockInfo`, `_squadActive`, `_squadLockDays`; `.sqn-*`). Backend: `users/{uid}.squadLockedUntil` (server-only), written on leaving by `publishSupporter`, cleared hourly by `squadLockSweep`; `squadActive` (`functions/squadLock.js`) gates the badge, the +150 XP, TigerCoins (`SQUAD_LOCKED`), freezes and tiers. A tiger on each window (`squad-notice/tiger-{leave,back}`).
- **"How it works" windows for streaks and freezes**: a new ⓘ on the streak card and the freeze card's ⓘ open a `openDialog` ceremony with the picture (flame / freeze cube) swelling behind from the top (`backfire` `at: "top"`, `.bk-fire--top`). Streak rules (a day = the full set, a milestone every 5 days × the combo, combo up to ×5, a missed day resets both, milestone table with the member's combo highlighted, Gold / Platinum relight) show the real pill, flame, badge, cube; freeze rules (slots by badge, melt rule) end on a cubes-only table (`streakRulesHtml`, `freezeRuleHtml`, `[data-qrules]`, `.qsr-*`, `.qsr-table--freeze`). Combo badges take the rarity colours ×1…×5 (`.qd-combo--1…5`). i18n `questsRules{Ok,How}`, `questsStreakRule{Day,Palier,Combo,Miss,Repair}`, `questsStreakTableTitle`, `questsStreakCol{Day,Combo,Bonus}`.
- **Give envelopes: choose which, several at once**: the give window shows up to 10 envelopes drawn from the pile (`_givePile`: gift envelopes by origin and the chips' "Surprise", contents never shown) as a fanned hand (`.tc-give-envs`, `--r` / `--y`); check up to the day's allowance, pick a friend, hold Give. `giveEnvelope` takes `ids` (≤ 10). Several received at once arrive as ONE `envelope_gift` notice with `data.count`: `showEnvelopeGift` fans three envelopes behind the card turning toward the mouse (`.tch-env-pile`, `tilt` sets `--tx` / `--ty`) and "Open them all" opens the pile; the bell reads `notifEnvelopeGiftMany`. i18n `envGivePickEnv`, `envGiveSentMany`, `envGiveChip`, `envGiveEgg`, `envGiveGift`, `envGift{Title,Text,See}Many`.
- **Requests** (issue 181): a Copy button on every message of a conversation (`copyMsg`, `.rep-msg-copy`); the "All requests" tab counts the open ones, loaded as soon as a TigerMaster / admin opens the window; the team gets a "Requests" tab in the notification centre (six tabs in two rows, `.nc-tabs--grid`) with a "New request #n" card that opens it in the team view (`request_new`, `teamReport`, `openReport({ tab: "all" })`; backend `reportIssue` notifies every TigerMaster / admin).
- **Treasure hunt** (the trophies view, renamed; `trophiesTitle`, `trophiesFriendTitle`): the banner becomes an explorer's temple (CSS stone blocks over the wall colour, torch, swaying vines, tiger with backpack and flashlight, treasure map, open relic; `TROPHY_SCENE`, `assets/img/trophies/banner-explorer/`); the count card reads "N stickers collected" with no total and no bar (`trophyStuckLabel`; `trophyCount` removed). A friend's sticker album in their TigerSquad view (read-only, `renderTrophiesView(host, friend)`). Veiled entries show "?" and a tier until a friend holds them, then "Seen at <friend>'s" (`_reveals`, `tigertag.trophyReveals.<uid>`, `trophySeenAt`). A band of light sweeps every earned sticker (`.trp-gloss--tile`); the unlock window waits 2.2 s before it can be closed (`UNLOCK_LOCK_MS`).
- **Launch splash redone**: a local page `renderer/splash/` drawing the welcome page's ecosystem scene (now shared: `css/72-hero-scene.css`, `hero-scene.js` `fillHeroScenes`), "Tiger Studio Manager 2", a loading line in the user's language (`splashLoading`, language kept in `userData/splash.json`), a bar fed by the real startup steps (`splashStep`) and a fade into the main window.
- **Invite view**, four passes: the reward in the title, three numbered steps, one orange "Copy my link", WhatsApp / Discord copy / e-mail with the rest under "More", the code second, QR in a narrow column (Enlarge → the big QR on the celebration frame), "Message to share", "Confirmed friends" with an ⓘ, "A friend invited you?" as one card (code field with Paste, days-left pill, "I don't have a code"), a "+3" badge on the Invite button, banner scene of two tigers passing an envelope (`INVITE_SCENE`, `invite-hero/`). Many `referral*` keys added, reworded or removed.
- **`openDialog()`** (`renderer/dialog.js`, rule `.agents/rules/dialogs.md`): one window helper with the "notice" and "ceremony" looks, owning cross, Escape, outside click, focus and motion; the leave / return windows, "Your request moved" (tiger holding a ticket) and the invite's big QR moved onto it (`.dlg-*` in `60-modals.css`).
- **Illustrations at the size they are painted**: `npm run art:build` (`scripts/build-art-ladder.mjs`, `sharp`) makes `-{128,256,512,1024}.webp` and `renderer/art-ladder.js`; `artImg()` (`renderer/art.js`) draws them with srcset + `sizes="auto"`; `art:check` in the pre-commit. Rules in `docs/ART-DIRECTION.md`.
- Agent rules: `paint-last-known.md`, `dialogs.md`; `token-efficiency.md` and `release.md` extended.

### Changed
- **Send TigerCoins**: at least 20 coins per envelope (`minAmount`) and one envelope a day to the same friend; friends already sent to today are greyed "Sent today", quick amounts start at 20, errors `SEND_MIN` / `SENT_TODAY` (`showSendCoins`, `.tcs-f.is-sent-today`; backend `sendCoins`, `sentToday`).
- **Freeze card**: no "Give" button any more — each cube still to give is the button (`.qf-gift[data-qf="give"]`), the window is titled "Give a freeze"; streak and freeze cards share one head (`.qd-card-head`, picture at 44 px, title, ⓘ); a freeze already given leaves a dashed gift cube; the Combo badge sits under the week's pills.
- **Trophies off the open-source app**: Studio ships no trophy art, text or list. The album reads the public catalogue on the cdn (`tigertag.trophyCatalog.<lang>`, families included: `familyName()`) and the cards of the trophies held (`trophyCards`, `tigertag.trophyCards.<uid>`); 276 `trophy_<id>` / `trophyHow_<id>` keys and 8 `trophyFamily_<family>` keys removed from the 11 locales; `docs/TROPHIES.md` keeps the mechanics only; rule `.agents/rules/trophies.md`. Trophy tiers, new batches (community milestones, printer brands, Bambu Lab transports, envelope piles) and their art live in the backend.
- **Trophy albums kept on this device**: yours (`tigertag.trophyAlbum.<id>`) paints at once and listens only to trophies with `earnedAt` after the last sync; friends' albums (`tigertag.friendTrophies.<id>`, 50 newest) paint at once and read only what is new; a REST COUNT catches a trophy taken back; percentages from one doc `trophyStats/_all`.
- **Cold launch reads only what changed**: `_listenSynced` keeps `tigertag.inv.<id>` / `tigertag.products.<id>` and their sync time and listens only to docs with `syncAt` after it (minus 1 h), plus one REST COUNT per collection (a mismatch → full re-read); Studio stamps `syncAt` on its own writes; copies older than 30 days are re-read in full.
- **Fewer XP recomputes**: `_recordUsage` batches telemetry fields of 4 s into one write; quest counters flush after 8 s (was 1.5 s) and on blur; a focus re-check-in at most hourly (was 10 min). Backend: add/remove-only scale and TigerSpool triggers, `xpTelemetry` recomputes only on a field the XP reads, repeat `dailyQuest` answered from stored docs, `tigerCoins` reads only chips still in the pile, `communityStats` full count every 10 min + live counters (`communityLive.js`) and now builds the squad boards.
- **TigerSquad leagues read the server's board** (`squadBoard/world|countries|nat_<CC>`, `squadRank/{uid}`; ≈ 6 reads + 20 history rows instead of every member): `_loadSquadBoard`, `_squadRank`, `_squadMemberCount`, `_loadLeagues`; backend `functions/squadBoard.js`, nightly `squadRanksNightly`.
- **Navigation draws at once**: a friend visit keeps the own listeners alive (`_ownStash`); the last 5 friends visited stay in memory (`_friendCache`, fresh for 3 min); TigerCoins `status` reused 30 s (`_coinStatus`); squad board kept 2 min; community history and league curves read once a day; XP log kept 10 min; a requests tab reopened within 20 s keeps its list. Measured on a full tour: 4,232 reads → 1,736; friend re-visit ~900 ms → ~200 ms.
- **View bar** paints every entry at start-up with the last session's figures (`_barCache`, `_barCachePatch`, header cache `bar`); rule `paint-last-known.md`.
- **Friend view header**: a friend's stock weight and value are no longer shown (counts only) and the own TigerCoins, readers, scales, TigerSpool and Tiger Touch icons step aside (`FRIEND_HIDDEN_STATS`, `body.is-friend-view`).
- **Game UI tokens** (`--game-*` in `00-base.css`, light and dark): Tiger Quests, TigerSquad, the album and the chest / envelope stages take their colours from one shared set; the local `--tsq-*` / `--qw-*` / `--sqr-*` palettes alias them.
- **Every illustration in HD**: 182 pictures of quests, TigerCoins, TigerSquad and trophies re-exported up to 1024 px (masters rebuilt in `assets-src/img/`); the four TigerSquad rank badges, the chests sticker and the TigerCoin faces redrawn.
- "Tutorial missions" is now "Missions" (`questsJourney`); the view bar's hover names are names only (`questsDailyHeading`; `questPillLabel` removed).

### Fixed
- Missions grid on a narrower window: cards squeezed six to a row, titles and badges broken word by word. `.inv-quests-view` is a container (`quests`): six cards from 1500 px of view, three below, two under 820 px; banner title shrinks under 1300 px.
- Light theme readability: grey text deepened (`--muted`, `--faint`, `--neutral`, `--game-muted`, `--ink-*`); new text-twin tokens (`--primary-text`, `--success-text`, `--warning-text`, `--danger-text`, `--info-text`, …) used for text in accent hues; daily-quest icons, category badges, Combo pills, hero banners (dark frame), Rankings rows, Workshop rarity words, XP history filters, Deals badges, the "Secret mission" card and completed chapter tiles read in Light.
- "All requests" showed requests closed on GitHub as open: the list re-reads the open ones on GitHub (`myReports` `all`, `syncReport`, at most every 2 min per instance).
- The light band across a sticker never showed (mask URL relative to the stylesheet, 404; `artUrl`).
- Two trophy stickers showed the wrong picture (the XP coin, the empty dashed cube).

## v2.49.0 — 2026-11-01

### Added
- **Trophies** (`docs/TROPHIES.md`): a sticker album in the Tiger Squad group (`#vtgSquadTrophies` after the Workshop, view mode `squadTrophies`, every account; `renderer/quests/trophies.js|css`, `initTrophies`). 116 trophies: action trophies in six families (flame and freezes, TigerCoins, Squad and friends, chips — TigerTag / TigerTag+ / TigerTag+ Certified —, workshop — TD1S, RFID reader, USB scale, mobile app, first printer / rack / list, updates —, Tiger devices), one per Workshop loot and feat (same rarity), 1st of one's country / 1st in the world at a daily ranking, tier trophies with pop-culture names (TigerCoins sent / received 100 / 1,000 / 10,000, envelopes sent / received 10 / 50 / 100, freezes sent / received 5 / 25 / 100), brand trophies for the TigerTag+ Certified sellers (Rosa3D, eSun, Sunlu, Jamg He, R3D, Landu, Lotactree, Smart3DPT — 1 / 10 / 50 / 100 certified chips, family "Brands", `cert_<brand>_<n>`; `XP_COUNT_VERSION` 3 recounts each account once so its certified chips get their brand), a "TigerMaster" trophy, hidden ones and event trophies (`trophyEvents`, newest first, codes through the TigerCoins code window, now open to every account; TROPHY_OWNED / NOT_IN_SQUAD explained, no "0 TigerCoins" toast for a trophy-only code). Banner through `heroHtml` (own desk scene `assets/img/trophies/banner/*.webp`, count per rarity on the desk, n / total card), Deals-style bar (All / Earned n / Locked n, search filtering in place, family filter `selectHtml`, "Have a code?"). Earned stickers die-cut from whole pictures (`assets/img/trophies/stickers/<id>.webp` 256 px, masters in `assets-src/img/trophies/`), tier colour (`--tier-*`, `--sticker-edge`), date and "owned by n % of tigers"; locked as silhouettes, hidden as "?", missed events dated; how-to in the shared `#toolInfoPop` bubble (`.trp-st-info`, `_DOC_TIP_SEL`). Sticker card in the `tc-reveal` + `tcx` frame: sticker following the pointer (`tilt`) with a shaped gloss, art behind the window (`backfire` `dur` / `soft`), "Download" (PNG, counted once per sticker server-side). Unlock window once per device (`tigertag.trophies.<id>`): rays, `backfire`, stamp or impact sound, queued. Bell notice `trophy`. Flat gold cup `assets/img/trophies/cup.webp` replaces `trophy.svg`; new `icon_trophy.svg` (`.icon-trophy`). Backend: `functions/trophyRules.js` (source of truth), `functions/trophies.js`, rules.
- **Referrals** (`docs/REFERRALS.md`): an Invite view in the Tiger Squad group (`#vtgSquadInvite`, view mode `squadInvite`, `renderer/quests/referrals.js|css`): friend link + Copy, share buttons (WhatsApp, X, Facebook, Reddit, Telegram, Bluesky, Discord = copy, e-mail; new `logo_{reddit,telegram,bluesky}.svg`), QR + download, "Who invited you?" while the account is under 7 days old, recruits with their state. Also reached from the Friends panel, the sidebar's "+ Invite" chip, the account dropdown and the empty friends list. Onboarding step 2 gets the optional "Who invited you?" field (prefilled by a friend link, Paste); a friend link opened by an account that can still claim goes there instead of Add friend. Validation = the invitee's e-mail verified within 7 days: 3 gift envelopes each, a ceremony per side, notifications `referral_joined` / `referral_validated`; referral trophies for both sides. Backend `functions/referrals.js` (claim / status / seen), rule `referrals/{inviteeUid}`, `COUNTERS.referrals`, `test/referrals.test.js`; Hub gift card and admin Referrals tab (`config/referrals`).
- **Send TigerCoins to a friend** (`showSendCoins` in `squad-deals.js`, `_sendCoinsTo`, `#btnSendCoins`, `.tcs-*`): the week's allowance by TigerSquad level (bronze 50 / silver 100 / gold 250 / platinum 500), members only (friends for 7 days), amount 10 / 25 / 50 / 100 / Max or typed, required message (140 chars, MESSAGE_REQUIRED), recap balance before → after, postage fee (0 % to start, destroyed), 2 s hold. The friend gets an envelope of the coins; the coin history shows "Sent to <friend>" with the fee, and bought freezes. Backend `tigerCoins.js` `sendCoins`, settings `config/tigerCoins.transfer` (Hub admin).
- A friend's envelope with a message opens as a **letter** (`showEnvelope` isLetter / `playLetter`, ≈ 2.5 s): avatar on the seal, seal snaps, parchment unrolls, message wiped in, signature + avatar stamp, coin rises and counts up; one synthesised sound per beat (`coin-sound.js` `waxSnap`, `flapOpen`, `unroll`, `stamp`, `letterCoins`, `depositTick`, `deposited`). "Collect" held 2 s flies the coins to the header counter (`flyCoins` exported from `coin-pull.js`, `#topCoinsBal.is-pulse`).
- **Report a friend's message** from the envelope window, with "Also block {name} and remove them from my friends" ticked by default (`_coinsCtx.reportMessage`, `.tc-env-report*`, `icon_flag.svg`). TigerMasters and admins resolve "Reported messages" at the top of "All requests" (Remove the message / Dismiss; `msgReportsHTML`, `resolveMsgReport`). Backend `tigerCoins.js` `reportMessage` → server-only `messageReports/{toUid_envelopeId}`, `myReports` `messageReports` / `resolveMessageReport`.
- Friends panel: each friend's received freeze slots (filled / free), a fillable slot opens the freeze window on that friend; "Give freezes" under "Give an envelope" (`renderFriendsList`, `#btnGiveFreeze`, `questFreezeStatus`, `questFriendFreeze`, `openFreezeFor`, `openFreezeGift`, `freeze-modal.js` `preselect` / `forFriend`, `.fp-freeze`).
- **Request conversations** (`renderer/report/index.js` `openThread`, `threadHTML`, `sendReply`, `_showAlert`; `replies.js` `unseenReplies`, `alertNews`): each request in My requests opens its GitHub issue's comments (team left with avatar, own right, notes centred); answer from the app without a GitHub account; close one's own request from it; unseen team replies flagged ("New reply"); a reply or status change opens an alert window once per change; open requests re-checked every 30 min; request / reply notifications open the conversation (`arg`). The answer box is the request form's (toolbar, drop zone, capture, PDFs, JSON, "Attach a printer's data", 5 files, chips in the thread). Backend `myReports` `thread` / `comment`.
- **All requests for every member** (read-only): questions accept answers from any member, posted as "Community" with their pseudo (10 per hour per question, reaches the reporter as a reply). TigerMasters (`roles: "tigermaster"`, server-only, granted from the Hub admin; `state.isTigerMaster`, ctx `isTeam`) and admins answer as the team ("Ptitlouis · TigerMaster"), see the reporter labelled, and Mark as done / Decline (`allHTML`, `syncAll`, `resolveThread`). Backend `myReports` `all`, `as: "team"`, `resolve`, `helperCommentBody` / `HELPER_MARK_RE`, `test/reportThread.test.js`.
- **Chest opening ceremony** (`showChest`, `.tcc-*`): layered chest art (`assets/img/tigercoins/chest/`) on a stage lit in its type's colour, held 2 s "Open the chest" (rattle, glowing seam), lock pops, lid flies, rays, coin fountain, envelopes rising one by one; contents count hidden; its own D-minor music (`chestMusicStart`, muted with the coins sound); sounds thud / rattle / unlock / burst / pop.
- Achievement "Chests opened": +200 XP per chest opened, kept for good (`XP_ACHIEVEMENTS`, `XP_CATS`, `docs/QUESTS.md` §4; backend `ACH_XP.chest`, trigger `xpChests`); the ladder shows every repeatable action's ×N from ×0 (`ladderHtml`).
- **Sign-in card** in My profile (issue 176; `#eacSigninGroup`, `_paintSignin`, `_signinReauth`, `.eac-signin-*`): the account's email and sign-in methods; an email + password account changes its address (verified by link) or its password after the current password; Google-only accounts are told their address is Google's; the address is re-read from Auth on each open.
- Lotactree masterspool containers, White 150 g and Transparent 194 g (`spools_filament.json`, PR 168 by Ptitlouis6012, issue 165).
- Demo printer "Elegoo Centauri Carbon (OpenCentauri COSMOS)" (Klipper model 166): a CANVAS of four lanes under both `AFC_stepper` and `AFC_lane`, chamber sensor, `led case` / `led hotend` (`services/demo/faces/klipper.js`, `shapes.js`, `docs/DEMO-MODE.md`).

### Changed
- Shared UI kit `renderer/quests/ui-kit.js` (`heroHtml`, `filterBarHtml`, `searchHtml`, `selectHtml`, `infoTipHtml` on `.ui-info[data-tip]`): the seven banners, the Deals / Trophies bars and the send / give friend searches use it, same look.
- Big moments in a window get the art behind it (`backfire` in `coin-pull.js`, `.bk-fire`): flame on relight, TigerCoin on collect, open chest on a full set; none under reduced motion.
- "Relight the flame" replaces streak repair (`freeze-modal.js` repair / `relight`, `REPAIR_DAYS`, `.qfm-mend`, `.qfm-flame`, `.qfm-rl-*`): TigerSquad badge above, the perk said plainly ("your broken streak comes back in full if you relight it today or tomorrow — once a month"), "Relight my 12-day streak"; a ceremony after the hold (embers, catch, flash, days ticking back, `sfx.relightRise` / `relightIgnite` / `relightDay`) over a fireplace crackle (`fireStart`, starting on hover of the strip). The glowing "Streak broken · Relight the flame" strip moved to the foot of the streak card (`repairStripHtml`).
- Drawn streak flames (`assets/img/quests/streak-flame{,-small}.webp`); freezes to give melt through five drawn stages (`freeze-melt-1…5.webp`, replacing the CSS squash; `freeze-melt.webp` unused), a dashed gift cube when none (`freeze-gift-empty.webp`); "Delivery in 4 days" / "Delivery in 20 h 47" instead of the next batch (`deliveryText`); the freeze rule in an ⓘ popover with a per-badge table (`freezeRuleHtml`, `FREEZE_CAPS`, `.qfp*`); "0/3" counts and the dashed "To give" box gone; buying = clicking an empty "Mine" slot (`.qf-slot-buy`).
- Freeze windows (buy / give / repair) in the send-coins style: friends in one row (search past 8, free slots as cubes, pick grows in gold, full / already-given friends left out), cube above the card and the button, one golden 2 s hold always pressable with a red `errorTip` bubble on what is missing, no Cancel; windows opened on a friend scroll it into view and keep it there (`showPicked`).
- "Daily set complete" window wherever you are (`fullset-modal.js`, `.qfs-*`): chest bursts open, bonus XP counts up, streak +1 with combo and palier bonus.
- Streak card: the current week as seven pills (lit / icy / dashed today / dim), from `quests/daily.history` with the live streak filling gaps (`streakWeek`, `.qd-pip`, `.qd-week`); a finished quest keeps its full bar; the chest card drops "Earned · 5/5".
- Send TigerCoins redesigned (amount as the hero, required message on a paper card, compact, fee only when non-zero); the TigerCoins history makes the balance the hero with a Send pill (`.tch-bal*`), no Earned / Spent totals; an opened chest has its own art (`chest-open.webp`).
- A friend's envelope opens in its own window (avatar on the gold seal, tilt, message, "Open it") instead of a card leading to Deals (`_announceEnvelopeGifts`, `sealAvatar`, `.tch-env-seal`).
- TigerCoins windows and the header paint cached figures at once, then patch what changed: `tigertag.coinlog.<id>` (100 newest), `tigertag.header.<id>` (stat cards, balance, envelopes, allowance); friends cache keeps TigerSquad membership, unread profiles read first (`_coinLog`, `_cacheFriends`, `_ensureFriendProfiles`, `renderStats`).
- Workshop: the four legendary devices open their ecosystem sheet (`[data-eco-open]`, `.qw-tile.is-eco`).
- "Later" on the requests alert snoozes it 30 min or to the next launch (`_snoozeTimer`); feedback panel 470 → 540 px; requests drawn as pills with white icons.
- Trophy card / unlock: rarity · family chip above the sticker, the sticker spilling over its halo.
- Agent rule `.agents/rules/trophies.md`.

### Fixed
- Initials-only avatars were pushed to the top-left in the send / freeze friend rows, the envelope seal and the letter stamp: all drawn with `userAvatar(…, "av-fill")` (`.av-fill`).
- A new, empty account saw an empty header; the cards now show at 0 (`renderStats`).
- The header's TigerCoins card was read once 6 s after sign-in; it now appears on joining the TigerSquad and hides on leaving (`_refreshTopCoins`, `_setSupportPublicly`, `syncUserDoc`).
- The week's pills stayed dim for a streak older than the server's day history.
- Plurals follow each language (`t()` uses `Intl.PluralRules`: one / few / many / other); real plural forms for `coinLog_chest`, `coinLog_surprises`, `coinLog_chests`, `questsStreak`, `squadDealsClaimDays`, `coinsSendErrRecent`, `questsFreezeSlotsFree`.
- A spool already linked to its own maker chips was offered "Link the N proprietary third-party Tags" for other chips on the TigerPOD; it is now greyed in Grouped spools (`_createGroupMemberCard`, `.gp-member--linked`).
- Feedback topic cards lost the left colour stripe (`.rep-kind-card`).
- The "Turn on debug mode" quest counted only at the next launch (`questGauge("debugMode", 1)`).
- Klipper AFC units listing each lane twice (`AFC_stepper` + `AFC_lane`, CANVAS on an OpenCentauri CC1) showed 8 slots for 4 lanes (issue 167; `f.afcLanes` / `f.afcObjs`, `PROTOCOL.md` §7d).

### Removed

## v2.48.0 — 2026-10-30

### Added
- Signed in with no spool, "Where do we start?" (`_paintInvStart`, `_onInvStartClick`): kicker "Welcome to your workshop 2.0", three cards opening the flow behind each first win — Add a spool (`openAddChooser`), Plug in my TigerPOD (the card turns green with "Your TigerPOD is ready · Waiting for a scan…" while a reader is plugged in, else the TigerPOD sheet — `_paintInvStartPod`) and Choose my printer (Printers view + `openPrinterBrandPicker`; TigerPOD and printer cards desktop-only) — and a Tiger NFC Connect line. Each card carries its picture top right (`.inv-start-art`): the spool, the TigerPOD, and a printer drawn at random from every brand catalogue, swapped every 2 s in two beats (old out, then new in — `_cycleInvStartPrinter`, held while the window is hidden). The search row and colour bars stay. The Printers view with no printer shows the same screen instead of its empty card. First spool → "One less spool to look for." toast.
- "Going further (optional)" strip above the spool list once that screen has been seen: TigerPOD and real printer steps, each with "I don't have one", ticked when done, gone when both are done/skipped or closed (`_renderFirstSteps`, `tigertag.firstSteps.<uid>` = on / closed / done + `.skip`, `#firstSteps`).
- One ecosystem window for the six products (`ECO_SHEETS`, `_renderEcoSheet`, `openEcoSheet`, `#ecoModalOverlay`, `.eco-*`): fixed 840 × 760 grid (840 × 552 two-column under 800 px of height), six equal text tabs (TigerPOD · TigerScale · TigerSpool · Tiger Touch · TigerNFC · TigerTag; ← → / Home / End), a scan bay (device on a tech grid, NFC waves, scan beam, prev / next), name + promise, a reserved live-state line (green only when plugged in / online), three benefits, the data path with light packets, "How to get one" in three steps, footer with the main action, "See the guide" (wiki) and an "Other links" menu. Autoplay carousel: a progress bar fills the open tab (4.5 s, `animationend` of `.eco-tab-prog`), held on hover, focus inside or menu open; switch = 160 ms fade-out then a two-beat ease-in. Opened by the reader icon, the first-steps cards, and the TigerScale / TigerSpool / Tiger Touch header icons while the account has none. The TigerPOD sheet presents the TigerPOD Mini only (one printed part + two ACR122U slid in).
- Account emails from TigerSystem: sign-up check, resend and password reset go through the backend's `authMail` (branded, in the app's language, from noreply@tigersystem.io, `tigersystem.io/auth/<token>` link), falling back to Firebase's mail in the app's language (`_authMail`, `_sendVerification`, `_setAuthLang`; zh → zh-CN, pt → pt-BR, pt-pt → pt-PT).
- "Check your inbox" step after an email sign-up or an unconfirmed sign-in: animated envelope, the address, "I've confirmed, sign in", "Send the email again" (once per 30 s), "Use another address" (`showCheckMail` / `hideCheckMail`, `#lmCheck`, `.lm-check*`).
- Product mockups in `assets/img/mockups/` (masters in `assets-src/img/mockups/`): `tigerpod-mini`, `tigerscale`, `tigerspool`, `tigertouch-tour`, `tigertag-spool`, `imac-frame` / `imac-studio`, `iphone-frame` / `iphone-connect`, `studio-screen`, `hero-ecosystem` (+ `-transparent`); flanges translucent, the three readers carrying the same spool.

### Changed
- Shown as **Tiger Studio Manager 2** (window title in `main.js` and `inventory.html`, sidebar "Studio Manager 2"); `productName`, update feed and user-data folder unchanged.
- Signed-out welcome redone as its own page (`.signin-ph-*`, `_syncSignedOutLook`): top bar with the name and a language select (`#signinLangs`, `_paintSigninLangs`), headline, four benefits (scan any RFID chip, printers, sharing, maker deals), "Create my account" (`openAddAccountModal(mode)`) / "I already have an account", "No TigerPOD needed"; an animated ecosystem scene (`_fillHeroScenes`, `[data-hero-scene]`): floating iMac, iPhone and Tiger Touch, the TigerPOD ⇄ TigerScale ⇄ TigerSpool flip around a shared spool hub; "Works with" + printer brand logos lit one after another (`_signinGarland`); header and view bar hidden while signed out (`body.signed-out`), sidebar too with no remembered account (`body.no-accounts`). The same scene in the sign-in dialog and the account setup's first step; paused while the window is hidden (`body.anim-paused`), still with reduced motion.
- First launch uses the system language when shipped (`_systemLang`: first OS match, pt-BR / pt → `pt`, other pt-XX → `pt-pt`, zh-* → `zh`), else English.
- `icon_printer.svg` is a real 3D printer.
- The TigerSpool and Tiger Touch discovery windows and the old TigerPOD window are replaced by the ecosystem sheets (`openTigerSpool` / `openTigerTouch` / `openTigerPodModal` route there; `spoolConnState`, `touchConnState`, `isScaleOnline`, `onScalesChanged` / `onSpoolsChanged` / `onTouchsChanged` feed the live line).

- Add-product side card: no rule under the header nor above Make.

### Fixed

- "From the catalogue" rebuilt every row per keystroke, reloading and flickering photos: rows keyed by product id, kept rows moved, only new ones built; a synced index rebuilds (`_catalogPickFill`, `_cpkFilledIndex`).
- A TigerData / TigerData+ with linked maker chips offered no "Burn the TigerTag(+)"; it now does (unlinked maker chips on the TigerPOD still turn it into "Link").
- The password-field eye toggled `-webkit-text-security`, ignored by `type="password"`; it now switches `password` / `text` (`makeEyeToggle`).
- Klipper logo was a letter K; now Klipper's own mark (`renderer/printers/klipper/img/logo.svg`).

### Removed

## v2.47.0 — 2026-10-29

### Added
- Maker chips (Bambu Lab, Creality… MIFARE 1K) are linked, never burned, on every creation path. "Add product" (manual) draws a held banner in place of Make by what sits on the TigerPOD (`_adpRenderFoot`, `#adpFootBanner`): unlinked maker chips → "Link the proprietary third-party Tag(s)" (creates the TigerData, then links them); a reader → "Burn the TigerTag"; no reader → plain Make. `saveAddProduct` / `_catalogCreate` / `_openCreatedSpool` take `linkMakers`. The catalogue product card offers "Link" when maker chips simply sit on the TigerPOD (`pendingMakerChips() || presentMakerChips()`); "+" with unlinked maker chips already present opens the maker-chip choices (`openAddChooser`).
- Live chip banners: Link / Burn follow what sits on the TigerPOD now — chips swapped while the new-chip card is up replace the earlier ones, the open view stays put (`_refreshChipBanners` on card-present / foreign-chip, `ctx.chipsChanged`; the held flush no longer reopens the chooser). The spool card's banner slot `.dp-chip-banner` is swapped alone (`_wireDetailChipBanner`). `pending` / `offer` store no chip list: a link takes `presentMakerChips()` at the hold. Link and Burn banners share one height (`.lc-offer-ic` in the burn logo's 52 px box).
- Inventory search matches a linked maker chip's UID, whole or partial, any case (`_rowChipUids`, read live outside the cached haystack; exact-code match too).
- TigerCoins in the header for TigerSquad members (`#topCoins`, painted by `_paintDealsBadge(n, coins)`): one flat card like the stat cards — balance (opens the history), envelopes waiting (opens the pile, or the "how to get envelopes" help at 0), gift (gift-code window); each switches to the Deals view first. `openEnvelopeHelp`, `openCoinsCodeModal`, `openCoinsHistory` exported from `squad-deals.js`; `_topCoinsCtx`.
- Mini colour bars in the catalogue pick card (`.cpk-hue`) with their own window (`state.cpkHueFilter`): `wireRangeBar` takes a group (`hueGroup`), `_hueFilterOn` / `_colorsMatchHue` / `_catalogFilter` take the window to test.
- Easter egg `codeEnvelope` (red envelope beside the tiger in the "Gift code" window; backend `EGGS.codeEnvelope`, deployed).
- TigerCoins history coin flips on a click (three turns, nine gold stars, `sfx.coinFlip` synthesised in `coin-sound.js`).
- "Works with" caption over the new-chip card's brand column (`.lc-brands-cap`, smaller for fr / es / it / pt / ru).

### Changed
- TigerCoins history rows say which way they went: envelope received green bar + arrow in + friend avatar (`userAvatar(f, "sqb-avatar")`, `ctx.friendAvatar`) + "From {name}" + "+1"; given red bar + arrow out + "To {name}" + "−1"; coins earned gold, spent red (`.tch-row--in/out/coin`). The list has a fixed height with the loading animation centred (`loadingHTML` on the header ctx), totals show "0" without a sign.
- Every envelope can be given to a friend, the chips' included (`giveEnvelope` `id: "chip"`, backend); the give window always opens with the day's count (`envGiveCount`) and says why when nothing is left.
- Header stat cards are 68 px tall (`--top-card-h`), content centred.
- Opening a deal closes every other side card first (`ctx.closeOtherSides` → `_closeAllSidePanels({ keepDeals })`).
- While the new-chip card is up, a spool card offers no upgrade to TigerData+ / TigerTag+; closing the card gives it back.
- The link grid of the "+" card searches tags, reads the live inventory and updates keyed cards in place (`data-lc-key`).
- NFC tag tester open → scans open, add and offer nothing (`_testerUp`).
- A single RFID reader still shows its numbered dot (NFC tools, burn window).

### Fixed
- Deleting a spool deletes its maker chips' links (`markSpoolDeleted`, same batch); a link to a missing spool no longer blocks relinking (`presentMakerChips`).
- A maker chip placed while the "+" card is up was ignored; it now waits in the card (`holdForeignChip`) and the product card offers no burn.
- A maker chip placed while "Add product" was open was ignored and Make went on to the burn.
- A TigerData spool with linked maker chips offered "Burn the TigerTag+".
- "Upgrade to TigerData+" swapped in after closing the new-chip card did not open the catalogue (its click is now wired with the other chip banners).
- The TigerCoins history opened small and grew when the log arrived; its row icon rule blew the friend's TigerSquad badge up to 30 px (`.tch-row > img`).

### i18n
- Added `envGiveCount`, `linkedChipWorksWith`, `coinLog_to`, `coinLog_from`; removed `coinLog_given`, `coinLog_received`.

## v2.46.2 — 2026-10-28

### Added
- Brand column in the new-chip card (`.lc-brands` between the rail and the pane, `renderer/rfid_protocol/linked/linked.css`; card 612 → 664 px), decorative and `aria-hidden`: the app icon's tiger on top, then Bambu Lab, Creality, Anycubic, Snapmaker, Elegoo, Qidi, Prusa, FlashForge, Klipper, OctoPrint, Fluidd, Mainsail as monochrome masks. A periodic sheen; a logo brushed by the mouse lights up in its brand colour, grows and glows, held 900 ms after the pointer leaves (`.is-lit`, `wireBrandColumn` in `renderer/rfid_protocol/linked/index.js`); one top-to-bottom garland of that effect each time the card opens; the tiger grows slowly while the pointer is in the column (`.is-awake`). Growth is a negative inset on the masked element (a transform blurred the mask). Reduced motion: colour only. New `assets/svg/icons/logo_qidi.svg` (Qidi's cube over its wordmark, from QIDIStudio's vector paths) and `logo_mainsail.svg`.
- `envGive` easter egg in the Friends panel's "Give an envelope" window (`tapEgg`, glints while not found; backend `functions/tigerCoins.js` `EGGS`). `_coinsCtx` carries `easterEgg` / `eggFound`. New rule `.agents/rules/easter-eggs.md`.
- squadDeal `list` action (backend `functions/squadDeals.js` `listDeals`): the Deals view in one call — active + archived deals, the caller's claim on each, the links their XP opens, same scope as `firestore.rules`, Timestamps as epoch ms.

### Changed
- Deals view loads through squadDeal `list` (one HTTP call) instead of ~45 Firestore reads, which queue behind every listener an account starts on a switch; the direct reads stay as the fallback. One load per account at a time (`_dealsLoading`), each account's last Deals kept for the session (`_dealsByUid`), and a data signature guard (`host._sqdSig`) so the view is rebuilt only when what it shows changes (countdowns tick in place; an expiry forces a repaint).
- Catalogue sync reads the RFID-Guide snapshot `database/id_catalog.json` from raw.githubusercontent.com (~1 s, 1.6 MB gzip), kept on disk with its ETag (`userData/catalog-snapshot.json` + `.etag`) so an unchanged catalogue is a `304` (`_fetchCatalogSnapshot`, `main.js`). `product/get/all` is the fallback, its pages with 60 s and two retries (`_fetchCatalogPage`).
- Product images: grid cards ask the CDN for `medium` (`GRID_CARD_IMG`); every call site resolves the sized URL (`resolvedImg(cdnImg(url, size))`); the local image cache warms the grid rendition; side-card photos ask for the rung covering the photo as laid out (`_panelImg`); `_cdnSizeFor` accepts a rung up to 10 % smaller than the slot. Images are used as the CDN serves them, nothing is resized locally.
- Image cache hygiene: once per session the map keeps the newest `&v=` of each product × rendition and main deletes unreferenced files (`_imgCacheTidy`, `img:prune`, preload `imgPrune`); `img:get`'s fetch factored into `_fetchImageBuffer` (same per-hop SSRF check).
- "Give an envelope" window: the envelope turns toward the mouse with glints (`tilt`, `.tch-env-tilt`, `.tch-glints`); the "Give" button is the "Open ×10" button (`.tp-ten`, held 2 s via `holdToFire`, now exported from `coin-pull.js`), text only; the friends search 16 px under the title.
- `scripts/check-theme-tokens.mjs`: prusa, qidi, klipper, octoprint, fluidd, mainsail exempt as brand colours.

### Fixed
- Account switch from the Deals view left the cards as skeletons for 10-40 s (measured 12 s Tiger Tag, 42 s OpenMaker) and rebuilt the view 3-18 times; now ~1 s on a first visit, instant on the way back, 1-2 rebuilds.
- A product photo changed in the catalogue never reached the spools: `get/all` carries no `updated_at` and `product/get` sends it as an ISO string read as NaN. A spool whose `url_img` differs from the list's `img_src` is stale (unless it shows the user's own photo); `updated_at` parsed as ISO or epoch (`_catalogStaleRows`, `_catalogTime`).
- Catalogue sync never completed past ~2 700 products: 19 pages of ~11 s each, a 20 s per-page abort with no retry threw the whole sync away.
- A catalogue refresh (`_refreshApiData`) overwrote a photo the user set themselves (`url_img_user`).
- Retina flicker ("tile memory limits exceeded"): ~40 closed overlays were `opacity: 0` full-window `backdrop-filter` surfaces still composited; closed `.modal-overlay` and `.sfe-backdrop` are now `visibility: hidden` after their fade-out. Grid cards are skipped off screen (`content-visibility: auto`).

### Docs
- `ROADMAP.md`: G7 Bambu Lab "skip objects" (#156), G8 Anycubic ACE Pro 2 drying + filament left (#157).

## v2.46.1 — 2026-10-28

### Added
- NFC tools (`renderer/rfid_protocol/eraser/`, a fourth choice of the new-chip card `#chipChoicePanel`, opened by the search bar's "+" or a blank chip): while it is up a scan opens nothing (`chipEraserOpen()` in `onRfidTagScanned` / `onRfid` / `onRfidForeignChip`); reader plates by colour only. Held (1.5 s) chip banners: "Format your RFID chip" (orange, `rfid:format` → TigerTag Init) and "Write in NDEF", a form with seven choices — link, Wi‑Fi, contact, e‑mail, phone, SMS and "Reset NDEF" (`rfid:erase`) — ending in "Write to the chip". A spool of the inventory on erased / written chips leaves it, as with the spool card's Erase / Recycle.
- NDEF encoder `services/ndef.js` + `rfid:write-ndef`, `rfid:ndef-size`, `rfid:ndef-capacity` (preload `writeNdefTag`, `ndefSize`, `ndefCapacity`): one NDEF record in a Type 2 TLV — URI records with the NFC Forum prefix codes, Wi‑Fi Simple Configuration credential (`application/vnd.wfa.wsc`), vCard 3.0 (name, phone, e‑mail, company, website); short / long record and 1- / 3-byte TLV length; sized to the chip from its capability container (page 3 byte 2 × 8: NTAG213 144, NTAG215 496, NTAG216 872 bytes), checked per chip; written through `_writeChipImage` (UID-guarded, surgical, read-back verified, pages past 39 verified — `services/nfc-process.js` `read-now` takes `start` / `length`). The content is never logged.
- Links in the NDEF form accept any scheme (rtsp://, rtmp://, ws://, mqtt://, smb://, ftp://, app links): no scheme → https://, a bare local address → http://; the host is checked as typed (domain with a 2+ letter TLD or punycode, IPv4 ≤ 255, IPv6, localhost, .local), port ≤ 65535, login allowed; javascript:, data:, file:, vbscript:, about:, blob: refused (`normUrl`, `validHost`, `validUrl`).
- Auto mode on "Format" and "Write to the chip" (`autoMode`, `addAutoSwitch`, a switch inside the banner, `data-no-hold`): every chip placed is written at once, once per placement, the banner burning on its own (`burnFx`) with a beep and a count under it; a chip of an inventory spool is never written by Auto; one Auto at a time.
- Last link (`tigertag.nfc.lastUrl`) and the contact card (`tigertag.nfc.contact`) kept in localStorage; a Wi‑Fi password never.
- "Burn the TigerTag+" held banner on every catalogue product card (`_burnBannerHTML`, `_catalogCreate(…, { autoBurn })`); with no chip on a TigerPOD a press shows the red TigerPOD bubble. The "Build TigerPOD →" pill is gone.
- FlashForge AD5X, Creator 5 and Creator 5 Pro in the Klipper model list (issue #108, `renderer/printers/klipper/models.json` ids 163–165).

### Changed
- Linked chips: any MIFARE Classic 1K maker chip (Bambu Lab, Creality, Qidi, Anycubic…) links to a spool, which may carry both its 2 TigerTag chips and its 2 maker chips, or only the maker chip(s).
- "Upgrade to TigerTag+ / TigerData+" goes through the catalogue: the violet banner opens the picker at the right edge (`openCatalogPick({ upgradeFor })`, `_cpkUpgradeFor` in `_syncPanels`), the picked product's card offers a held upgrade; the target follows the spool (`_upgradeTier`), a TigerTag only evolves with its own chip on the TigerPOD (`_spoolChipOnPod`); `_convertToPlus` applies the product's data (`_catalogDocFromApi`) to the spool and its twin in one batch and flags the chip for rewriting (`needUpdateAt`). The product-ID form and its help window are gone.
- "Please update NFC" is a held orange chip banner (`_chipBannerHTML(…, { update })`, `_wireChipBanner`), the "Updated" pill gone.
- The search bar's "+" toggles the new-chip card and always offers "Link to my inventory", greyed without a chip on the TigerPOD (red bubble with the TigerPOD on click, `refreshChipChoice`); tapping the view already open does nothing.
- Shared red help bubble (`_showErrorTip`): stays while the pointer moves, optional picture, closed by a press outside it.
- Weight manual entry edits in place at the same height (input + ✓ ✕ in the value row, Net / On scale switch with a sliding highlight and its one-line calculation); "Balance" renamed "On scale".
- "+ Material" is held 1 s on the grouped-spools, spool and product cards.
- Detail panel: a Color row (one `colorBg()` swatch) replaces the "Change colour" chip.
- Paxx U1 firmware fallback `v2.0.0-paxx12-24` (issue #142).

### Fixed
- macOS: "Update" installs again. electron-updater reports the download as soon as the zip is cached while Squirrel.Mac still fetches it from its local proxy and checks the signature (~30 s); the 6 s forced exit added in v2.32.0 for Windows killed the app before that, so nothing was installed. "Update" is offered once Squirrel.Mac holds the update (native `update-downloaded`, `_macSquirrelReady`) and the forced exit is armed only from then; Windows unchanged.
- While the new-chip card is up a scan no longer opens, creates or updates a spool, nor reopens the card on another view (`_chipCardUp`, also at the top of `_processNfcScans`).
- An NDEF tag (a phone's, the NFC tools') is no longer created as a made-up spool: a chip is a spool only when its `id_tigertag` is a TigerTag version of `id_version.json`.
- Eleven `toast()` calls with the wrong arguments threw (issue #154's "API refresh failed", the TigerTag+ upgrade stopping after the write); they use `_flashMessage`.
- A spool with no container shows a "Choose a container" row again (issue #154); the post-create container question waits for the burn window (`_cpAskYieldToBurn`).
- Bambu RTSPS camera recording taps the live stream instead of a second connection the printer cut after ~10 s; the live view dates pictures on arrival (PR #152 by @Ptitlouis6012, with review fixes).
- FlashForge "wrong serial number" says to turn on LAN mode first (issue #86).
- USB scale reopened after a system resume; silence logged (issue #138, Windows, not reproduced on macOS).

---

## v2.45.0 — 2026-10-27

### Added
- Linked chips (spec `docs/LINKED-CHIPS.md`): a third-party MIFARE Classic RFID chip — Bambu Lab and Creality CFS (tested); QIDI Box and Snapmaker U1 stock tags use MIFARE Classic 1K too (untested) — linked to a spool of the inventory by its UID, in `users/{uid}/rfidAliases/{UID_HEX}` `{ spoolId, chip, vendor, linkedAt }` (backend rule: owner-only, fixed shape). `services/nfc-process.js` passes the card's ATR, `main.js` sends `rfid-foreign-chip { readerName, uid, chip }` for a card with no readable pages, `preload.js` `onRfidForeignChip`. New module `renderer/rfid_protocol/linked/` (`index.js`, `linked.css`): scans buffered 1.5 s (the TigerPOD's two readers), a linked chip opens its spool; target doc = the spool's, for a twin the chip with `tag_index` 1 (unknown → the smaller UID), a deleted target hands over to its twin; the detail panel lists the spool's linked chips with ONE held Unlink that removes them all. No XP, no envelope. Not yet: third-party NTAG formats (Anycubic ACE, ELEGOO Canvas, OpenSpool) — Studio reads their pages and ignores what is not a TigerTag — and Prusa OpenPrintTag (NFC-V, not read by the ACR122U).
- New-chip side card `#chipChoicePanel` ("What would you like to do?"): opened by an unknown maker's chip, a blank NTAG (TigerTag Init, erased or empty NDEF — it replaces the Add product panel that opened before) and the search bar's "+" (whose drop-down menu `#searchAddMenu` is gone). ONE card: a rail of square choices with ⓘ bubbles and a pane where the chosen view slides in from the right (`adopt` / `embed` / `unembed` borrow the existing cards; `_syncPanels` skips a borrowed card): Add from the catalogue (opened straight away — new `#catalogPickPanel`: brand filter + the catalogue search, a tap opens the product's catalogue card, whose "+ Inventory" creates the TigerData+), Add manually (the Add product panel), Link to my inventory (`#chipLinkPanel`: the inventory's own grid cards grouped like the inventory, search pill + brand filter; for a maker's chip the materials with no linked chip, for a blank NTAG the chipless TigerData / TigerData+). Chips waiting for a creation are linked when it is saved (`linkPendingTo`, 15 min, cleared by any other creation).
- Spool card banners, held 1.5 s with a green "burning" fill, a sweeping glint and a glowing logo (`cloud-burn-*`, off with reduced motion; effects stop while held): "Link the proprietary third-party Tag(s)" with an ⓘ saying it is a UID-only link (`linkOfferHtml` / `wireLinkOffer`), and "Burn the TigerTag" / "Burn the TigerTag+" by tier with an ⓘ saying the data go into the chip (`.cloud-encode-banner--hold`). The burn banner opens the burn window with `{ auto: true }` (`_cemAuto`): the burn starts by itself once every reader holds a chip checked blank. After a link or a burn every side card closes and the material's card opens alone (`_closeAllSidePanels`).
- External IP cameras (PR #141 by @Ptitlouis6012): "Add a 3D printer" → External cameras → IP camera (RTSP, MJPEG, WebRTC/WHEP), Test button; cards of the camera wall like a printer's (placed, moved, resized, fullscreen — items shaped like a printer, `brand: "extcam"`, place `camWallPlan`) and cards on the printers board (`boardPlan`). `renderer/ext-cam/ext-cam.js`, `extcam:*` IPC in `main.js`. Stored in `users/{uid}/devices/{id}` with `type: "camera"` (backend rule: owner-only, `hasOnly`, deployed); the login is kept in clear for its owner behind an eye toggle, never written into the DOM, and MCP output masks a login typed into an address. Review fixes: RTSP and MJPEG both read by ffmpeg in main (a page fetch is blocked by CORS on most cameras), `-protocol_whitelist`, readers killed on page reload, RTSP retries on TCP when UDP ends before a picture, WHEP offer times out after 8 s, one WebRTC retry at a time with the dead peer connection closed, a position change moves the card in place instead of redrawing the board, theme tokens.
- TigerCoins history: the balance card on the Deals banner opens every movement of `users/{uid}/coinLog` (envelopes, chests, gift codes, deals, envelopes given / received) with earned / spent / balance and tabs (`openCoinsHistory` in `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js`).
- Easter egg found → a celebration before the envelope opens (`celebrateEgg` in `renderer/quests/coin-pull.js`).

### Changed
- Tiger Quests banners (Today, Missions) rebuilt from separate layers like the others, each tiger one picture standing on the desk; tigers of the Missions, League and XP history banners aligned on the desk's top edge.
- The burn window shows a MIFARE Classic chip in orange as an incompatible NFC tag and blocks the burn (`_cemChipIncompatible`, hint `encIncompatible`).
- Snapmaker U1 on the Paxx firmware (PR #145 by @Ptitlouis6012): the camera wall card opens the side card, the photo / record / folder bar appears, photo from `/webcam/snapshot.jpg`, video from `/webcam/stream.mjpg` (copied by ffmpeg) with a fall-back on the still read every 200 ms; the still and the stream probe are read by main (`cam:fetch-still`, `cam:probe-stream`) — from the page they are cross-origin.

### Fixed
- Issue #146: the decimal → hex UID migration dropped the leading 0 of 7-byte NTAG UIDs (`04D8593DD42A81` became `4D8593DD42A81`), so scans and TigerScale missed the spool and made duplicates. Conversion padded to whole bytes; pure helpers in `renderer/uid-repair.js` (same plan as the mobile app's `uid_repair.dart`, tests `scripts/test-uid-repair.mjs`); truncated spools, twin pointers and `uidMigrationMap` entries are repaired silently on the owner's inventory (`maybeRepairTruncatedSpoolIds`, one atomic batch per spool, the newer doc wins a collision).
- Elegoo CC1: recording a video failed — the camera's own `<img>` address is now the stream whatever it looks like (PR #147 by @Ptitlouis6012).
- TigerPOD not credited to an account when another account had recorded it earlier in the same Studio session (`hydratePodSignal` resets the session max).
- Easter eggs and friends' envelopes: clicks still coming after a discovery no longer skip it (input ignored for 2 s, `guardInput`); League and Maker's Workshop eggs no longer show an error instead of the celebration.

- Tiger Squad → TigerCoins, envelopes and Exchange: members open ENVELOPES, win TigerCoins and trade them in the Deals view's new "Exchange" tab (the deals priced in coins — free cap 1,200, free polo 2,000). Envelope TYPES and CHEST types (TigerHub admin, owner): an envelope type is a rarity split in % with presets (only common, uncommon or better, rare or better, epic or better, only legendary, true random on the main table), "Standard" being the main table; a chest type = N envelopes of one type, in its own colour; what is given keeps the split of its type at that moment. A surprise envelope is a server-side draw on its type's split, coins from the main table (common 70 % 5–10 · uncommon 20 % 15–25 · rare 8 % 40–60 · epic 1.8 % 100–150 · legendary 0.2 % 500, ≈ 16.5 coins each, editable by the owner in the TigerHub admin): every TigerTag+ Certified chip the account ever recorded gives one (a twin spool = two). Gift envelopes come on top — a TigerHub campaign to an audience (XP, certified chips, TigerSquad, league countries, uids), an automatic milestone bonus (XP / certified chips, "reaches" once or "every" N, retroactive or not), a gift code typed in the three-box scratch-card modal ("I have a code"; a code batch for RFID chips or cards, CSV export), or a shared red envelope (WeChat style: a pool split at random between the first N members, with the luck rank) — each giving coins, N envelopes of a type or N chests of a type; envelopes are drawn when received, chests wait in the pile. The TigerCoins card on the right of the Deals banner (like the other Tiger Squad heroes' cards) shows the balance, one pile, "N envelopes to open", and "Open my envelopes" opens the gifts first, then the chests (a chest in its type's colour with gold bands: "Open the chest" lifts the lid, a light spills out, its envelopes are revealed), (red envelope with a gold seal: "Open it" lifts the flap on the coins, or on N surprise envelopes → "Open them"), then the chips' envelopes, "Tiger Arcade Jackpot" (gacha × casino, designed with ChatGPT; `renderer/quests/coin-pull.js` + `renderer/quests/coin-sound.js`, new): no panel, no counters, no close button: the envelope and the buttons float over the dimmed app with a turning spotlight, a sound toggle top right, a click outside leaves; the big illustrated red envelope (tiger seal) floats with a light sweeping over it and follows the mouse — it tilts in 3D towards the pointer with a glare under it (the revealed card too, its light following the pointer); HOLD it to open one (an SVG ring fills, it trembles, beeps from the very first instant, faster, higher and louder, with a soft echo; the audio engine wakes when the stage opens), or hold "Open ×10" below for 2 s (the button strains, shaking harder and glowing brighter; the pointer is captured so the shaking never drops the hold; a too-short click shakes "no" and a bubble says "Hold 2 s" / "Hold to open") — the backend draws 1 to 10 per pull; ×1: the envelope shakes while its glow runs once through every rarity colour, grey to gold, the same sequence whatever was drawn (the card alone tells the result), cracks, and a card rises in its rarity's material — matte paper (common), green enamel (uncommon), blue holo foil (rare), purple neon with a light running round the rim (epic), solid gold with a crown (legendary) — the rarer, the bigger the screen gets: a rare sends a blue shockwave; an epic dims the room with purple neon rays, two shockwaves, lightning flickers, purple sparks, an EPIC banner and a lighter slam; an epic and a legendary in the same ×10 play their backgrounds together (the epic's rays turning the other way under the gold, its banner stepping aside); a legendary takes the whole screen: the room goes dark, a white-gold flash, two shockwaves and god rays across the viewport, a full-screen fountain of coins and sparks, a huge LEGENDARY banner, the card slamming in with a screen shake and a deep boom (×1 and ×10) — sparks as many as it is rare, coins fly to the TigerCoins bar behind; ×10: face-down envelopes dealt then flipped in cascade, every card turning towards the mouse with its light following, a pause and a pop on an epic or a legendary, then the rarity counts (badges in each rarity's colour) and the total pop up below the cards and float up above them, staying there until the member moves on (no backdrop, out of the layout: the cards never move); after any reveal no button — a click anywhere goes back to the pile (or out when it is empty), the ×1 card big and alone with its amount; every moment has a Web Audio sound (no file) — the reveal gets louder with the rarity: a riser and a boom deeper and longer from rare to legendary, a richer chord each step (epic: a dark minor turn resolving bright), muted state remembered (`tigertag.coinsSound`); reduced motion keeps only fades, the rarity and the amount. - Tiger Quests: Explorer secret bonus level "Under the hood" (`e6`, +200 bonus) — hidden until Explorer 1–5 are won, like d6; five actions: change a friend's place in your Friends list (`friendReorder`, counted when a drag really moves someone and the order is saved), turn on the AI assistant (`aiAssistant` gauge, set once the switch is on, already-on counts), turn on debug mode (`debugMode` gauge, same), daily quests 10 days in a row (best streak), lock a slot in a rack (`rackSlotLock`, one slot or "Lock all") — Studio `renderer/inventory.js` (`_applyFriendReorder`, MCP `paint`, user-doc `Debug`, `toggleSlotLock`, `lockAllSlots`); backend `functions/quests.js` + table test (12 levels, 60 actions; 22 passing; deployed), `docs/XP-SYSTEM.html` regenerated; i18n `questLevel_e6`, `questAct_e6_friendOrder`, `questAct_e6_aiAssistant`, `questAct_e6_debugMode`, `questAct_e6_streak10`, `questAct_e6_slotLock` (× 11). - Tiger Squad → Deals ("%" in the view bar's Tiger Squad group, members only — a non-member gets the TigerSquad invitation, a friend's view hides it): partner offers with nothing stored in the app — the `squadDeals` Firestore collection, managed only from the TigerHub admin. A deal has a status set in the admin — draft (hidden), active, archived — and shows live when active, inside its start / end dates and aimed at the member's league country (`userProfiles` `tigerSquad.country`, else the picked country; no country = worldwide). "Get my code" accepts it: the backend records the member's claim and returns the shared code, or for a "unique" deal one single-use code of the pool, the member's for good (with what is left / "Sold out"). Below the deal's `minXp` the card is locked with the XP to go (the link in `secret/main` is XP-gated by the rules). Three sections: live; "Archived" — the member's history: deals they accepted then marked "I used it", or that were archived / ended, whatever the country; "Ended deals" — archived or past their dates and never accepted. Only deleting a deal removes it everywhere. - Admin: reset one account's day of daily quests — `claimSupport` action `resetDaily { uid }` (admin only) takes back the day's XP (open, missions, full set, streak step + its bonus), forgets the day and forces a new draw at the next check-in (`drawSalt` in `quests/daily`, `drawDaily(…, salt)`; salt 0 keeps every other draw unchanged) — backend `functions/quests.js` (`resetDailyToday`), `functions/supportWebhooks.js`, test (19 passing); deployed

### Changed
- League rank card: 5 px more between the league badge (flag / globe) and the rival's avatar (`.sqr-rival-row > :first-child`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`).
- Maker's Workshop banner rebuilt from layers (hero-layers.js): a new tiger in the TigerTag cap (from the real cap's photo), arms open with the blue cuffs, still behind the desk with its bottom outline just above it (`assets/img/tigersquad/workshop-hero/tiger.webp`, source in `assets-src`); the four legendary devices stand on the desk, silhouettes until owned. The old two-layer tiger (`legendary/tiger*.webp`) stays: the notification centre's empty state and the profile preview still use it. Banners are now a named container (`container: hero / inline-size`) so rules inside a layered scene can query the banner's width.
- League banner ("La Ligue des Makers") rebuilt from separate layers (hero-layers.js): three tigers — fist raised with SILVER on a light-blue ribbon, GOLD on a red ribbon, thumbs-up BRONZE on a green ribbon — each with the blue hoodie cuffs before the paws, still, behind the desk with their waist cut just under its top edge and their resting paws on the desk (separate `*-paws` layers holding only the white paws and their outline, cut out by colour — no hoodie or medal in front of the desk); trophy (rocking; a click makes it jump, spin and flash gold with a fanfare — `data-trophy`, `hl-cheer`; it is also easter egg `leagueTrophy`: three quick clicks give one common envelope, once per account, no envelope drawn), printer, four twinkling stars. `assets/img/tigersquad/league-hero/*.webp` (masters + source sheets in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/league-hero/`); `LEAGUE_SCENE` in `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`; the flat `tigersquad-scene-v3*.webp` removed.
- XP History banner rebuilt from separate layers like Deals: the tiger in the TigerTag cap pointing at its chart (still, behind the desk), monitor, star coins (floating), logbook, an hourglass that turns over (half turn with a bounce) on each click (`data-xph-hourglass`, handled in the XP history view's click handler in `renderer/inventory.js`), three twinkling sparkles — `assets/img/quests/xph-hero/*.webp` (masters + source sheet in `assets-src/img/quests/xph-hero/`); the flat `xp-history-scene.webp` removed. The tiger was regenerated from the real TigerTag cap's photo (clean logo + "TIGER TAG") and is split in two layers of the same size and place: the body behind the desk, its waist cut just under the desk's top edge, and the resting paw (`tiger-paw.webp`) in front of the desk. Both banners now use one helper, `renderer/quests/hero-layers.js` (`heroLayersHtml`, `.hl-scene` / `.hl-box` / `.hl` in `renderer/quests/quests.css`); the Deals scene's `.sqd-scene` / `.sqs` classes became `.hl*`.
- Deals side card: no "Deal detail" header any more — the picture runs edge to edge from the top of the card, like a spool's detail panel; an announcement's kicker moves into the body as its eyebrow (`renderer/quests/squad-deals.js`, `.sqd-side-art` in `renderer/quests/quests.css`).
- Notification centre, community nudges (Wiki, Discord, GitHub, free 3D files, coffee): illustrations in the art direction instead of the coloured app-icon chips — `assets/img/notifications/community-{wiki,discord,github,makerworld,coffee}.webp` (masters in `assets-src/img/notifications/`; the coffee is a tiger-sleeve takeaway cup); `COMMUNITY[*].art` + `_communityArt()` in `renderer/inventory.js`; the old `.notif-ic--wiki/discord/github/makerworld/shop/coffee` chip CSS removed (`renderer/css/20-friends.css`).
- Maker's Workshop tiles and Feats rows answer the hover: the art hops (`qw-hop`, off with reduced motion) and a short note plays, higher with the rarity, with a sparkle on legendaries (`sfx.hover` in `renderer/quests/coin-sound.js`; delegated listener in `renderer/quests/index.js`).
- My profile, AI assistants: the "on this computer vs online" info bubble moved to the local "AI assistants" card title; the online card keeps its explanation in the title's info bubble (`eacMcpWebSub`, reworded for any assistant) and shows four tiles — Claude, ChatGPT, Grok, Le Chat — that copy the address and open that assistant's section of the wiki guide (toast `eacMcpWebCopiedFor`; Grok and Le Chat sections added to the TigerSystem-Docs guide). New logos `assets/svg/brands/{chatgpt,grok,mistral}.svg`. The Community card (TigerSquad toggle + flag) moved to the left column, between Profile and Preferences; its XP / certified / world-rank line under the toggle is gone (`#eacSquadXp` and its CSS removed, `_renderSquadXp` now only repaints the header badge); rows in My profile get 8 px between them, so the Language / Country / Price controls no longer touch (`renderer/css/60-modals.css`) (`renderer/inventory.html` `#eacMcpWeb`, `renderer/css/60-modals.css`, `renderer/inventory.js` `wireMcpHosted`).
- Feedback panel, "What is it about?" cards: the product artwork is white (`--on-accent`) instead of orange, and Tiger Studio uses the TigerSystem framed tiger (`assets/svg/logos/tigersystem-icon-framed-white.svg`, `renderer/report/index.js` TARGETS); in the light theme the tile turns solid orange so it still reads (`renderer/report/report.css`).
- Maker's Workshop redesigned (two panels kept): Loot as illustrated tiles — legendary relics large (`assets/img/tigersquad/legendary/*-flat*.webp`), gear 4 across, chips 3 across — and Feats as aligned rows (art · name + hint · ×N · rarity · XP · status, empty ×N cell kept); items not owned drawn as a black silhouette (like the banner's devices) with a lock; on tiles the XP is a pill overhanging the card's top edge. TigerTag+ Certified is now uncommon. New illustrations `assets/img/tigersquad/loot/{tigerscale,tigerspool,tigertouch,tigerpod,td1s,usb-scale,rfid-reader,printer,chip,chip-cert}.webp` (`chip`, the black TigerTag chip, shared by TigerTag and TigerTag+; `chip-cert` = the same chip with a green shield) (masters in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/loot/`, relics cut whole from `legendary/devices-flat2-source.png`); Feats rows illustrated too: `assets/img/tigersquad/feats/{mobile-app,speedrun,first-rack,first-list,friends,photo,social,community}.webp` (masters in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/feats/`; the phone shows the TigerTag logo), Bonus TigerSquad reuses `tigersquad-gold.webp`, app updates reuse `notifications/update.webp`; the `icon_mobile_app` mask is no longer used there. `ctx.loot()` items carry `art`; chips no longer pass `value`. Workshop banner: devices drop out before the next-level card on narrow windows. The two columns no longer sit on background panels (lighter page, 28 px between them). The "See the wall of thanks" link is gone from the Workshop (kept on the League; `.sqb-wall[hidden]` now really hides it — the global button rule had overridden `hidden`). Every Tiger Squad banner (League, Workshop, Deals) keeps its title and tagline on one line, sized to the banner; the League/Deals scene hides below 1000 px of banner (`renderer/quests/quests.css` `.sqr-hero`) (`renderer/quests/index.js` `ladderHtml`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `renderer/inventory.js`).
- Tiger Squad → Deals: the "I have a code" modal redone from a ChatGPT mockup (`assets-src/img/tigercoins/code-modal-mockup.png`) and named generically "Gift code" (a code gives coins, envelopes or chests): separate art layers each moving on its own — the tiger pointing at the boxes, a chest overflowing with coins, a pile of envelope and coins (`assets/img/tigercoins/code-tiger.webp`, `code-chest.webp`, masters in `assets-src/img/tigercoins/`) — a title plaque with a floating gift, big glowing boxes, a big Redeem button, a close button, and where codes come from along the bottom (Discord, RFID chip, printed card, shared envelope) — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (`openCoinsCodeModal`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.tcx*`), `assets/svg/icons/icon_card.svg` (`.icon-card`); i18n `giftCodeTitle`, `codeSrcDiscord`, `codeSrcDiscordSub`, `codeSrcRfid`, `codeSrcRfidSub`, `codeSrcCard`, `codeSrcCardSub`, `codeSrcShared`, `codeSrcSharedSub` (× 11), `coinsCodePlaceholder` removed
- Tiger Squad → Deals: the TigerCoins banner card is minimal, one row — the illustrated tiger coin and the balance, an envelope button with how many wait, a gift icon for a code; the illustrated coin (`coin.webp`) and envelope replace the CSS-drawn ones everywhere (prices, Exchange tab, pile); a deal paid in TigerCoins with no badge reads "Free" instead of "0 €"; a badge's colour says the kind of perk, read from its text (`badgeKind`): a percentage orange, an amount off red, XP green, free black and gold (the cap and polo badges cleared in Firestore) — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (`coinsBar`, `dealParts`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.tcoin`, `.sqd-coins*`, `.tc-env-mini`); i18n `squadDealsFree` (× 11)
