- Edit my profile: the "hold 1.5 s" line under the trash button is now its hover bubble ("Hold 1.5 s to confirm", the shared hold-to-confirm hint) — `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/inventory.js`; i18n `idmHoldHint` removed (× 11)
- Edit my profile redesigned (from a ChatGPT review): 480 px, four cards — Profile photo (88 px avatar ringed in the theme colour, the name beside it, "Change avatar" and the held trash button on one row, "Hold 1.5 s to delete" under them), Display name (read-only until its pen is pressed: the pen becomes the orange check and the field opens; the check or Enter saves and closes it, Escape gives the old name back — `_setNameEditing`), Theme colour (the chosen colour's name top right, 7 × 2 plain colour dots, the chosen one with a white ring and a check), Live preview (a mini header and a mini Tiger Squad banner in the theme colour — the banners' desk with the tiger behind it and its paws on it (tiger.webp + tiger-front.webp), your avatar and name, following the field as you type) — and a footer with Done; the swatches lose their `title=` tooltips (aria-labels instead) — `renderer/inventory.html` (`#identityOverlay`), `renderer/css/60-modals.css` (`.idm-*`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`_paintIdmAvatar` paints the name, colour name, preview and hint); i18n `idmPhotoTitle`, `idmHoldHint`, `idmPreview`, `idmNameEdit`, `themeColor_*` (14) (× 11)
- Header avatar (own banner): a click opens "Edit my profile" (it opened the photo picker); the window fills its own fields (display name, theme colour) when opened on its own; "Remove avatar" is now a square trash-icon button held 1.5 s (hold-to-confirm with its fill), no longer a click — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_fillIdentityFields`, `openIdentityModal`, header click, `setupHoldToConfirm` on `#avatarMenuRemove`; the header button's `title=` tooltip removed), `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/css/60-modals.css` (`.idm-btn--icon`)
- Tiger Quests → Explorer 2: "Printers connected (2)" replaced by "Try a demo printer" (`e2.demo`, the `demoPrinter` quest counter; the demo printer itself still earns no XP and stays out of the printer count) — backend `functions/quests.js`, `docs/XP-SYSTEM.html` regenerated (deployed; accounts move at their next recompute); Studio `docs/QUESTS.md`; i18n `questAct_e2_printers` removed (× 11), `questAct_e2_demo` reused
- Tiger Squad → Items renamed "The Maker's workshop" / "L'Atelier du Maker" (view-bar label and banner title, `squadItems` + `squadGrimoireSub` × 11): two panels "Loot · Legendary items & gear" and "Feats · Rewarded actions". Layout from a ChatGPT review: Loot in three groups — Legendary relics (large showcase tiles, n / 4), Gear, Chips & certification (row cards); Feats as two columns of row cards (icon left); each panel header shows "11 / 11 unlocked" (Feats: "done"), a 4 px progress track and "4 487 XP earned"; only legendary cards glow once owned (green stays Epic's colour); chip cards read "190 XP earned" — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`lootHtml` groups, `ladderHtml(items, …, countKey, groups)`, `CHIP_IDS`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qw-group*`, `.qw-cards--relics|gear|chips|rows`, `.qw-ladder-bar`), `renderer/inventory.js` (chip `value`), `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`; i18n `questsRelics`, `questsGear`, `questsChips`, `questsUnlocked`, `questsAccomplished`, `questsXpEarned` (× 11); i18n `squadItems` (value), `squadGrimoireSub`, `questsLoot` (value), `questsLootSub`, `questsFeats`, `questsFeatsSub` added; `questsLootTitle`, `questsBadgesEyebrow`, `questsBadgesTitle` removed (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → Items: the next squad level (badges, title, progress, how) moves into the banner's right card, framed like the Rankings card; hidden on narrow banners — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`squadItemsHeroHtml(ctx, owned, nextCard)`), `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqi-next`)
- Tiger Quests → Missions: the chapter row sits straight on the view — no box around it — so the page breathes — `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qm-chapters`)
- My profile: avatar, display name and theme colour move into an "Edit my profile" window, opened from the avatar or the pen beside the name (photo change / remove, name with its save check, the 13 colours + custom picker with an ⓘ on what the theme colour does); the panel's hero keeps the identity only — same controls and ids, the panel's own handlers — `renderer/inventory.html` (`#identityOverlay`, `#eacIdentEdit`; the avatar popover menu removed), `renderer/inventory.js` (`openIdentityModal`, `_paintIdmAvatar`), `renderer/css/60-modals.css` (`.idm-*`); i18n `eacIdentTitle`, `eacThemeColor`, `eacThemeColorTip`, `idmDone` (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → Items redesigned: the two tables sit side by side (stacked under 1300 px), each one grid of cards sorted by rarity instead of a band per rarity — the card carries its rarity (Diablo colour on its top edge, glow when owned, rarity name in its foot), an ×n count badge, a two-line description instead of a cut-off one, the XP with a check (owned) or a lock; each table's header shows owned / total and the XP it brought — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`lootHtml`, `ladderHtml`, `rarityOf`, `earnedOf`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qw-ladders`, `.qw-card*`; the old `.qw-rung` / `.qw-loot` / `.qw-item` rules removed)
- Tiger Squad → Items, Profile & community: two uncommon items say what updates earn — "App updates" (+50 XP per new version installed) and "Speedrun" (+50 XP when updated within 3 days of the release), each with its count (from the XP breakdown `updates`: speedruns = (xp − 50 × n) / 50) — `renderer/inventory.js` (`loot`); i18n `xpUpdatesHint`, `xpSpeedrunHint` (× 11)
- TigerTag chips as three common items in Items (TigerTag 1 XP, TigerTag+ 2 XP, TigerTag+ Certified 10 XP, each with its count and XP): each physical chip (UID) is counted once, in its CURRENT type — the backend now updates a counted chip's points when its type changes (re-programmed TigerTag ↔ TigerTag+, certified or not), so its XP moves from one item to the other (it used to only ever upgrade to certified); the XP breakdown carries `chips.tag / plus / cert` — backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`syncChip`, `storeTotals`: the account doc is also rewritten when only the breakdown's shape changed, and `xpBreakdown` is replaced, not merged, so a removed part such as `publicProfile` really goes); Studio `renderer/inventory.js` (`loot` → `chipItem` ×3); i18n `xpChipTypeHint` (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → Items: two tables — "Legendary items & rarity" keeps only physical things (Tiger devices, TD1S, USB scale, RFID reader, printers, TigerTag chips); a second "Achievements · Profile & community" table holds the rest (phone app, TigerSquad bonus, first rack / list, friends, photo, social and community links), both on the rarity ladder — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`lootHtml` → `ladderHtml` ×2), `renderer/inventory.js` (`physical` flag in `loot`); i18n `questsBadgesEyebrow`, `questsBadgesTitle` (× 11)
- XP: the public profile no longer earns XP (+5 removed) — backend `functions/xpRules.js`, `functions/supportWebhooks.js`, `docs/XP-SYSTEM.html` regenerated (not deployed; accounts lose it at their next recompute); Studio `XP_ACHIEVEMENTS`; i18n `xpAchHint_publicProfile` removed (`xpAch_publicProfile` kept for the XP history's past entries)
- Tiger Squad rank card: both rival lines (country, world) are always shown — a league you lead shows the gold medal and "Number one" on its line, the layout no longer changes at #1; `squadTopSpot` and the `.sqr-top` styles removed (× 11) — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Tiger Squad: every rank number ("#4") is drawn by one helper, `rankNo()` (exported by `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`), with one look — white, mono, bold, a shadow that reads on any row colour: the league rank columns, the national "#1" beside a maker's flag, the rank card, the full "View all" list (podium tints kept) and the header's rank chip — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-no`)
- Tiger Squad in a friend's view: the Rankings page shows what THE FRIEND sees — their rank card (league, XP, country and world rank, their next rivals), their country league and their rows highlighted; no Join button, never your XP. The view bar keeps the Tiger Squad group with Rankings only there (Items and History are about your own account); back home everything is yours again — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_squadGate`, `_renderProgressChips`, `_renderSquadView`, `_openSquadBoard` viewer, `_squadRankCtx` friend / squadXp), `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (no Join on a friend's card)
- Tiger Squad rank card: the next rival is shown per league — a line for your country (flag) and one for the world (globe), each with the rival's avatar, name, XP to catch up and a thin bar; leading a league shows the gold medal and "Number one" on its line (`squadTopShort`, new × 11); leading both keeps the single "Number one. The throne is yours." — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`squadHeroHtml`: `rivalIn`, `rivalRow`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-rival-row`, rival column 200 px)
- Tiger Squad rank card: the country and world ranks show as flag / globe + "#n" only ("en Pologne", "mondial" kept as aria-labels); at #1 worldwide the next-rival slot shows the gold medal and a short line (`squadTopSpot` rewritten in 11 locales: "Number one. The throne is yours.") instead of a two-sentence paragraph — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`squadHeroHtml`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-top`)
- Tiger Squad countries league: each country's row in its flag's colours — up to three bands split at 45° with a short blend, like a bicolor / tricolor spool (135deg), a text shadow keeping names and XP readable on bright stripes, a border in the main colour; your own country keeps its orange frame. The colours are read from the bundled flags by a script (largest areas, ≥ 8 % of the flag) — `scripts/build-flag-colors.mjs` (new; `NODE_PATH` to a checkout with sharp), `renderer/quests/flag-colors.js` (new, generated, 257 countries), `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`countryRow`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-row.has-flag`)
- Tiger Squad leagues: each maker's row (national league, Makers worldwide) wears their theme colour — their public profile's avatar colour as a left-to-right fade (50 → 12 %) and a tinted border; makers without one keep the plain row, your own row keeps its orange highlight — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`memberRow`, `--mc`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`memberColor` in the rankings context), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-row.has-mc`)
- Tiger Squad, Tiger Quests (Today, Missions) and XP history show the app's page loader (spinner + rotating phrases, the one a friend's stock shows) while their data loads, instead of a bare "Loading…" line or empty league columns — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_openSquadBoard` first-load loader, XP history, `loadingHTML` in the quests context), `renderer/quests/index.js`
- View bar: the TigerSquad group is labelled "Tiger Squad" (with a space, like "Tiger Quests") — `renderer/inventory.html`
- Theme colour: the avatar colour a user picks personalises their space — a glow behind the header (`--theme-tint` on `header.top::before`, the SAME gradient as the banners' wall so header and banners share one tone, fading out at the bottom), the hero banners' wall (Tiger Quests, TigerSquad, XP history) and the Missions cards' illustration backdrop (`--hero-wall`), blended into the navy so white text stays readable; in a friend's view everything takes THE FRIEND's colour (their `avatarColor`), back home yours again — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_themeColorHex`, `_applyThemeColor`, called from `paintAvatar` for `#sbAvatar` and from `renderFriendBanner`), `renderer/css/60-modals.css` (`header.top::before`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qm-art`)
- My profile redesigned (layout from two ChatGPT mockups, proposal 1): a double-width panel (780 px) in two columns of cards — Profile (display name, social links with an n/5 counter), Community (TigerSquad, country league), Preferences (theme, language & region, prices) on the left; AI assistants, From the web and your phone (address, how to connect, connected assistants) and Advanced on the right; the colour strip sits beside the identity; the two long connector paragraphs became ⓘ bubbles; Sign out stays pinned below; one column under 820 px; fits a 1000 px tall window without scrolling. Second pass from ChatGPT's review: the avatar wears the member's TigerSquad ring and level badge like every avatarMarkup() avatar (`_paintEacSquad`, also refreshed with the header chips; the pen badge gives way to it, the hover pen stays); the TigerSquad stats on two planned lines (XP · certified / world rank) with the toggle on the title line; connected online assistants in a card of their own (`#eacMcpGrantsCard`, hidden with the connector) with name + revoke on one line and the date / last use on a full-width line that never breaks; 16 px colour dots; Community uses the outline heart. Nothing removed, every id kept — `renderer/inventory.html` (`#editAccountModalOverlay` body), `renderer/css/60-modals.css` (two-column block), `renderer/inventory.js` (`eacSocialsCount`); i18n `eacGroupProfile`, `eacGroupCommunity`, `eacGroupPrefs` added, `eacGroupAppearance` removed (× 11)
- TigerSquad leagues: a member or a country holding the same place as at the start of the day now shows a small grey dash under its XP (▲ / ▼ only when it moved); nothing when it was not ranked this morning — the three leagues already compared with the server baseline, countries and national ranks simply had not moved — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`rankMoves` returns null without a baseline, `moveHtml`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-move.is-same`)
- Tiger Quests → Missions: the chapter heading band between the chapter row and the level cards is gone (the hero card and the chapter row already say it); a selected chapter keeps its green bars when all its levels are won; long chapter names end in an ellipsis inside their tile instead of running over the next one — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`journeyHtml`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qm-head*`, `.qm-segs*` removed); i18n `questRankDesc_1`, `questRankDesc_2` removed (× 11)

### Fixed
- Deals side card: going back and forth quickly between two deals no longer leaves a half-open or wrong card — the card opened always slides in OVER the previous one, which closes behind it unseen (`closeUnder` forces the closed style before the card leaves its under slot — the two changes in one style pass made it slide out on top); a card still covered that is picked again is closed while hidden and slides in on top like any opening (`setOpen` in `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js`). Deals loading skeleton: the cards' top corners are rounded again (`.sqd-card--skel .sqd-skel-art`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`).
- The "Tiger Studio pleases you?" notification (and any coffee community event) opens the support chooser — Buy Me a Coffee / Ko-fi / PayPal, anchored to the sidebar's coffee button — instead of going straight to Buy Me a Coffee (`_openCommunityLink` in `renderer/inventory.js`).
- Camera (PR #139, Ptitlouis6012): stopping a recording on a Bambu RTSPS camera no longer freezes the app on Windows — one `_jpegSplitter` in `main.js` (native `Buffer.indexOf`, resumes between chunks, buffer capped at 2/4 MB) replaces the byte-by-byte JPEG scan of the Bambu RTSPS, Prusa RTSP and Anycubic FLV cameras.
- TigerSquad membership is enforced beyond the view buttons: leaving the squad (or landing on a non-member account) moves out of the squad views and closes the envelope stage (`_enforceSquadGate`); opening / giving envelopes and the XP / progress notification cards offer to join instead (`_squadOnly`); `closePullStages()` exported from `renderer/quests/coin-pull.js` (`renderer/inventory.js`).
- Switching account no longer shows the previous account's TigerCoins and envelopes: the Deals cache and the envelope badge are reset on switch, and a failed Deals load clears the coins (`renderer/inventory.js`).
- TigerHub admin (uncommitted): a deal illustration uploaded from the admin is scaled to the 800 px square, up as well as down (a small photo used to sit in the middle of a white frame); a nearly square picture (4:5 to 5:4) is cropped to fill it, a wider or taller one fitted whole on white — `features/admin/DealsPanel.tsx` (`toWebp`). Firestore: R3D deal illustration replaced (Atome3D product photo, full square); new deal "Filament Jamg He -10 %" (Atome3D code TIGERSQUAD-JAMGHE10 on the Jamg He Filament Refill collection, one use per customer); Tiger Touch deal illustration = the Panda Touch angled shot with live cards (TigerTouch assets/mockups/angle/panda-touch-angle-home-cards.png, squared on white); new deal "Filament Sunlu -5 %" (Atome3D code TIGERSQUAD-SUNLU5 on the Sunlu Filaments collection, one use per customer)
- NFC tag tester redone: a header (chip icon, title, one line of help, close), one card per reader (status dot, name, "Chip detected · UID" / "Waiting for a chip", Read button pulsing while it reads), an idle body with pulsing rings that lights green when a chip sits on it, an empty state when no reader is plugged; the result leads with the colour swatch, brand + material and aspect · diameter · weight, the chip version (and "Certified") as badges, then the UID, the fields (colour, nozzle, bed, drying, TD, tag n of m, made on, product ID, message) and the raw memory; "Empty TigerTag" for an initialised chip with nothing written. It now reads what rfid:read-now really returns (`uid`, `tagData` = the SDK raw dict, IDs named through the reference tables passed by the host) — it expected `rawUid` / `tigerTag` and so showed "Unknown format" with no UID for every chip; placing or removing a chip patches that reader's card instead of wiping the last result — `renderer/rfid_protocol/tigertag/index.js` (`_resultHtml`, `_patchReader`, `initRfidTester({ label })`), `renderer/rfid_protocol/tigertag/modal.css`, `renderer/inventory.html` (header), `renderer/inventory.js` (`initRfidTester` call)
- TigerHub admin (uncommitted): a deal opens read-only (picture with badge, status, partner, title, description, demand counters, code, link, dates, countries, XP to unlock, days to use) with an "Edit" button switching to the form; the open deal is in the URL (`?s=deals&d=<id>`, `d=new` for a new one) so the browser's Back returns to the list; Cancel goes back to the read-only view, saving a new deal opens it, deleting returns to the list — `features/admin/AdminApp.tsx` (`dealId`, `go(section, uid, deal)`), `features/admin/DealsPanel.tsx` (`openId` / `onOpen`), `features/admin/adminT.ts`
- TigerHub admin (uncommitted): deals reordered by drag & drop (grab a deal, drop it on the place it should take; the grip dots and a hint line show it) instead of up / down arrows — `features/admin/DealsPanel.tsx` (`drop`, `dragId` / `overId`), `features/admin/adminT.ts`
- TigerHub admin: an "XP & leagues" card in each user view — XP balance, world / own-country / countries-league ranks (built like Studio's leagues), gained / lost / activities over 7 d / 30 d / 90 d / all, the balance curve, the split by category (Studio's XP_CATS, each opening its parts) and the whole activity log (category filter, pages) — `lib/admin/xp.ts` (`getUserXp`), `app/api/admin/xp/route.ts`, `features/admin/UserXpCard.tsx`, `features/admin/UsersPanel.tsx`, `features/admin/adminT.ts` (`adminLocale`, FR strings)
- Tiger Quests → Explorer 2: the slot quest reads "Edit a filament in a printer" / "Éditer un filament dans une imprimante" (it is counted when a slot's filament edit is saved) — i18n `questAct_e2_slotAssign` (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → The Maker's workshop: the chips always read up the range, TigerTag → TigerTag+ → TigerTag+ Certified (they were sorted by XP earned) — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`CHIP_ORDER`, `ladderHtml` sort)
- Tiger Squad banners (leagues, Maker's workshop, Deals, XP History) use the Tiger Quests title block: the "Tiger Squad" eyebrow pill, the view's name as the big title ("The makers' league", "The Maker's workshop", "Deals", "XP History") and the spaced subtitle, all at the same place (XP History gets the Tiger Squad banners' 8 px top padding) — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`, `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js`, `renderer/quests/xp-history.js` (`.qm-hero-txt` markup), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-hero` carries the `--qw-display` / `--qw-bg` / `--qw-tiger` the block reads; `.sqr-hero-sub`, `.sqr-hero-tag`, `.sqr-hero-orange` removed); i18n `squadBrand` (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → leagues: the ▲ / ▼ arrows count places won or lost over 7 rolling days instead of since midnight (most days nobody moved, so the leagues were a wall of grey dashes); the grey dash is gone (a row that did not move shows nothing) and hovering an arrow says what it means ("+2 places in 7 days") — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`moveHtml`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-move::after`), `docs/firestore-schema.md`; i18n `squadMoveUp`, `squadMoveDown` (× 11). Backend (`communityStats` DEPLOYED, not committed): `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`communityStats` files the ranks daily as `squadRanks/{YYYY-MM-DD}`, keeps 7 days, copies the one from 7 days ago to `squadRanks/current` with `baseDay`), `firestore.rules` (comment), README. Firestore: today's ranks seeded as `squadRanks/2026-10-25`
- Camera photo of a cross-origin stream (Elegoo Centauri Carbon 2 …, cut out of a window capture) no longer shows the record / photo / folder buttons: they were hidden with a 150 ms fade while the capture is taken two frames later; now hidden at once — `renderer/css/40-printers.css` (`.cam-snap-hide > .cam-cap-bar`)
- Tiger Squad → Deals: announcements and link-only deals, managed in the TigerHub admin. A `squadDeals` doc has a `kind` — `deal` or `announcement` (information, no code: eyebrow, title, card text, card button, side-card text in light markdown — "## " heading, "- " bullet, "1. " step, "> " note —, one button to an https or mailto link; same status, dates, countries and drag-and-drop order as the deals, dashed card, transparent art on the warm gradient). Every text a member reads is per language `{ en, fr, … }` (Studio shows the member's language, else English, else the first filled; an older plain string reads as French). `codeMode: "link"`: no code — "Unlock the offer" claims it (same countdown, counters, "I used it", "My codes") and shows the link. The hard-coded "Become a partner" card and side card are gone: it is now an announcement in Firestore with its 11 languages (taken from the old locale keys), next to a new "Join the Discord" announcement (11 languages, its own illustration — `assets-src/img/tigersquad/ann-discord*.png`) — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (`annCard`, `annSide`, `mdLite`, `linkOnly` / `got` in `dealParts`, announcements in `sortDeals`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqd-ann`, `.sqd-side--ann`, `.sqd-md-list`, `.sqd-md-note`); i18n `squadDealsUnlock`, `squadDealsUnlocked`, `squadDealsOfferAvailable`, `squadAnnMore` (× 11), the 17 `squadDealsPitch*` / `squadPartner*` keys removed. - Tiger Squad → Deals: new banner scene (the tiger behind the desk, paws on a gift, between a shopping bag, % coins, coupons and a globe with pins — generated in the mascot style, one transparent layer per object) replacing the CSS tiger + coins; the brand card becomes "TigerTag ecosystem · Become a partner · Put your deals in front of the makers of the Squad, in your country or everywhere, and get seen · Join the ecosystem" with its own illustration (the tiger shaking hands with a spool shopkeeper, market stall, globe with pins); the cards of a row are as tall as each other (no longer every row as tall as the tallest card) — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (hero `.hero-scene`, `pitchCard`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-hero.sqd-hero` scene variables, `.sqd-pitch`, `.sqd-coin*` removed), the scene built from separate layers `assets/img/tigersquad/deals-hero/{bag,coins,globe,tiger,gift,tickets,sparkle}.webp` (`dealsSceneHtml`, `.sqd-scene`): the tiger, wearing the TigerTag cap, stands still BEHIND the desk (its own plane under the desk), the gift (beside him) and the other objects stand ON the desk's top stripe, in a 3.4 : 1 box (`--hero-art-w` 560 px); light animation — coins float, globe sways, tickets rock, sparkles twinkle, `assets/img/tigersquad/deals-partner.webp` (masters + sources in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/`); i18n `squadDealsPitchKicker`, `squadDealsPitchTitle`, `squadDealsPitchCta`, `squadDealsPitchSubject` reworded, `squadDealsPitchBody` (× 11)
- Tiger Squad → Deals: the grid is light — a card shows the illustration (discount badge, countdown), the partner, the title and one state line (the code once got, or the XP to go); clicking it opens a side card from the right, like a spool's detail panel, with the large illustration, the full description, the dates / countries and every action (get my code, copy, see the offer, "I used it" — held 1.2 s with `setupHoldToConfirm` and its green `.hold-progress` fill + hold hint bubble, so a stray click cannot file the deal away — lock bar); the app's » close tab on its left edge (`.pp-close-tab`, long press = close every side card, as elsewhere: `_setupCloseTabHold(root)` now rewires a tab a view renders itself, and `_closeAllSidePanels` closes the deal side card), the card again or Esc closes it, and it stays open across refreshes; while the deals load the view shows shimmering placeholder cards (no more "Coming soon" flash); the brand card is kicker + title + "Contact the team" — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (`dealParts`, `dealCard`, `dealSide`, `setOpen` in `wireDeals`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`_renderDealsView` passes `openId`, `loading`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqd-side*`, `.sqd-close-tab`, `.sqd-card-state`, `.sqd-card--skel`); i18n `squadDealsClose` (× 11), `squadDealsPitchBody` removed
- Tiger Squad → Deals: offer cards in one format for all, like the filament cards — fixed width (165–185 px), square illustration showing the product whole on white, every row as tall as its tallest card, one-line title, three-line description; the discount badge bigger at the top right, the claimed code's countdown on the illustration at the bottom left, the code on one line; a last "Your offer here?" card for brands, "Contact the team" opening an e-mail to the public contact address with a prefilled subject (also under "Coming soon" when there is no live deal) — `renderer/quests/squad-deals.js` (`pitchCard`, `PARTNER_MAIL`, timer moved into `.sqd-card-art`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqd-grid`, `.sqd-card-art`, `.sqd-card-badge`, `.sqd-timer`, `.sqd-code`, `.sqd-pitch`); i18n `squadDealsPitchKicker`, `squadDealsPitchTitle`, `squadDealsPitchBody`, `squadDealsPitchCta`, `squadDealsPitchSubject` (× 11). The deals list measures the demand: a summary (codes asked for, last 7 days, marked used, used / asked) and on each deal "N codes asked for · +N in 7 days · N marked used" (`claimed` counter + `claims` count queries, new `claimedWeek` in `listDeals`). Firestore: cap and polo moved to the top; the 8 deal illustrations re-made square (white margins cut, product filling the frame, the polo's "M" size badge removed)
- Tiger Squad → Deals: the banner was squeezed to ~40 px as soon as the deal cards arrived (the view is a scrolling flex column and its blocks could shrink); they keep their height now — `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqd-view > *`)
- Pre-commit on Windows: `check-agent-rules` crashed on every commit (the repo root came from `URL.pathname`, "/C:/…", giving "C:\C:\…") and, once past that, reported 30 false errors on a CRLF checkout (`core.autocrlf=true`); the root now comes from `fileURLToPath` and files are read with CRLF turned into LF; `i18n-remove.mjs` had the same path defect — `scripts/check-agent-rules.mjs`, `scripts/i18n-remove.mjs` (from a maintainer's Windows report)
- Quest "Share your friend link" (daily + Discovery 5): the text now says where it is done — the Friends menu's "Share link" button (the header's Share button deliberately does not count) — i18n `questDesc_shareFriendLink` reworded (× 11)
- Daily quest "Find a <brand> filament in the catalogue": opening that brand's product card from a catalogue search did not count — only the Brand filter did. Opening a catalogue product now counts its brand (also for Explorer 5 "brands explored"); the quest text says so — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_catViewProductCard`); i18n `questDesc_findBrand` reworded (× 11)
- Edit my profile: changing the photo dropped you out of the window — Escape in the photo cropper also closed "Edit my profile" (both listened to the key). The window now ignores Escape and backdrop clicks while the cropper is open, and comes back once the photo is uploaded or cancelled — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_cropperOpen`, `_changeAvatarFromPanel`)
- Tiger Quests: the secret mission card (and the others) flashed at random — every XP, Firestore or window-focus update rewrote the whole view, restarting the cards' entrance and legendary glow animations. The view is now left alone when its markup has not changed (signature guard) — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`renderQuestsView`)
- Tiger Quests: Studio 2.43.0 showed the secret mission "Behind the scenes" (d6) as a plain sixth Discovery level with raw key names, before Discovery 1–5 were won — it does not know secret levels. The server now sends secret levels only to an app that declares them (`secretLevels: true` in the check-in) — backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`dailyQuest`), README; Studio `renderer/quests/index.js` (`checkIn`)
- My profile: with 5 social links the left column ran past the window — Community moved to the right column and Advanced to the left, both columns fit again — `renderer/inventory.html`
- Tiger Squad leagues: your own row (country, national league, Makers worldwide) lost its flag / theme colours — the selected-row rule painted its own orange fill over them; it now only adds the orange frame and glow on a coloured row. Flag bands are mixed into the dark row instead of made transparent, so a white or yellow stripe stays a mid tone and the rank (now white) and names stay readable — `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-row.is-me`, `.sqr-row.has-flag`)
- View bar: a hidden group or button stayed on screen (the display rule beat `hidden`) — e.g. Tiger Quests in a friend's view — `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css`
- Tiger Quests → Missions: "Join the TigerSquad" showed no XP (its mission pays 0 because joining is paid as the TigerSquad bonus) — the row now shows the +150 XP it really brings (`xpElsewhere` in the quests context) — `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/quests/index.js`
- Social links could be piled up (six variants of tiktok.com…) and each one paid +5 XP: a profile now keeps at most 5 links, one per site (the add row disappears at 5), and the social XP counts each KNOWN network once (X, Instagram, YouTube, TikTok, Facebook, LinkedIn, Twitch, Discord, GitHub, WhatsApp) — a made-up site or a second link to the same network earns nothing — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_cleanSocials`, `SOCIALS_MAX`, `_renderSocialsEditor`); backend `functions/xpRules.js` (`SOCIALS_MAX`, `SOCIAL_NETWORKS`, `socialNetworksOf`), `functions/setProfile.js` (`cleanSocials` keeps one per site, max 5, extra ones dropped not refused), `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (social part), test; not deployed
- Daily quests: "Find a filament from <brand>" could draw Atome3D, a brand with no product in the catalogue yet, so the quest could not be done — taken out; the draw is now Rosa3D (4× weight), Overture, Polymaker, Sunlu, eSun, Bambu Lab, ELEGOO, Lotactree, Smart3DPT, R3D, Landu and Jamg He — every one with products in the catalogue (44 to 894); Prusament removed — backend `functions/quests.js` (`DAILY_POOL`, not deployed)

- League banner rank card: the "Tied with …" line under the next rivals (often empty: "Tied with —").

## v2.43.0 — 2026-10-24

### Added
- Printer settings → Camera → "Start the camera automatically" (per printer, every brand, off by default; saved as `camAutoStart` on the printer doc — the printers rule is owner-only with no field whitelist, no rules deploy): once the printer is online its board feed starts on its own, re-checked on each board render and live tick — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_autoStartCams`, `openPrinterAddForm`, printer form save), `renderer/css/40-printers.css` (`.pba-toggle-row`); the explanation lives in an ⓘ hover bubble beside the title (`.eac-info`, now styled app-wide in `renderer/css/60-modals.css`; a click on it does not flip the switch)
- Tiger Quests backend (phase 1, in the `TigerTag_Firebase_Backend` repo): definition table + pure evaluators (journey Discovery 1–5 / Explorer 1–5 with level bonuses gated by lower levels and XP thresholds, daily quest 10 + 4 × 5 + 30 drawn per user per local day, +50 update / +50 Speedrun within 3 days of the GitHub release), wired into the XP recompute as new breakdown parts `daily` / `updates` / `journey`; new `dailyQuest` check-in endpoint; rarity rebalance (Tiger Scale 1000, Tiger Spool / Touch / POD 800, mobile app / TD1S 300, TigerSquad 150, RFID reader 100 and validated by a Tiger POD, first rack / list 20); rules: `telemetry/studio.quest` map (≤ 300 keys) + owner-read `quests/{doc}`; 17 unit tests (`npm test`); counter keys for the app listed in the spec — backend `functions/quests.js` (new), `functions/supportWebhooks.js`, `functions/test/quests.test.js` (new), `functions/package.json`, `firebase.json`, `firestore.rules`, `README.md`; Studio `docs/QUESTS.md`
- Internal — Tiger Quests implementation spec (daily quest, update/Speedrun bonus, 20-rank journey with Discovery 1–5 and Explorer 1–5, rarity rebalance, chip workshop, phases) — `docs/QUESTS.md`
- Klipper / OctoPrint add flow in clear steps: protocol (brand picker) → printer model on its own card (a click picks and moves on, "Not in the list" skips) → Scan or IP → the final settings card; the mode, scan and manual-address steps recall the printer being added (model picture + name, or the generic one when skipped) — `renderer/printers/model-list.js` (`pickedModelHtml`, `onPick`), `renderer/printers/klipper/add-flow.js`, `renderer/printers/octoprint/add-flow.js`, `renderer/css/40-printers.css`
- Printer camera photo + video capture: hovering any printer camera (side panel or camera wall) shows record, photo and folder buttons (the folder opens that printer's captures in Finder / Explorer; buttons keep their real size on the zoomed camera wall). Photos keep the stream's native resolution; videos are taken from the SOURCE, never re-encoded from the page — Bambu RTSPS H.264 and http MJPEG copied untouched by ffmpeg (MJPEG stamped at arrival time), Snapmaker stock-firmware stills read straight from the camera at its own refresh rate, blob: JPEG frames muxed untouched at their real timing, WebRTC/MediaStream cameras (Creality, Anycubic) through MediaRecorder at 25 Mbps; 10-minute cap. Files are filed per printer in `Pictures/Tiger Studio/Captures/<brand>_<MAC|serial|id>/Photos|Videos/` (placeholder MACs ignored) with a `printer.json` keeping every name / IP / model the printer had, so the history survives a new IP or a rename — `renderer/printers/cam-snapshot.js` (new), `main.js` (Camera captures), `preload.js` (`camCapture`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`initCamSnapshot` wiring), `renderer/css/40-printers.css`
- Demo mode: idle camera views of the real printers (Bambu Lab X1C and A1, Elegoo Centauri Carbon 2, Creality Ender-3 V4, FlashForge AD5X, Snapmaker U1, Anycubic Kobra X), one still per model at the camera's native resolution, for an idle demo camera and mockups (not wired into the simulator yet) — `services/demo/idle/`
- Tiger Quests in Studio (phase 2): a Tiger Quests button in the sidebar under Notifications (visible collapsed or not, badge = what is left today) and a daily-quest chip next to the XP chip (today's progress, green when the set is complete) open the Quests views. Tiger Quests and the TigerSquad are VIEWS of the main area, like the stock and printer views: the view bar, after Printers, has a Tiger Quests group (today's quests n/5 with a bar → Today view; the current journey level, e.g. "🏕️ Discovery 1 3/5" → Journey view) and a TigerSquad group (tier, XP, world rank → TigerSquad view: countries, makers, achievements, history; every other way into the rankings opens this view too); the search bar, the stock filters and the colour bars are hidden there; both groups are hidden in a friend's view. The Quests views take the look of the Tiger Quests presentation page — one dark game world (cut-corner panels, diamond check boxes, skewed segmented bars, mono numbers with an orange glow, a gold full-set box): Today shows the day's quest log (earned / 60 XP on a segmented track) next to the current level's card; Journey shows the rank map (20 nodes with their level pips, ranks 3–20 coming soon) above the level cards; the TigerSquad view follows the light / dark theme — Today (open the app + 4 missions drawn by the server, full-set bar, streak, time to the next draw) and Journey (20 rank chips, ranks 3–20 "coming soon", each level with its 5 action bars, bonus and state: won / in progress / finish the levels below first / unlocks at N XP); a celebration card (burst, sound, +XP) on every mission, full set and level won. The app checks in with `dailyQuest` on launch, on window focus (10 min apart) and at local midnight, follows `quests/{daily,journey}` live, and reports what the user does as batched counters in `telemetry/studio.quest`: theme to light, storage view, weight saves, notes, favourites, spools put in racks (drag or auto-fill), printer rename / arrange, camera opened (wall, board, side panel) / photo / video, list created / made public, buy link, minimum stock, .ttag export, table sort, friend link shared, friend stock visited, What's New, demo printer, print finished (a real printer going from running to done), spool assigned to a printer slot (Bambu, Creality, Elegoo, Anycubic, Snapmaker, FlashForge), chip scan opening a spool, chip recycle / format to TigerTag Init, NFC tester read, catalogue brand / material filters; gauges for spools in racks and distinct materials. The journey's level table comes from the server (`dailyQuest` answer) — `renderer/quests/index.js` (new), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (new), `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/inventory.js` (`_renderQuestPill`, `_questGauges`, `_questPrintEdges`, counter calls), `renderer/printers/cam-snapshot.js`, `renderer/rfid_protocol/tigertag/index.js`, `renderer/printers/{bambulab,creality,elegoo,anycubic,snapmaker,flashforge}/index.js`, `renderer/CODEMAP.md`, `docs/QUESTS.md`, `docs/i18n-keys.md`, `llms.txt`; backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`dailyQuest` returns `levels` + `rewards`)
- TigerSquad view: the Makers tab is gone (the makers league is the third column of the first tab); every way into the rankings opens that tab, now named Rankings, each of its three columns titled (Countries League · 🇫🇷 France league · Makers worldwide). Rankings tab: three leagues side by side — the countries league, the member's own country league (only the members of their country ranked by XP, theirs highlighted, "🇫🇷 #1 in France of 80" on top) and the makers worldwide (the Makers list) — one column when narrow; in the Makers ranking each member shows their rank inside their own country next to the flag (🇫🇷 #3), and your line reads "#2 worldwide of 138 · 🇫🇷 #1 in France of 80" — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_openSquadBoard`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- a Quests / TigerSquad view restored at launch kept the stock filters, the "+" and the colour bars on screen (the view chrome is now set in renderInventory, which every render goes through).
- XP history in a side card that slides in from the right (like Notifications): the TigerSquad view's History tab, the "XP gained" notification and every other way to the history open it; it shows the cached history at once, then refreshes, and closes on a click anywhere outside it — `renderer/inventory.html` (`#xpHistPanel`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`openXpHistory`, `closeXpHistory`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Quests Journey view: the rank's levels in a carousel (arrows + snap) that opens on the next level to finish and keeps its place while progress updates; the rank map is one sideways strip; a "Legendary items & rarity" loot ladder lists every XP item by rarity in Diablo colours (legendary orange: Tiger Scale, Spool, Touch, POD — epic green: phone app, TD1S — rare yellow: USB scale, RFID reader, TigerSquad — uncommon blue — common white), owned ones lit with a check, Tiger devices drawn in their rarity colour — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`lootHtml`, carousel), `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `renderer/inventory.js` (`loot` for initQuests, `state.xpBreakdown` from the live account doc, `SQUAD_XP`)
- Daily quests screen redesigned (Quests → Today): a banner (workshop + tiger artwork, calendar icon, title, caption), today's progress (n / total on a segmented bar, earned / available XP), the full-set chest (locked / one quest left / earned with a check), one row per quest (icon tile, title + description, Quick / Exploration / Challenge badge — presentation only, 0/1 or 1/1 bar, XP, status with "done … ago"), and a side column: daily streak with 5 flame pips and the next streak bonus, next draw countdown with a ring and the reset time, the real reward table, your stats (quests completed, days played, current / best streak, daily XP); stacks on narrow windows — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`todayHtml`, `questRow`, `MISSION_LOOK`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `assets/img/quests/daily-quests-banner.webp` + `full-set-chest.webp` (masters in `assets-src/img/quests/`)
- Streak bonus: +100 XP every 5 consecutive days with the daily full set; the server also keeps when each part of the day was done and the lifetime count of quests done (backend `functions/quests.js` `advanceDaily`, 18 unit tests) — `docs/QUESTS.md`
- Daily quests screen polish: the full-set chest at 96 px on the left with a golden halo — dimmed with a small lock and the reward still to win (gold) until the set is done, then full brightness, glow, green check and the XP in green; the day's bar is now one segment per quest (as many as the day has), each filling with a short animation when its quest is done; quest rows brighten with a soft accent glow on hover and keep a light success tint when done; a quest completed while the app is open pops its check, fills its bar and flashes its XP — once, never replayed on re-render; streak pips orange vs blue-grey with the day count larger; the next-draw ring runs orange to blue; badges one size. Respects reduced motion — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`freshlyDone`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Daily quests jackpot: completing the full set while the daily screen is shown plays a ~2 s sequence — a light sweeps the progress bar, the bonus panel lights up, the grey chest turns gold with a short scale and rays, 16 gold dots / stars / orange confetti burst from it (inside the panel, no mouse capture), the XP counts up from 0, rises and shines, the green check pops; then a stable earned state. Only on a real incomplete → complete change seen while the app is open: never on launch with the set already done, never on re-render (data refreshes during the sequence wait for its end), never under reduced motion (earned state at once). Clicking the open chest (or Enter / Space on it) plays the jackpot again, display only. The locked chest is fully grey, with a little gold light on hover; a quest done gets a short green pulse around its card; while the daily screen is shown the top toast and its sound are skipped (no double celebration). Display only: XP still comes from the server — `renderer/quests/index.js` (jackpot, `countUp`, `BURST`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Daily quests: the chest stays closed until the set is done (new closed-chest artwork, grey while locked); in the jackpot the closed chest turns gold then fades into the open one. The next-draw ring is the Tiger Touch ring — a dark blue track (#26456F) and one blue (#3da4f0) rounded arc from 12 o'clock that charges up to midnight — `renderer/quests/index.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `assets/img/quests/full-set-chest-closed.webp` (master `assets-src/img/quests/full-set-chest-closed.png`)
- TigerSquad → Rankings view redesigned: a hero (workshop + tigers + trophy artwork, "TIGERSQUAD — The maker league", tagline, the account's league card with tier badge, XP, national and world rank — or a Join card); My rank (avatar, league, XP, rank in the country out of N makers, world rank out of N, next rival = the nearest member above with more XP and the real gap, a progress bar toward them, "top of the board" at #1, tie shown, no-country and non-member states); Community pulse from the public totals the TigerHub page reads (publicStats/community + its daily history: total XP across all accounts, spools tracked with the TigerTag / TigerTag+ / Certified / TigerData / TigerData+ split, connected printers with the top brands), SVG sparklines over the real history, growth only for spools and printers (none for XP: the rarity rebalance rescaled every total), "updated … ago" from the doc, read when the view opens and at most once a minute; the three leagues as cards (title only — the account's own ranks are in the hero card) with gold / silver / bronze medals for the top 3, the account's row highlighted, the top 5 plus your own row, and View all for the full list. The Achievements tab shows its list again (the rankings block hid it: its display rule overrode `hidden`). The whole TigerSquad view is one dark world like the Quests views — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (new), `renderer/inventory.js` (`_openSquadBoard` inline, `_loadSquadPulse`, `_squadRankCtx`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `assets/img/tigersquad/tigersquad-rankings-banner-v4.webp`, `rank-medal-1..3.webp` (masters in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/`)
- TigerSquad Rankings, tighter: the account's rank lives once, in the hero's right card (avatar, name, league badge, XP, rank in the country and worldwide out of N makers, next rival with the real XP gap and a progress bar, or the top-spot / tie / no-country / join states); the standalone My rank section is gone; Community pulse is one compact strip (the intro carries the context — what it is, "across N accounts · updated … ago" — then total XP, spools, printers: icon, value, label, over a full-width sparkline of the real history drawn behind the text and fading in toward the right); hero 220 px, tabs 40, strip ~90, rows 50: at 1600×900 the three leagues show their header and 4 rows, 5 at 1920×1080; new banner artwork: one continuous workshop scene from edge to edge (no fade, no added band), the hero 220 px tall so the crop runs from the tigers' heads to the trophy like the mockup, the title kept readable with a text shadow — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Removed i18n: `squadRankCountryLine`, `squadMyRank`, `squadMyRankSub`, `squadRankIn`, `squadRankWorld`, `squadPulsePrintersSub` (no longer used) — 11 locales
- TigerSquad rank card rebuilt like the mockup: avatar column (avatar, flag, globe) beside name, league, XP, "#2 in France", "#2 worldwide" — tighter type, no "out of N makers"; the next rival with avatar, name, XP to go, a chevron and the bar. The TigerSquad and Tiger Quests views no longer sit in a panel of their own (no dark box, grid or padding around them): their blocks sit straight on the view — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Removed i18n: `squadNoCountry`, `squadOutOfMakers` (no longer used) — 11 locales
- TigerSquad "Achievements" and the Journey's "Legendary items & rarity" merged: the view bar's TigerSquad group now holds the sections — the Gold chip (rankings), Items (a view of its own) and History (the side card) — so the page has no tabs under the banner; the Items view shows the next squad level, then every XP item on the rarity ladder (Diablo colours, owned ones lit), the TigerTag chips included under Common with the XP they earned; the ladder is no longer under the Journey (the window keeps its plain Achievements list) — `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/quests/index.js` (`questLootHtml`)
- Added i18n: `squadItems` — 11 locales
- Sidebar: no more XP / world-rank / daily-quest chips under the account name (the Tiger Quests button keeps its badge); the XP and world-rank chips moved to the header, after the TigerSquad badge, and open the rankings (`_paintOwnXpChips`, patched in place); the view bar's TigerSquad group is an icon toggle like Inventory — Rankings, Items, History (name on hover, the sliding selection bubble) — with no figures in it; vertical separators before the Tiger Quests and TigerSquad groups like between the other groups — `renderer/inventory.js` (`renderSupporterBadge`, `_renderQuestPill`, `_renderProgressChips`), `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/quests/index.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `renderer/css/10-settings.css` (dead `.sb-xp-pill` / `.sb-rank-pill` rules removed)
- View bar, Tiger Quests group: an icon toggle like the others — today (checklist) and journey (new compass icon), each with a thin progress line under the icon, the figures in the hover name ("Daily quests: 5 of 5 done", "Discovery 2 · 4/5"), the sliding selection bubble — `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/inventory.js` (`_renderProgressChips`, `_setBar`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `assets/svg/icons/icon_compass.svg` (new), `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css` (`.icon-compass`)
- Tiger Quests: "Journey" renamed "Missions" (11 locales); the Missions view drops its carousel and sideways strip — the map of the 20 ranks wraps, then every built level is shown, rank by rank (Discovery, Explorer) in a grid; a rank on the map jumps to its levels. The daily screen's banner now uses the TigerSquad hero layout (same height and type: "TIGER QUESTS", "Daily quests", "Small actions, big progress!") on the daily artwork — `renderer/quests/index.js`, `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Removed i18n: `questsPrevLevel`, `questsNextLevel`, `questsDailySub` (no longer used) — 11 locales
- Tiger Quests → Missions redesigned: a 150 px hero on new artwork (a tiger explorer over a glowing map in the workshop) with "MISSIONS", the tagline and a card for the current chapter (its levels won and a bar); the 20 chapters in one sliding row with arrows (chapter number, icon, name, world, level pips; current one lit, built ones selectable, the rest "coming soon"); the selected chapter's heading (icon, name, world · a line about it, "n / 5 levels completed" with segments); its five levels as illustrated cards in five columns (three below 1450 px, two below 1000): name, theme, done / 5 and the bonus or a COMPLETED badge, a bar (orange, green when won), an illustration slot (warm glow until the artwork arrives), the five missions (check when done, half dot when started, count, XP), and a footer with the bonus and the state. Unused styles of the previous journey and daily layouts removed — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`journeyHtml`, `missionCard`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`, `assets/img/quests/missions-banner.webp` (masters `assets-src/img/quests/missions-banner*.png`)
- Added i18n: `questsCompleted`, `questsInProgress`, `questsLevelsDone`, `questsPrevChapter`, `questsNextChapter`, `questRankDesc_1`, `questRankDesc_2`; removed `questsWon` (no longer used) — 11 locales
- Tiger Quests: the 20 rank emoji are replaced by SVG icons drawn as masks in the current colour (1 tent, 2 hills at sunrise, 3 mountains, 4 desert, 5 canoe, 6 sailboat, 7 wave, 8 ice crystal, 9 aeroplane, 10 balloon, 11 satellite, 12 moon, 13 planet, 14 comet, 15 ringed planet, 16 compass, 17 star, 18 sparkles, 19 galaxy, 20 infinity) — chapter row, chapter heading, hero card, view-bar label — `assets/svg/ranks/rank-01…20.svg` (new), `renderer/quests/index.js` (`rankIcon`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Settings → Data: "Download JSON" is now "Export .ttag" — the whole inventory (twins and chip backups included) in the TigerTag file format, with a busy state while the backups are read; the raw JSON download is gone. The inventory bar's Export / Import buttons are removed (import stays in Settings → Data and by drag-drop; selection export removed with them) — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_exportAllTtag`), `renderer/inventory.html`, `docs/i18n-keys.md`
- View bar, TigerSquad Items button: a progress line under the star toward the next squad level (Bronze → 20 TigerTag chips, Gold → 50 Certified), figures in its hover name — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_renderProgressChips`), `renderer/inventory.html`
- i18n: `settingsExport`, `stgDataHelp` reworded; removed `ttagExportShort`, `ttagImportShort` — 11 locales

### Changed
- XP History, cleaner: the XP Evolution curve is one smooth line drawn like the TigerSquad pulse — the balance sampled at even steps over the range (7D: 28, 30D: 30, 90D: 45, All: 60 samples; `chartSeries`) instead of one point per transaction, then a monotone cubic (never dips between two steps) in an amber→orange gradient with a soft glow over a fading orange fill, a pulsing dot on today's balance; no more green / red dot per transaction nor their legend — hovering anywhere moves one dashed guide and one focus dot to the nearest sample, its tooltip giving the time, the balance and what changed in that step (`sampleTipHtml`; the per-transaction `tipHtml` is removed). The activity log shows its category icons bare, without the coloured box — `renderer/quests/xp-history.js` (`chartSvg`, `smoothPath`), `renderer/inventory.js` (curve hover), `renderer/quests/quests.css`; i18n `xpGain`, `xpLoss` removed (× 11)
- XP History banner: its own scene in the mascot style — the tiger, waist-up behind the desk, pointing at a monitor with a rising XP curve, star coins, an open logbook with a quill and an hourglass (transparent, one layer, on the shared CSS wall + desk) instead of the TigerSquad scene — `assets/img/quests/xp-history-scene.webp` (master + generated original in `assets-src/img/quests/`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.xph-hero`)
- Tiger Quests Explorer 2: "Try a demo printer" replaced by "Turn on camera auto-play for a printer" (`e2.autoCam`, counter `camAutoStart`, bumped when the switch is turned on in a printer's settings or on a new printer; a Demo printer does not count) — backend `functions/quests.js`; Studio `renderer/inventory.js`, `docs/QUESTS.md`; i18n `questAct_e2_autoCam` added (× 11); `questAct_e2_demo` kept until every journey doc has been recomputed (old docs still list `e2.demo`), remove it afterwards
- Own profile header: the XP and world-rank chips moved from the badge line to the Share line, after the Share button — `renderer/inventory.js` (own banner markup, `_paintOwnXpChips`)
- Creality side panel follows Snapmaker: the print card is always shown right under the video (idle: printer picture, "No active print", 0 %, Standby, 0m), then Control, Temperature; in Control the disable-motors, files and light buttons are three square icon buttons heading the info column (Elegoo `.elg-ctrl-actions`, new `.cre-action-btn--motors` icon-only variant) — they get their own row only while printing, when Control is hidden — `renderer/printers/creality/index.js`, `renderer/css/55-creality.css` (`.cre-ctrl-disable` removed), `renderer/css/50-snapmaker.css`
- Demo printers (simulator, `demo: true`) never earn XP: the app drops their quest actions (`questBump(key, n, printer)` skips a demo printer — slot assign in all six brands, camera open, rename; the cam wall and board arrange count only when a real printer exists), and the backend leaves them out of the printer count (+20 XP each, printer missions, daily camera quest), of the XP history and of the community stats — Studio `renderer/quests/index.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/printers/{anycubic,bambulab,creality,elegoo,flashforge,snapmaker}/index.js`; backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`realPrinters`), `functions/index.js`
- XP history is one page (view mode `xpHistory`; the History button, the XP notification and the TigerSquad views open it — the side card is gone): a banner, four tiles across the page (XP gained / lost with a big green up / red down arrow) (total XP, XP gained / lost and activities over 30 days), the XP balance curve over 7D / 30D / 90D / All with a dot per transaction (green gain, red loss) and a tooltip (date, activity, XP, balance), drawn at its card's real width and redrawn on resize, beside the XP breakdown ring over 30 days at the same height (gains by category — Daily quests, Missions, TigerSquad, Social, Gear, Inventory, Bonuses — every loss as Adjustments, the net in the middle); then the activity log (date, activity, category chip, XP, balance) across the page, with category / gains-losses filters, search and pages of 12; one column under 1350 px. Read from the server log `users/{uid}/xpHistory` (up to 2000 entries, cached 60 s) — `renderer/quests/xp-history.js` (new), `renderer/inventory.js` (`openXpHistory` → the page, `_loadXpLog`, `_xphCtx`, `_renderXpHistView`, `_paintXpHistView`; `closeXpHistory` removed), `renderer/inventory.html` (`#invXpHistView`; `#xpHistPanel` removed), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.xph-*`; side-card rules removed)
- TigerSquad Community pulse: the sparklines are inset from the card's edges (12 px each side, 8 px from the bottom) so the curves never touch the border — `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-spark`)
- Tiger Quests → Today: the banner takes the Tutorial missions banner's style — the orange "TIGER QUESTS" tag, "DAILY QUESTS" as the white title and the caption in spaced capitals under it (same markup, size and type, `.qm-hero.qd-hero`); its scene stays on the right; the scene is dropped under 765 px inside — `renderer/quests/index.js` (`todayHtml`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Tiger Quests: the Missions view is named for what it is — "Tutorial missions" (banner title, view-bar label, chapter row), 11 locales — `questsJourney` (value changed), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (narrow-banner thresholds for the longer title)
- Tiger Quests → Today: the full-set bonus is +20 XP instead of +30 (a perfect day is 50 XP: open 10 + 4 × 5 + 20); the app's fallbacks follow — backend `functions/quests.js` (`DAILY.fullSet`) + tests, `renderer/quests/index.js`, `docs/QUESTS.md`
- Tiger Quests → Today: new full-set chest artwork in the mascot flat style — closed (brown planks, gold bands, a tiger-paw padlock) and the same chest open (coins, orange and blue gems, golden glow), drawn on the same square so the jackpot's cross-fade lines up — `assets/img/quests/full-set-chest-closed-v2.webp`, `full-set-chest-v2.webp` (masters in `assets-src/img/quests/`), `renderer/quests/index.js`
- Tiger Quests → Missions: the "+X XP" of every action row sits in the last column, so it lines up whether or not the row has a count (0/5) — `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qm-row-xp`)
- TigerSquad menu for non-members: Rankings, Items and XP history in the view bar open the "Join the TigerSquad" invitation instead of a view that is about membership; members are unchanged — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_squadGate`)
- Tiger Quests → Missions: the Explorer level bonuses follow the Discovery scale — +50 / 75 / 100 / 125 / 150 XP instead of +200 / 250 / 300 / 350 / 400 (the app reads them from the server's level table); backend `functions/quests.js` + its test, `docs/QUESTS.md`
- Tiger Quests → Missions: each level card has its illustration — ten transparent scenes in the banners' mascot style (First look, My stock, My printers, Shopping & lists, Community, Organised stock, Printing, Social, Chip workshop, Collector), contained over the card's warm glow, 24–39 KB each — `assets/img/quests/mission-{d1…d5,e1…e5}.webp` (masters + generated originals in `assets-src/img/quests/missions/`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.qm-art`)
- TigerSquad leagues: the three columns have the same height (every row 52 px) and show as many rows as the view has room for, at least 5 — measured after layout and on every resize; when your own row ranks below the cut it takes the last slot, under a dashed mark — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`setLeagueRows`, `leagueRows`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`_fitSquadLeagues`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- TigerSquad leagues: countries shown like members — a round avatar with the country's flag inside instead of an emoji (also the national league icon, the small flags next to names and the ranks in the banner card); the top three countries get a gold / silver / bronze ring matching their medal. Flags from flag-icons (MIT, 270 square SVGs, licence kept) — `assets/svg/flags/` (new, + `LICENSE`, `README.md`), `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`flagAv`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- TigerSquad leagues: a green ▲ / red ▼ with the number of places under each XP shows the rank movement since the start of the day, from a server baseline — the backend's `communityStats` writes the leagues' ranks on the first minute of each Paris day to `squadRanks/current` (`world`, `national`, `countries`; built exactly like the app's leagues; signed-in read, server-written) and the app compares the live leagues against it, so every device shows the same arrows; the old per-device localStorage baseline is removed — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`rankMoves`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`_loadSquadPulse`, `rankBase`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`; backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js` (`squadRankSnapshot`), `firestore.rules` (`squadRanks`), `README.md`
- TigerSquad Community pulse live: the totals are followed with onSnapshot while the view is open (stopped when it is left or on sign-out), each counter rolls to its new value (1.6 s ease-out, up from 0 the first time) and the gain pops out of it ("+40 XP", 1.8 s), as on the TigerHub page — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_loadSquadPulse`, `_stopSquadPulse`, `_rollPulse`), `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`pulseHtml` exported, `data-roll`), `renderer/quests/quests.css` (`.sqr-gain`)
- TigerSquad rank card in the banner: two columns — you (avatar, name, league, XP) with your country and world ranks on one line under it, and the next rival — 409 × 120 px instead of a tall stack, centred on the banner's height (it may overlap the desk and the printer) — `renderer/quests/squad-rankings.js` (`squadHeroHtml`, `.sqr-card-ranks`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Hero banners on narrow windows (each banner is a size container): the art shrinks down to 80 % at most, then the banner drops parts in steps instead of overlapping — TigerSquad: the next rival (< 1020 px inside), then the ranks line (< 790), then the scene (< 730); Missions: the level card (< 960, the chapter row below shows the same progress), then the scene (< 715); Today: the scene (< 765). The TigerSquad scene reserves only the tigers and the trophy, so the card may cover the printer — `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Tiger Quests → Missions and TigerSquad → Rankings: new banners in the same flat mascot style, as transparent scenes on the shared CSS backdrop — Missions: the tiger explorer with backpack and map pointing at a corkboard of linked mission cards, compass, rolled map, a 3D printer printing a gold trophy (centred between the title and the level card); TigerSquad: three tigers in blue hoodies with silver / gold / bronze medals, seen from the waist up behind the desk, their paws, the gold trophy and the printer resting on it (the scene is two layers of one image — the bodies under the CSS desk, what rests on the desk over it, `--hero-front` / `--hero-sink`), stars, a 3D printer printing a gold star (centred between the title and the rank card). In all three heroes the scene is a flex item between the title and the card (`.hero-scene`, fixed height, `--hero-scene-w` reserved): it never slides under the card; the desk is the hero's `::after`, the front layer the scene's `::after`. One set of variables now drives the three heroes: `--hero-wall` (colour or gradient), `--hero-desk`, `--hero-desk-h`, settable on any ancestor — `assets/img/quests/missions-scene.webp`, `assets/img/tigersquad/tigersquad-scene-v3.webp` + `tigersquad-scene-v3-front-v2.webp` (masters + generated originals in `assets-src/img/quests/` and `assets-src/img/tigersquad/`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Tiger Quests → Today: new banner in the flat mascot style (the tiger at its desk dreaming up a print, Eiffel tower on the printer, spool rack). The artwork is a transparent scene (objects only) set on the right; the wall and the desk are CSS, so the backdrop is customisable (`--hero-wall` / `--hero-desk` / `--hero-desk-h`, shared with the Missions and TigerSquad banners) — and the banner works at any width with no cropping; 150 px tall, the same height, padding and title sizes as the Missions banner — `assets/img/quests/daily-quests-scene.webp` (masters `assets-src/img/quests/daily-quests-scene.png` + the generated `daily-quests-scene-source.png`), `renderer/quests/quests.css`
- Catalogue product card opens at once: a click shows the card from what the catalogue already holds (brand, material, aspects, colours, photo, measure) in ~15 ms instead of waiting ~1.3 s for `product/get`; the detail (temperatures, description, links) then refines the same card in place, with no second slide — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_catApiFromItem`, `_catViewProductCard`, `_catViewShowProduct`)
- Side-card swap (a card opened over an open one): the close tab with the double chevron stays where it is until the new card has landed, and the ghost of the old card is removed at the real end of the slide (a busy frame used to let the tab jump and could uncover an empty strip); a swap started mid-swap drops the previous ghost — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_ghostSwapPanel`, `_setTab`)
- Catalogue Grid view fast on the full 18 000-product catalogue: the grid is virtualised — only the rows on screen plus 4 either side exist (~130 cards instead of thousands after a long scroll), the body padding keeps the full scroll height, moving the window trims and adds cards at the two ends in one Range delete so the cards that stay never reload their photo; a resize re-measures columns and card height. Typing no longer re-sorts 18 000 items with localeCompare (one Intl.Collator sort per sort key, cached as a rank), no longer rebuilds the Type / Brand / Material selects (only when the catalogue, the language or — for Series — the brand changes) and compares result lists by identity instead of joining every id. Measured on 18 581 products: a deep scroll step 7–20 ms flat (was 14 → 300 ms and growing), a keystroke ~5–15 ms of script. Images that leave the DOM before they were requested are unobserved, so dropped cards are freed — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_catViewGridRender`, `_catViewGridCards`, `_catSortRank`, `_catViewFillFacets`, `_catViewSearch`, `_imgForget`), `renderer/CODEMAP.md`
- Tiger Touch header icon: new 3/4-view trace with a narrower screen (PR #114, Ptitlouis6012) — `assets/svg/icons/icon_tigertouch_3d.svg`, `renderer/IoT/tigertouch/tigertouch.css`
- Internal — agent rules reorganised: one rule per file in `.agents/rules/` (29 files), `CLAUDE.md` "Rules" is now their index (critical rules applicable from the index alone) and the rest of `CLAUDE.md` is a corrected codebase reference; new `AGENTS.md` points to the index; `AGENT.md` (old inventory web-page spec) moved to `docs/INVENTORY-WEB-PAGE-SPEC.md`. New pre-commit guard `scripts/check-agent-rules.mjs`: fails when a rule file is not indexed, the index cites a missing file, a path cited in `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENTS.md` / a rule file does not exist, or a printer-plugin string table (Klipper, OctoPrint) misses a locale or has a shifted `s11()` row; unused `en.json` keys are a warning only (0 after the cleanup; keys built as t(`x${y}`) / t("x" + y) and keys named in renderer JSON such as `tutorial.json` count as used; `--orphans` lists them). Stale claims fixed: locale counts (11, incl. `nl`) in `llms.txt`, `README.md`, `docs/REVIEW-BRIEF.md` and two code comments; `Debug` described as a client-written preference (any account) instead of admin/server-only; `userProfiles` written only by `setProfile`; file sizes, brand count, file map — `CLAUDE.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `.agents/rules/*`, `llms.txt`, `README.md`, `docs/REVIEW-BRIEF.md`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/IoT/tigerscale/index.js`, `scripts/check-docs-drift.mjs`, `scripts/check-agent-rules.mjs`, `scripts/i18n-remove.mjs`, `.githooks/pre-commit`
- Klipper: a "Custom port" switch in the manual add form and in the printer settings — off by default, switching it on fills in 7125 ready to be typed over, opens by itself when the stored address carries a port (issue #112 — a non-standard Moonraker port had to be guessed as `:80`). Still stored in the ONE `ip` field as `host:port` (the form Studio and the Tiger Touch firmware both read; a separate `port` field would be ignored by Tiger Touch): split into the two boxes on read, merged on save, 7125 left implicit, invalid port refused, `host:port` typed in the address box moves its port to the Port box. Generic `schema.toForm` / `schema.fromForm` hooks let any brand show one stored field as several boxes, and a generic `toggleKey` / `toggleDefault` field option keeps a rarely-needed field behind a switch (`wireFieldToggles`) — `renderer/printers/klipper/plugin.js`, `renderer/printers/klipper/add-flow.js`, `renderer/printers/modal-helpers.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`

### Fixed
- Creality camera flashing black with the board card and the side panel both open: re-attaching a video to the stream it already had restarted it, and every printers snapshot rebuilt the open side panel (and its `<video>`) even when that printer's doc had not changed — `renderer/printers/creality/widget_camera.js` (`addCreCamConsumer`), `renderer/inventory.js` (`refreshOpenPrinterDetail({ fromSnapshot })`)
- a friend could send you a friend request again, and it could not be accepted (the friendship docs already exist, so the accept batch was refused) — the backend rule now refuses a request from someone already in your friends; Add friend says "You're already friends" and does not send; an incoming request from an existing friend is deleted on arrival and never shown; accepting one just clears it — `renderer/inventory.js` (`sendFriendRequest`, `_adfChanged`, `subscribeFriendRequests`, `acceptFriendRequest`), `friendAlready` (new, × 11); backend `firestore.rules`; integration guide rule + doc + changelog (committed)
- Product card and spool detail side cards: a transparent product photo sits on the spool colour like on the grid card, no longer on a dark square inside the colour frame; the photo stays hidden (plain colour) until its bytes are in, then fades in — no empty rounded frame drawn while it loads — `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css` (`.panel-img` inset rule), `renderer/inventory.js` (load listener adding `.img-loaded` to `.panel-img`)
- Bambu Lab H2S / H2D / H2D Pro / H2C / X2D: the light button drives both chamber strips (`chamber_light` + `chamber_light2`), on when either reports on; not yet seen on a real H2 (PR #113, Ptitlouis6012) — `renderer/printers/bambulab/lights.js`, `index.js`, `PROTOCOL.md`
- Elegoo CC1: Canvas trays shown in the side panel too; slots asked at connection, when a Canvas is plugged in and when a job ends, plus every 60 s (PR #118, Ptitlouis6012) — `renderer/printers/elegoo/sdcp.js`, `PROTOCOL.md`
- Klipper: part-fan percentage follows the `[fan] max_power` ceiling, like Mainsail (PR #124, Ptitlouis6012) — `renderer/printers/klipper/core.js`, `PROTOCOL.md`
- Klipper / OctoPrint cameras: a failed picture no longer stays dimmed, it retries by itself (3 s doubling to 30 s); `mjpegstreamer-adaptive` polls snapshots like Mainsail (PR #125, Ptitlouis6012) — `renderer/printers/cam-img-recovery.js`, `klipper/camera.js`, `octoprint/camera.js`, `docs/PRINTER-PLUGINS.md`

### Removed
- 230 unused i18n keys (left over from removed screens: old settings / credentials / account modals, retired debug tab, rack migration, encoder modal, old printer labels…), verified unreferenced anywhere in the repo, removed from all 11 locales with the new `scripts/i18n-remove.mjs` (text-preserving, aborts if any other key would change); 2036 → 1806 keys — `renderer/locales/*.json`, `docs/i18n-keys.md`, `llms.txt`

## v2.42.1 — 2026-10-19

### Changed
- Anycubic cloud printers: the printer record now also keeps the slicer's app id / secret / version (`acuCloudAppId`, `acuCloudAppSecret`, `acuCloudAppVersion`, returned by main's cloud-certs IPC next to the client certificate) so a Tiger Touch can sign the REST calls it sends (pause / resume / stop / light); a record without them is re-imported once. Owner-only document; masked in MCP output (`secret`) — `main.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`
- Tiger Touch header icon: the founder's 3/4-view artwork of the screen with its TIGER TOUCH wordmark (vector trace, 24 KB; the 1.3 MB embedded bitmap of the source dropped), sized to the neighbours' ink — `assets/svg/icons/icon_tigertouch_3d.svg`, `renderer/IoT/tigertouch/tigertouch.css`
- The account setup (language → pseudo + avatar) opens at sign-in whenever the account is incomplete: no pseudo on the account (`users/{uid}.displayName`, checked on the server after a 1 s grace — a name left in localStorage no longer counts, is no longer written back unvalidated, and only pre-fills the setup), no valid language (`users/{uid}/prefs/app.lang` not one of the 11), or no avatar (neither a photo nor a #rrggbb colour on the public profile); finishing it always saves the language shown, even if kept as proposed (`_accountSetupIncomplete`) — `renderer/inventory.js`
- Avatar colour on the public profile = ONE field, `color: "#rrggbb"` (any colour the user picks). Backend: `setProfile` writes only `color` (no longer `color: "custom"` + `customColor`); the 5 profiles still saying `color: "custom"` got their `customColor` copied into `color` by the admin-only one-off `migrateProfileColor` (nothing deleted — `customColor` stays for older app versions, nobody writes it any more). Studio reads both shapes (`profileColor`, the setup's avatar check) — without it, a friend whose profile said "custom" got no avatar colour — `renderer/inventory.js`, backend `functions/{setProfile,migrateProfileColor,index}.js`
- Public profile (`userProfiles/{uid}`) is now written only by the backend's `setProfile` Cloud Function — the Firestore rule refuses client writes, so Studio's own writes were being silently lost. Pseudo (account card + onboarding), avatar colour, photo (upload to `avatars/{uid}` then `photoFromStorage`, removal via `photoRemove`), social links, public visibility and a regenerated friend code (still claimed client-side on `publicKeys/{code}`) all go through `setProfile` (`renderer/inventory.js` `setProfile()` / `profileErrorText()`); its refusals are shown to the user instead of swallowed; the sign-in no longer republishes the profile (never a Google first name or e-mail prefix as a pseudo) and the friends-count write is gone (the backend keeps it). Pseudo rules aligned with the server: 2-40 characters (inputs and onboarding), no link or e-mail, 3 changes a day — `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/inventory.html`, `docs/firestore-schema.md`, `llms.txt`

### Fixed
- Elegoo Centauri Carbon (CC1, SDCP): the Canvas hub's four trays now show on the board (colour, material, name, brand) instead of a single "Ext." slot — SDCP command 324 asked at connection then every 30 s unless the status push says no Canvas (`AmsConnectStatus`) or the firmware refuses it; read-only. Format from the Home Assistant integration, checked against the CC1 demo printer, not yet on a real CC1 + Canvas (contributed by @Ptitlouis6012, PR #100, fixes #98) — `renderer/printers/elegoo/{sdcp,canvas}.js`, `renderer/printers/elegoo/PROTOCOL.md`, `scripts/test-elegoo-canvas.mjs`
- Internal: bundled product catalogue refreshed (`id_catalog`).
