## v2.42.0 — 2026-10-18

### Added
- Klipper and OctoPrint add flow: the choice card (Scan / Enter IP) now lists the connector's models with their pictures and a search ("Which printer is it?", optional) — the pick carries into the settings form as model + name. Shared host service `renderer/printers/model-list.js` (`mountModelList`) — `renderer/printers/{klipper,octoprint}/add-flow.js`, `renderer/css/40-printers.css`
- Remaining filament length next to the weight in the spool detail ("353 g ≈ 141 m"), estimated from the weight, the material's density and the spool's diameter; follows the weight slider live. Filament only (`id_type` 142) — never for resin, accessories or spare parts; hidden when the density or the diameter is unknown (issue #89) — `renderer/inventory.js` (`filamentLengthM`, `filamentLengthText`), `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css`
- **Demo mode (steps 0–5, open to every user on the desktop app)** — a printer simulator in the main process: one shared print engine per demo printer (heating → leveling → printing → complete → next print, accelerated 20×; pause / resume / stop = cancel; temperature setpoints ramp; colour changes follow the print's slots), a generic print library whose camera picture follows the print (paused freezes the frame, complete shows the finished object), and brand faces that answer the real protocol on a local address so the protocol cores connect unchanged — Klipper (Moonraker, AFC lanes), OctoPrint, FlashForge (AD5X + 4-slot station, serial / access code checked), Prusa (CORE One + MMU3, HTTP Digest), Creality (K2 Plus + CFS: ws 9999, Moonraker 7125, MJPEG 8080, thumbnails on a shared port-80 router that refuses non-local clients) Snapmaker (U1, 4 toolheads, JSON-RPC ws 7125, still camera) and Elegoo in two variants — CC2 + Canvas on an `aedes` MQTT broker (password checked) and CC1 on SDCP v3 WebSocket; the Add-a-printer card lists one row per variant; Bambu Lab (P1S + AMS: MQTT over TLS :8883 with access code, `pushall` / `get_version` / commands, JPEG-over-TLS camera :6000, FTPS :990 file server serving each print's `.3mf` with its plate thumbnail — PASV `0.0.0.0` and no EPSV, as on the firmware) and Anycubic (Kobra 3 V2 + ACE Pro: MQTT over TLS 1.2 :9883, FLV camera :18088 encoded on the fly by the bundled ffmpeg), both LAN only behind a self-signed demo certificate (`services/demo/certs/`) the drivers do not verify. Booth content tooling: `npm run demo:add-print` turns filmed footage into a library print (60 s loop, thumbnail, optional empty-plate / finished-part photos shown before and after the print), and booth events (opt-in) — about one print in three runs out of filament mid-way, pauses with the slot empty, then the spool is swapped and the print resumes. A demo printer prints all the time: the first print starts right away, each next one is drawn at random (never twice in a row), and filament colours are drawn from the app's preset palette (the "Select Color" sheet) — per printer at start-up, and for the slots a print uses at every print and follows the finished one after 4 s; only the user's Stop leaves it idle, Pause holds until Resume. Add-a-printer card shows a *Demo printers* section to every user (one click → a normal record flagged `demo: true` on `127.0.0.1:<port>`, or on a loopback host of its own for the fixed-port brands — macOS creates the missing `lo0` aliases in one administrator prompt), *Demo* tag on the board card and in the side-card title; the simulator starts / stops faces from the account's demo records. macOS measurements (loopback aliases, low ports) and the plan for the other brands in `docs/DEMO-MODE.md`; dependencies `aedes` 1.2.0 (MQTT broker, for the MQTT brands) and `ws` 8.21.3 (now direct, was transitive); `services/demo/prints/**` unpacked from the asar so the bundled ffmpeg can read the videos — `services/demo/{index,engine,library,addresses}.js`, `services/demo/faces/{common,klipper,octoprint,flashforge,prusa,creality,snapmaker,elegoo,bambulab,bambu-ftps,anycubic}.js`, `services/demo/prints/` (4 placeholder prints), `scripts/demo-render-prints.mjs`, `scripts/demo-add-print.mjs`, `main.js`, `preload.js`, `renderer/printers/demo-badge.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/css/40-printers.css`, `package.json`, `CODEMAP-main.md`, `renderer/CODEMAP.md`
  - Adding one: a single "Select a demo printer" row in the brand picker opens a side card with every model of every brand the simulator plays (≈400, grouped by brand, searchable); the pick becomes a demo printer of that model on its brand's own protocol (Elegoo picks CC1/SDCP or CC2/MQTT from the model) — `renderer/inventory.js` (`openDemoPicker`), `renderer/css/40-printers.css`
  - Every demo printer is its MODEL (`services/demo/shapes.js`, read from the brand's printer catalog): AMS / CFS / ACE / IFS → 4 slots, MMU → 5, several heads → one spool each, nothing → the external spool alone; `tools` (Prusa XL 2 / 5 heads, tool changers), `Enclosed` → chamber; a small override table for what the catalogs lack (dual-nozzle H2D / H2D Pro / H2C, Snapmaker U1 four heads, Elegoo Canvas / CC1). The faces report it the way the real machine does — Bambu: no AMS block on an AMS-less model, H2 dual nozzle in `device.extruder.info` (packed temps, active nozzle in `state` bits 4-7), the model's real serial prefix, and the RTSPS camera of the X1 / X2D / H2 / P2S families on :322 (H.264 over interleaved RTP, encoded by the bundled ffmpeg — `services/demo/faces/bambu-rtsp.js`); Prusa `mmu` only on an MMU model + the model name; FlashForge / Anycubic / Creality without their feeder when the model has none; Klipper reports the active tool. Changing a demo printer's model rebuilds its machine — `services/demo/{shapes,index}.js`, `services/demo/faces/{bambulab,bambu-rtsp,prusa,flashforge,anycubic,creality,klipper}.js`, `preload.js`
  - Demo printers on the computer's NETWORK address (`demoLan: true`), for devices that test against the simulator (Tiger Touch, the mobile app): the simulator serves loopback hosts and this computer's own IPv4 addresses (fixed-port brands on their real ports, the others on `ip:port`), the shared port-80 router answers LAN clients for a LAN host, the Creality K2 leaves 7125 to the Snapmaker there, a Bambu Lab printer takes "host:port" for MQTT (several behind one address; the camera only for the one holding 6000), and Studio rewrites their address when the computer's address changes (`repointLanDemos`, IPC `demo:lan-host`) — `services/demo/{index,addresses}.js`, `services/demo/faces/{common,creality,flashforge}.js`, `renderer/printers/demo-badge.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `preload.js`
- **Printer mocks usable from another device** (Tiger Touch, the mobile app): a `/mock/…` scenario API on both mocks — `/mock/set` forces the print state (printing / paused / complete / cancelled / error with a message / idle), progress, file, targets, Klippy state (Moonraker) or serial link (OctoPrint), `/mock/print` starts a fresh print, `/mock/state` dumps everything; camera URLs built from the address the client used (Host header) instead of 127.0.0.1; both listen on every interface and print their LAN addresses at start-up. The Moonraker mock also reports the extra temperatures of a real box (`temperature_sensor mcu_temp` / `raspberry_pi`, `temperature_fan chamber_fan`, `heater_generic dryer` taking `SET_HEATER_TEMPERATURE HEATER=dryer`). Explainer for agents of other repos: `docs/PRINTER-MOCKS.md` — `scripts/moonraker-mock.mjs`, `scripts/octoprint-mock.mjs`, `docs/PRINTER-PLUGINS.md`
- **Klipper: every extra temperature the printer reports** (contributed by @storm8488, PR #93): `temperature_sensor`, `temperature_fan` and `heater_generic` objects are discovered and polled (the chamber is not repeated); they live in a collapsible *Sensors* card under Temperature, which itself stays identical to the other brands; generic heaters there take a new target on click. The printer's Mainsail theme logo (`config/.theme/sidebar-logo.png`) replaces the default Klipper logo on the board and in the table when it exists (not in the HTTPS web version, where an `http://` image would mark the page insecure); optional plugin hook `renderLogo(p)` — `renderer/printers/klipper/{core,cards,plugin,logo,temperature-sensors}.js`, `renderer/printers/klipper/klipper.css`, `renderer/inventory.js`, `docs/PRINTER-PLUGINS.md`, `scripts/test-klipper-display.mjs`
- **Tiger Touch in Studio** (Panda Touch / K-Touch screen firmware, `users/{uid}/tigertouchs/{mac}` per Tiger-Touch `docs/TELEMETRY.md`): header glyph + hover (green active / blue screen asleep / red offline, 90 s / 11 min windows), side card per screen (Wi-Fi bars, "battery fitted", firmware, IP, demo-mode note, followed printers with the SCREEN's own online dot from `printers_online`, hold-to-confirm remove, raw JSON in debug mode with the LAN secrets masked), LAN mode, talked to from the renderer (`renderer/IoT/tigertouch/screen.js`, CORS open on the firmware): **Live view** on an 800×480 canvas — the whole screen once as `rle565` (≈30-55 KB, ~0.4 s), then `/screen.ver?wait=` long-poll and only the changed rectangle (`since=`, `X-Rect`); click = `/api/tap`, drag = `/api/swipe` with `wait=1`, the new view drawn ~1 s after the touch (was ~4 s per 1.1 MB BMP); 403 → re-read the document and retry once, 429 → 60 s, 503 → 1 s, network → backoff; at most two connections. Firmware without rle565 falls back to the BMP path in main.js. Screenshot from the same rle565 frame (~0.6 s) → PNG `tiger-touch-YYYYMMDD-HHMMSS.png`. "Live view is off" note when `lan_live` is false. The access code is never drawn, and a **Screenshot** (main process fetches `/screen.bmp?c=` → PNG) with Save / Copy / Report a problem (report card pre-filled with the picture and the Tiger Touch repo) / Retake; launch card when the account has none ("Available now", a big **Install Tiger Touch** button to the web installer, GitHub tile). A Tiger Touch is worth 100 XP — `renderer/IoT/tigertouch/{index,panel}.js`, `tigertouch.css`, `inventory.html`, `inventory.js`, `main.js` (`tigertouch:screenshot` / `:frame` / `:touch` / `:save-png`), `preload.js`, `assets/svg/icons/icon_tigertouch_3d.svg`, `assets/img/TigerTouch_card.png`
  - Backend (TigerTag_Firebase_Backend): `tigertouchs` owner-only rule (deployed); XP `tigerTouch: 100` counted by `recomputeXp` + create/delete triggers (deployed)
- Tiger Spool / Tiger Touch reports carry each device (name, firmware, status, Wi-Fi) and the printers it follows — name, brand + model, online as seen by the DEVICE and as seen by Studio (the two differ, which is the point) — rendered as a table in the GitHub issue; no address, code or MAC. The Tiger Spool side card gets a **Screenshot** button (its `GET /screen.bmp`, through main) in the same preview as Tiger Touch's (Save / Copy / Report a problem / Retake, now shared: `showScreenshot` in `IoT/tigertouch/panel.js`, sized to a portrait screen too), and a Tiger Spool report a "Capture the Tiger Spool screen" button — `renderer/IoT/tigerspool/{index,panel}.js`, `tigerspool.css`, `renderer/IoT/tigertouch/{index,panel}.js`, `tigertouch.css`, `renderer/report/index.js`, `report.css`, `renderer/inventory.js` (`isPrinterOnline` in the device contexts), `main.js` (`tigerspool:screenshot`), `preload.js`, backend `functions/reportIssue.js`
- Screenshot annotator: a modal card over the dimmed app (title "Point at what matters" + a one-line hint, the toolbar, the picture) instead of a full-screen takeover — `renderer/report/annotator.js`, `report.css`
- Report card: "Send" is never a dead button — on an incomplete form it points at the first thing missing (the empty field, focused and outlined, or a "Tick this box to send" bubble over the consent box); once the form is complete, a non-member gets the TigerSquad presentation — `renderer/report/index.js`, `report.css`
- Report card: sending a request is for TigerSquad members — a non-member's form shows a "Join the TigerSquad to send your request" panel whose button opens the squad presentation; Send looks unavailable until they join, and a click on it opens that presentation too (unlocked live once they join); the backend refuses a non-member (`SQUAD_REQUIRED`, read from `users/{uid}.supportPublicly`) — `renderer/report/index.js`, `report.css`, backend `functions/reportIssue.js`
- Report card: the first step is which part of the ecosystem the request is about — a vertical list with each product's artwork and a one-line hint (Tiger Studio, Tiger Touch, Tiger POD, Tiger Scale, Tiger Spool, TigerTag RFID Connect — the mobile app, whose repo is private: its consent line says the request goes to the team, not public), then the request types (catalogue fixes and new brands only under Tiger Studio), with the picked product as a way back; opened from a device (Tiger Touch screenshot) the product is already picked. A Tiger Touch request gets a **"Capture the Tiger Touch screen"** button (one per reachable screen), a Tiger Scale request a **"Capture the Tiger Scale screen"** one: the picture goes straight into the request and the annotator. The scale's picture is read by main from its live view (`tigerscale:screenshot` — `GET :81/stream?c=CODE`, one full frame of RLE565 column bands, then hang up); the scale is read at its published `lan_url` with its published `lan_access_code` (case-sensitive), only when `lan_live` is true — never asked of the user; a scale asleep (`screen_off`) gets one wake tap in the middle and 1.5 s before the read (~2 s in all on the bench) — `renderer/report/index.js`, `report.css`, `renderer/IoT/tigertouch/panel.js` (`tigerTouchScreensReady`, `captureTigerTouch`), `renderer/IoT/tigerscale/index.js` (`scalesReady`), `main.js`, `preload.js` The backend files the GitHub issue on that product's repo — Studio (Tiger POD too, tagged `[Tiger POD]` in the title), Tiger-Touch, Tiger-Scale-V3, TigerSpool-RFID, TigerTag_RFID_Connect — names the product in the issue body, and follows its status / comments there (`reportIssue` / `myReports` route by `target` → `githubRepo`; the `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` needs Issues read/write on those five repos) — `renderer/report/index.js`, `report.css`, backend `functions/reportIssue.js`
- TigerScale card: **Live view** and **Screenshot** of the scale's own screen when it publishes its LAN live view (firmware after 3.9.0). Live view reads `{lan_url}/stream` straight from the renderer (CORS open): the whole 480×320 screen, then only the changed RLE565 column bands, drawn once per FRAME_END; a click is a tap (`POST /tap`); a sleeping screen is woken first; the stream is aborted the moment the view closes (the scale serves two viewers), reconnects with backoff otherwise. Screenshot uses the shared preview (Save / Copy / Report a problem → a Tiger Scale request / Retake) — `renderer/IoT/tigerscale/{index,live}.js`, `tigerscale.css`
- TigerScale card: the raw-JSON debug block masks the live view's `lan_access_code` / `lan_url`; a "Live view is off on the scale" chip when `lan_live` is false — `renderer/IoT/tigerscale/index.js`

### Changed
- README and llms.txt refreshed for search and AI answers: about 380 printer models named with real examples, Tiger Touch (Panda Touch / K-Touch firmware, available on GitHub) in the ecosystem tables and its own section, a FAQ in plain questions and answers; llms.txt also documents the demo-mode architecture, the Klipper Sensors card and the plugins' host services — `README.md`, `llms.txt`
- Klipper and OctoPrint printers: click a temperature (nozzle, bed, heated chamber) to set its target, like the other brands; the panel no longer closes the editor on the next poll. Shared inline editor for plugins (OctoPrint's main side now allows the chamber target path) — `renderer/printers/temp-edit.js`, `renderer/printers/octoprint/main.cjs`, `renderer/printers/klipper/{cards,core,plugin}.js`, `renderer/printers/octoprint/{cards,core,plugin}.js`; the mocks accept the commands (`SET_HEATER_TEMPERATURE`; `/api/printer/tool`, `/api/printer/bed`) — `scripts/moonraker-mock.mjs`, `scripts/octoprint-mock.mjs`, `docs/PRINTER-MOCKS.md`; demo-mode engine takes setpoint changes from every face — `docs/DEMO-MODE.md`

### Fixed
- Klipper and OctoPrint cameras now fit their frame whole instead of overflowing it at their native resolution — `renderer/printers/klipper/klipper.css`, `renderer/printers/octoprint/octoprint.css`
- FlashForge: a printer opened before its first status answer (e.g. right after adding it) showed no camera until its card was reopened — the picture is now handed to the stream as soon as the camera address arrives — `renderer/inventory.js`
- MCP: printer / device access codes written with an underscore (`access_code`, `check_code`, `api_key`, `lan_access_code`) and the Tiger Touch `lan_url` are now masked like the other credentials — `renderer/inventory.js` (`MCP_SECRET_FIELD`)
- Internal: CODEMAP-main section ranges realigned with `main.js`; bundled reference data refreshed (`id_brand`, `id_material`, `id_catalog`).

## v2.41.0 — 2026-10-15

### Added
- **Printer plugin architecture** — a printer integration can now be a self-contained folder `renderer/printers/<id>/` that imports host services only (never another brand's files), in the spirit of a Home Assistant integration. Host: `renderer/printers/plugins.js` (`registerPrinterPlugin`, `printerPlugin`, `printerPlugins`, `printerPluginIds`, `mergePluginStrings` — a plugin ships its own strings, merged into the locales without overriding app keys); `main.js` loads every `renderer/printers/*/main.cjs` at start-up (`register({ ipcMain, channel })`, handlers namespaced `printer:<id>:<name>`, a failing plugin is logged and skipped); `preload.js` exposes one generic bridge `window.printerPlugin.invoke(id, name, …args)` that forwards only that channel shape; `identity.js` gains `registerPrinterIdentity` (ARP-MAC re-find for plugins). `inventory.js` dispatches generically at every brand touchpoint: brand list (`PRINTER_BRANDS = […, …printerPluginIds()]`), connect / disconnect / reconnect / ping / online, the job normaliser (`_getPrinterJob` takes the plugin's `job(p)`), live block + debug log (host ids `<id>Live` / `<id>Log`), camera banners, add flow, model catalogue (plugin-relative images resolved to `imageUrl`), brand logos, `stopAll` on account switch, MCP brand list. A plugin can set `noFilament: true` (no storage widget on the board, `widgetOn`). Contract and how-to: `docs/PRINTER-PLUGINS.md`. The seven historical brands stay hand-wired for now.
- **Klipper / Moonraker printers (beta)** — first plugin (`renderer/printers/klipper/`): any printer running Klipper + Moonraker (Voron, RatRig, Vzbot, Sovol SV07/SV08/Zero, Qidi, Elegoo Neptune 4, Creality K1/K2/Ender-3 V3, FLSun, Artillery X4, Kingroon…). Main side (`main.cjs`): allow-listed Moonraker HTTP (`printer:klipper:http`, `X-Api-Key` when set) + `/server/info` probe. Core (`core.js`): HTTP polling every 2 s (`POST /printer/objects/query`, as Home Assistant does) after a one-off feature detection (`/printer/objects/list`: extruders, Happy Hare `mmu`, AFC lanes, chamber sensor, lights, filament sensors; `/server/webcams/list`; Spoolman component); Klippy-not-ready state shown with its message; reconnect with backoff + re-find. Cards: job (largest G-code thumbnail via `/server/files/metadata`, layers, remaining = slicer estimate − elapsed or progress extrapolation, hold-to-confirm pause / resume / cancel), control (XY/Z jog with step, home X / Y / XY / Z, part fan and speed factor ±10 %, one toggle per light — `led` / `neopixel` / light-named `output_pin` via `SET_LED` / `SET_PIN`; moves locked while printing; patched in place on each poll, rebuilt only on a structural change), temperatures (per extruder, bed, chamber), filament slots from Happy Hare gates / AFC lanes / plain extruders. Slot writes (`MMU_GATE_MAP GATE= MATERIAL= COLOR= SPOOLID=`, AFC `SET_COLOR` / `SET_MATERIAL` / `SET_SPOOL_ID`) are implemented in the core (`klipperWriteSlot`), UI to come. Camera: MJPEG stream into an `<img>` or 2 s snapshot refresh, Moonraker's flips/rotation applied. LAN scan of `/server/info` on 7125 + manual add (IP or hostname, optional `:port`, optional API key). Catalogue of 161 machines with pictures — every model OrcaSlicer's profiles declare `gcode_flavor: klipper`, minus the closed-firmware brands Studio reaches through their own connectors (Anycubic, FlashForge, Snapmaker). Reference: `renderer/printers/klipper/PROTOCOL.md`.
- **Marlin printers through OctoPrint (beta)** — second plugin (`renderer/printers/octoprint/`): Marlin 2 (and any serial firmware) behind OctoPrint / OctoPi. Main side: allow-listed OctoPrint REST + probe (`/plugin/appkeys/probe` 204, else `/api/version`; a bare 401/403 is not taken as proof). **Application Keys** in the add flow: Studio requests a key and the user clicks *Allow* in OctoPrint (`POST /plugin/appkeys/request`, polled until 200 / 404), "Paste a key instead" as fallback. Core: one-off detection (`/api/version`, printer profile extruder count / heated bed / chamber, `/api/settings` webcam), 2 s poll of `/api/connection` + `/api/printer` (409 = serial link down → "OctoPrint is up, the printer isn't plugged into it" with a *Connect the printer* button, `POST /api/connection`) + `/api/job`; state mapping from the flags (complete = job at 100 %). Cards: job (thumbnail from the PrusaSlicer / Cura thumbnail plugins, remaining time, hold-to-confirm pause / resume / cancel), control (jog / home via `/api/printer/printhead`, fan and speed via `M106` / `M107` / `M220`, showing the last value sent — OctoPrint reports neither), temperatures; no filament card / slots / storage widget (OctoPrint keeps no filament identity — `noFilament`). MJPEG / snapshot camera with flipH / flipV / rotate90. Catalogue of 187 Marlin machines from OrcaSlicer's profiles (`marlin` / `marlin2`, Bambu Lab excluded). Reference: `renderer/printers/octoprint/PROTOCOL.md`.
- **"Find your printer" in the Add-a-printer card** — a search across every connector's catalogue at once (hand-wired brands + plugins), one row per machine (bare catalogue names prefixed with the brand label, notes in parentheses ignored, normalised key merges "K1" and "Creality K1") with its picture (60 px) and the connectors that reach it, a beta connector's Beta tag beside it. One connector → straight into its add flow; several (Creality K1: Creality or Klipper) → the choice opens in place. The model and its full name ride along (`_pendingAddModel`) and are preselected in the settings form whatever path the flow takes. Each typed word must start a word of the name ("k1" → K1 / K1C, not LK10); Enter takes the first match, Escape clears; the index rebuilds when the catalogues finish loading (keyed on the model count). `renderer/printers/model-finder.js` (no DOM), `inventory.js`, `inventory.html`.
- **Bambu Lab: Cloud ⇄ LAN switch** — a *Cloud | LAN* sliding switch beside *Connection tutorial* on the settings form of any printer added through the Bambu account (`cloud_<devId>`); LAN reuses the serial, the access code and the address learned from telemetry (prefilled, editable) and writes `mode: "lan"`, Cloud writes `mode: "cloud"`; the printer reconnects over the other broker without a reload (`_reconnectAfterModeSwitch`). `bambulab/mode-switch.js`, registry hook `connModeSwitch`.
  - **Automatic failover** (`bambulab/failover.js`): when the current way stays down 45 s, the other is probed quietly — LAN through a throw-away local MQTT session (`probe:<key>`), Cloud through the account's device list (`cloud-bind`, `online`) — and the printer is moved over only if it answers; never on a printer disconnected by hand, one probe a minute, 3 min cooldown after a switch; a flash message says what happened (`bblAutoSwitchLan` / `bblAutoSwitchCloud`). Saved through `ctx.saveBambuMode`.
  - **Cloud record kept in step with the account**: on every new cloud connection, then every 5 min (and during failover probes), the name, access code and model the account reports are written back in one update (`ctx.savePrinterFields`); a new access code — the printer issues one when its LAN mode is toggled — also restarts an open camera with it.
  - A printer the account no longer lists (switching it to LAN-only mode on its screen unbinds it) gets a banner in its live block and a flash telling the user to pair it again in Bambu Handy (`bblCloudUnbound`).
- **USB scale (DYMO M-series)** —
  - *Weigh again* key in the spool's weight section once a weighing is saved and the spool is still on the scale: re-arms the session without lifting the spool (issue #76).
  - The scale on the account: `telemetry/studio.usbScaleModel`, `usbScaleMaker`, `usbScaleProductId` (`"0x8009"`, the reliable model key — the M-series has no serial number), `usbScaleFirmware` (`"1.00"`), `firstUsbScaleAt` (once), `lastUsbScaleAt` (each connect and each weighing it applies, at most every 10 min, and on sign-in when already plugged). The server turns `firstUsbScaleAt` into the *USB scale* discovery — **100 XP**, kept for good (backend `ACH_XP.usbScale`, `DISCOVERIES`, deployed); the achievements list shows it. `main.js` sends the scale's maker / product id / firmware with `usb-scale-update` and `usb-scale:state`.
- **Printer catalogues** — Elegoo **Centauri 2** and **Centauri 2 Combo** (a Centauri Carbon 2 without the front door and lid, same MQTT protocol) and the **ELEGOO × emoji® Centauri Carbon 2 Combo** (a themed CC2, reported as a CC2 by discovery, picked by hand), with the manufacturer's pictures (the Combo and emoji® from Elegoo's product pages, background cut out); discovery tells them apart from the CC2 by name ("Carbon" → CC2, "Centauri 2" → Centauri 2, "… Combo" → Combo — `ELEGOO_MODEL_ID`, `elegooModelIdFromMachineModel`). Prusa catalogue (MK4S, MK4, MK3.9/S, Core One, XL, XL 2/5 toolheads, MINI/MINI+, MK3S+, MK2.5S) and Elegoo Centauri Carbon now show the machine instead of the placeholder. The full manufacturer picture set (442 pictures, 70 vendors) is archived as masters in `assets-src/img/printer_library/`.
- **Certified TigerTag+ counted apart in the chip-mix telemetry** — a TigerTag+ scanned in whose factory signature verifies (`tag.verify()` in main, the backend's own certification test, exposed as `_certified` on the scan payload) bumps `certifiedAddedTotal` instead of `plusAddedTotal`.
- Test benches without hardware: `scripts/moonraker-mock.mjs` (`npm run moonraker:mock -- --profile plain|happyhare|afc [--api-key K] [--auto-print]`: objects / query, print control, G-code macros incl. `MMU_GATE_MAP` and AFC `SET_*`, `G28` / `G1` with homing and bounds, `M106` / `M107` / `M220`, `SET_PIN` / `SET_LED` lights, MJPEG camera, thumbnails) and `scripts/octoprint-mock.mjs` (`npm run octoprint:mock -- [--port] [--api-key] [--tools] [--auto-print] [--closed] [--approve-after s]`: Application Keys, connection / printer / job, jog / home with homing checks, `M106` / `M220`, thumbnails, MJPEG camera — on macOS use a port other than 5000, AirPlay holds it).

### Changed
- **Beta printers** (Klipper, OctoPrint, Prusa, Elegoo CC1): the beta banner at the top of the live block becomes the Beta tag + an ⓘ whose hover bubble explains it, and the brand picker shows the same pair — shared `renderer/printers/beta-badge.js` (`betaBadgeHtml`, `betaBadgeInlineHtml`); `printerBetaNote` reworded as the bubble text.
- **Printer model picker** (every brand's settings form): a search field at the top once the list holds more than 8 models — every typed word must appear, any order, accents ignored; Enter picks the first match, Escape closes; an empty result points to the generic model; the list scrolls without a visible bar. `modal-helpers.js`.
- The Add-a-printer card's lists scroll without a visible bar; its result cards size to their content with top / bottom padding.
- Flash messages stay up long enough to read (~55 ms a character, 1.8–7 s) instead of a fixed 1.8 s (`_flashMessage`).
- Bundled reference data refreshed: catalogue 14,642 → **14,682** products.

### Fixed
- **USB scale lost after its auto-sleep until unplugged / replugged** (issues #74, #76): on some machines a DYMO M-series powers off without leaving the USB bus, so the open HID handle stayed silent with no error and the woken scale was never reopened. A scale is now announced connected only on its first real frame, and 5 s of silence closes the handle and resumes polling (`SCALE_SILENCE_MS`, watchdog in `main.js`).
- **Elegoo Centauri Carbon 1 camera never showed** ("camera connection failed"): the printer answers Cmd 386 with a scheme-less `VideoUrl` (`<ip>:3031/video`, seen on V1.4.49), which an `<img>` resolved as an app-relative path; `sdcpVideoUrl()` adds `http://`, swaps an empty / 0.0.0.0 / loopback host for the address the printer was reached on, refuses non-http(s), and keeps the default URL when nothing usable is left — PR #82 (Ptitlouis6012).
- The Elegoo camera widget showed FlashForge's "only one viewer is allowed at a time" on any load error (a CC1 takes 4 streams) and FlashForge's alt text; it now has its own `elgCamFailMsg` / `elgCameraAlt` — PR #83 (Ptitlouis6012).
- **Bambu Lab camera** (JPEG A1 / P1 and RTSP X1 / H2) could run two reconnect loops for one printer — a retry waiting on its timer survived a stop or a new start, and the two kept kicking each other off a printer that serves one camera client; a per-key generation (`_bambuCamGen`) now ends a stale loop.
- **Printer error messages never showed**: the printers context called `toast(msg, type)` against a `toast(el, kind, msg)` signature, so every brand's error toast (13 call sites) was silently dropped; it now goes through the app's flash message.
- **Add-printer flow left open on top of other side cards**: with the brand picker or a brand's choice / scan / manual card open, opening a printer, a spool, a group, the debug panel, the Firestore explorer, Friends or notifications put the new card underneath; the flow now closes first (`_closeAddPrinterFlow`, also in `_closeAllSidePanels`).
- **Printer side card header cut in half on scroll**: the off-screen Snapmaker file sheet made the `overflow: hidden` panel scrollable by script / anchoring; detail panels now use `overflow: clip`.
- **TigerData+ creation counter never reached Firestore**: `cloudPlusAddedTotal` was missing from the telemetry rules' field whitelist, so every such write was refused silently; whitelisted with the new `certifiedAddedTotal` and the USB-scale fields (backend `firestore.rules`, deployed).

### Backend & Hub (shipped alongside)
- Backend: `PRINTER_BRANDS` adds `klipper` and `octoprint` (printer XP achievement, per-brand community stats); `publicStats/community.printers { total, byBrand }`; telemetry rules whitelist; the USB-scale XP discovery. Deployed (rules, indexes, functions).
- Hub: TigerSquad page shows the printers connected, live and by brand; admin separates TigerTag · TigerTag+ · TigerTag+ Certified · TigerData · TigerData+ (protocol split and chip mix) and counts USB-scale users (last 30 days, models, per-user last use).

## v2.40.1 — 2026-10-14

### Added
- **Tiger Studio Web hotfixes** — web-only fixes ship without a desktop build while the version number stays the desktop's: `npm run web:hotfix` (`scripts/web-hotfix.mjs`) tags the current commit `web-vX.Y.Z.N` after checking it is on `origin/main` and that `package.json` there is the latest published release; `web-sync.yml` also runs on `web-v*` tags (refuses a base that is not the latest release, triggers the Hub deploy hook, waits for `version.json` = `{ version, webBuild }`); `scripts/build-web.mjs --web-build N` stamps the build number into `version.json` and `web-bridge.js` (About shows "X.Y.Z · web N", the reload check compares both); the Hub builds the highest `web-v<latest>.*` tag (found with `git ls-remote`) and fails on any version / build mismatch. `v*` tags still drive the desktop build. Docs: `docs/WEB_HOTFIX.md`.

### Changed
- **Studio Web at the root of `studio.tigersystem.io`** — the subdomain serves the app itself (Hub middleware rewrite, no redirect) and `tigersystem.io/studio` redirects there; the installed app's manifest is scoped to `/` (rewritten by the Hub after the build). Own origin, so its own sign-in (no session shared with the Hub any more; `_adoptSharedWebSession` stays but no longer fires).
- **Studio Web signs in through its own domain** — on `studio.tigersystem.io` Firebase Auth's `authDomain` is the page's host (`AUTH_PROXY_HOSTS` in `renderer/firebase.js`); the Hub proxies `/__/auth/*` to `tigertag-connect.firebaseapp.com` (exempted from `X-Frame-Options`), the Google OAuth web client gained the origin and `/__/auth/handler` redirect, and `studio.tigersystem.io` is a Firebase authorised domain. Google's account picker names tigersystem.io instead of `tigertag-connect.firebaseapp.com`, and sign-in stays first-party for Safari / iOS / installed apps. Desktop and other hosts unchanged.
- **Studio Web on iPad / touch** — with no saved choice the sidebar follows the screen (slim when taller than wide or under 900 px, full in landscape, follows a rotation); on `hover: none` screens the hover-only actions are shown (weight edit, list remove, rack and friend menus, reorder link, min-stock edit) and mouse-only helpers hidden (panel resize handles, debug ⌥-click hint); the KPI row fades its right edge under 1100 px; the NFC chip banners (encode / update / missing twin) are hidden on the web; the installed app keeps its top edge below the iOS status bar (`apple-mobile-web-app-status-bar-style: black`).
- Bundled reference data refreshed: brand **Filament 3D** (id 31781) — 138 brands; catalogue 14,463 → **14,642** products.

### Fixed
- **XP history showed some gains twice** ("+20 · +20" for one printer; also chips, TigerSquad, friends) while the total counted them once — a server-side race: two recomputations of one account (`storeTotals`, e.g. a printer or friend write touching two docs) both read the previous `xpBreakdown` before either wrote, so each logged the move. The backend (TigerTag_Firebase_Backend `functions/supportWebhooks.js`) now reads the split and writes history + daily `xp_gain` notification + totals in ONE Firestore transaction (12 functions redeployed 2026-10-14); the 259 duplicate `users/{uid}/xpHistory` entries on 42 accounts were deleted. No XP total was wrong. No Studio code change.
- **Studio Web was flagged "Not secure" and asked for local-network access** — the Creality / Snapmaker modules ping and connect to printers from the page (`ws://<ip>:9999`, `http://<ip>:7125`) as soon as the printer list loads: mixed content on an https page. `web-bridge.js` now refuses any insecure `WebSocket` / `fetch` on https (callers already treat it as "printer unreachable").
- Studio Web no longer requests the Agora SDK (Anycubic cloud camera — desktop-only, copied from `node_modules`, not in git), which answered 404.
- **Hold-to-confirm bubble** no longer stays on screen after the action closes its menu or rebuilds its card (e.g. deleting a printer): hidden before `onConfirm`, and on the next `mouseover` when its anchor is detached — PR #77 (Ptitlouis6012).
- **Printers board: cards piled up at the origin after a long drag** — a rebuild forced under a gesture longer than the 2 s watchdog wired a fresh gesture that never saw the release, so the objects stayed held for ever and the layout left the rebuilt cards without `left`/`top`. The gesture and its release (`_planG` / `_planEnd`) are module-level and rebind to a rebuilt board, held objects keep their drawn position (`_planHeldPos`), the watchdog no longer rebuilds under a moving hand, and a hold with no gesture behind it is cleared (`_planHealHeld`) — PR #78 (Ptitlouis6012).
- **Printers board: a dragged group could be pulled away for ever** (one card reached x ≈ 11 000, off-screen after a reload) — a carried group now stops one screenful (at the current zoom) past the furthest object it is not carrying; the limit beats a snap guide — PR #79 (Ptitlouis6012).

## v2.40.0 — 2026-10-12

### Added
- **Tiger Studio Web** — the renderer runs in a browser at `https://tigersystem.io/studio` (`studio.tigersystem.io` redirects there), from the same code as the desktop app. No bundler, no fork: `scripts/build-web.mjs` (`npm run web:build` / `web:serve`, `--out`, `--base`) copies `renderer/`, `data/` and a filtered `assets/` into a static folder whose `index.html` is `renderer/inventory.html` with an injected `<base href="<base>renderer/">`, so every relative URL resolves as on desktop. The Hub (TigerTag_Hub, Next.js on Vercel) builds the published latest Studio release into `public/studio/` at `prebuild` and serves it on its own origin. Docs: `docs/WEB_DEPLOYMENT.md`, `docs/STUDIO-WEB-PLAN.md`.
  - `renderer/web-bridge.js` — a browser `window.electronAPI` (`isWeb: true`) implementing what a browser can: `openExternal` (`window.open`), `getAppInfo` (`platform: "web"`), `lookupProduct` / `refreshApiData` / `fetchCatalogAll` (direct calls to `api.tigertag.io`, which answers CORS; one retry on the catalogue, whose upstream hiccups come back without CORS headers), `downloadImage` (anchor download). Inert in Electron (preload already defined `electronAPI`).
  - `HAS_NATIVE` (`!!electronAPI && !electronAPI.isWeb`) replaces every "electronAPI exists" desktop check (RFID encode/burn/update modals, chip banners, the toolbox chip block, the RFID listener block). Never test `!!window.electronAPI` for "desktop" any more.
  - `data-desktop-only` + `html.is-web` rule hide NFC readers bar and tester, TD1S, the updater (card, icon, banner), the printer views (`setViewMode` falls back to `grid` on web) and camera detach; `data-web-only` is the mirror rule.
  - **One sign-in with the Hub** — Studio and the Hub share the `tigersystem.io` origin and the same Firebase API key, so the DEFAULT app holds the Hub's session: with no Studio account yet, `_adoptSharedWebSession` copies that user (verified address or Google) into the account's named app. On the web the sign-in flows no longer sign the DEFAULT app out, and `fbSignOut` ends it only when it is the same account.
  - Friend link on the web: `?friend=XXX-YYY` pre-fills Add-friend (then dropped from the address), like `tigertag://friend/…` on desktop.
- **Installable as an app (PWA)** — the web build writes `manifest.webmanifest` (scope and `start_url` = the served folder without trailing slash, standalone, `assets/favicon/icon-192/512.png`) and the iOS home-screen tags. `web-bridge.js` keeps Chrome's `beforeinstallprompt`; the sidebar **Install the app** button (`#sbInstallBtn`, `data-web-only`, hidden until installable or on iOS) replays it, or on iPhone/iPad unfolds the Share → Add to Home Screen hint; hidden once installed (`display-mode: standalone`). Deliberately no service worker.
- **No version drift between desktop and web** — both follow the published "latest" GitHub Release. `.github/workflows/web-sync.yml` (on `release: published`) calls the Hub's `studio-release` Vercel deploy hook (`HUB_DEPLOY_HOOK_URL` secret) and waits until `tigersystem.io/studio/version.json` equals the release, failing the run otherwise. The Hub's `fetch-studio-web.mjs` resolves the latest release from github.com's `/releases/latest` redirect, asserts the built version equals the tag and fails the build on any mismatch (the live site keeps its version). An open web page or installed app compares `version.json` with its own version when it comes back into view (≤ every 10 min) and reloads onto the new release, unless a window is open or a field focused.

### Fixed
- **Low-stock notifications** stored `cdnImg(resolvedImg(url))` — the desktop's local `/img-cache/<md5>` path — in `users/{uid}/notifications`, so the photo was a 404 on the account's other devices and on the web. They now store the public CDN URL; the web skips the old local paths.
- FlashForge / Snapmaker / Creality background pings (`setInterval` 30 s at module load) threw `ctx.getState().printers is not iterable` while signed out.

### i18n
- Added: `sbInstallBtn`, `installIosHint` — 11 locales.

## v2.39.0 — 2026-10-12

### Added
- **Hover preview of table thumbnails** (issue #67) — in the inventory, catalogue and list tables, resting the mouse on a spool's / product's thumbnail shows a 220 px preview beside it: framed in the spool colour (`--spool-bg`), the sharper CDN rendition (`data-zoom` = `cdnImg(…, 220)` on `thumbHTML` / `_catThumbHTML`), one body-level `.thumb-zoom` element (never clipped by the table), shown after 220 ms so sweeping down the column doesn't flash, hidden on scroll / mousedown, placed left when there is no room on the right (`_showThumbZoom`).
- **TigerSquad badge and XP in reports** — the GitHub issue's *Environment* table carries the reporter's level and XP ("1,282 XP · 19 TigerTag+ Certified · 45 TigerTag chips"), read by `reportIssue` from `userProfiles/{uid}.tigerSquad.tier` and `users/{uid}.xpPoints` / `certifiedPlusCount` / `tigerTagCount` — never taken from the app; the report's "Automatic information" panel shows them before sending.

### Changed
- **Photos fade while a results list scrolls** — instead of vanishing and popping back, photos fade out (0.35 s) during the scroll hold and back in (0.8 s) one second after it, while the TigerTag logo on the colour square crossfades the other way. The fade exists only around the hold (`body.photos-held` → `body.photos-fade-in`, set for the length of the fade), never on a normal load, so grid rebuilds still don't blink (`_hueHoldSync`).
- **TigerSquad rankings** — Countries League and Makers ranks read "#1", "#2"… (wider, tabular rank column).
- **"TigerTag+ Certified"** — the product name (untranslated) replaces "certified TigerTag+" in the TigerSquad texts in every locale.
- Bundled reference data: brand **IEMAI** (id 50266) added — 137 brands; catalogue unchanged (14,463 products).

### Fixed
- **High CPU at idle** (issue #66) — looping CSS animations kept the renderer re-compositing the whole window at 60 fps. Measured on a Mac, idle on the inventory **51 % → 5.6 %** renderer CPU, printers view **49.5 % → 20 %** (Linux with software compositing was hit harder). Closed windows and the account menu stay in the page at opacity 0, so their pulses, spinners, rings and light sweep never stopped: everything inside a closed `.modal-overlay` or `.acct-dropdown` is now `animation-play-state: paused` (resumes on open); the TigerSpool / TigerScale "live" ping and the printers' "online" dot pulse 3 times when they come online, then stay lit.
- **Avatar photos that fail to load** now fall back to the initials: the inline `onerror` removed the image before switching its parent to initials mode, so it found no parent and the avatar stayed blank (rankings, friends' lists…). Photos are also requested with `referrerpolicy="no-referrer"`.

### Removed
- **FlashForge "Adventurer A5"** (issue #29) — no such model exists in FlashForge's range (Adventurer 5M / 5M Pro / AD5X); the entry was a placeholder with the 5M Pro picture. Removed from `data/printers/ffg_printer_models.json`, the connection tutorial's model picker, the network-scan detection (`probe.js`) and its two images. A printer saved with a model no longer in the catalogue now shows "—" instead of a bare catalogue number (`printerModelName`).

### i18n
- Added: `reportInfoBadge`, `reportInfoXp`; changed: `squadXpLine`, `squadLevelUpText_platinum`, `xpNextPlatinumHow`, `xpChipsHint`, `squadRule_silver`, `squadRule_platinum`, `squadRuleXp` — 11 locales.

## v2.38.0 — 2026-10-11

### Added
- **TigerSquad rankings window** (`_openSquadBoard`) with four tabs, opening at its final size (skeleton rows on the first load, last load reused at once and refreshed underneath, no visible scrollbar):
  - **Countries League** — members' published XP (`userProfiles/{uid}.tigerSquad.xp`) summed per `tigerSquad.country`, member count, own country highlighted ("France is #1 of 3 countries").
  - **Makers** — every member by XP: avatar with level ring + badge, flag, level, own row highlighted.
  - **Achievements** — total, a next-level explainer (`_squadNextLevelHTML`: current → next badge, progress bar for counted rules — 20 TigerTag chips for Silver, 50 certified TigerTag+ for Platinum — and the join / coffee action; "Nothing above you but the sky" at Platinum), then every achievement earned (XP + count) or locked (worth + how), discoveries marked "yours for good" (`_renderMyXp`, `XP_ACHIEVEMENTS`).
  - **History** — gains / losses with date and running total, from `users/{uid}/xpHistory`.
  - Entry points: account menu ("TigerSquad Ranking" for a member, "Join the TigerSquad" otherwise — both an orange-tinted pill with a bold, light-swept label), a rank chip next to the sidebar XP chip ("#2"), the support menu's member row, the requests card, the XP line in My profile.
- **World rank** — "#3 worldwide of 9" (1 + members with more `tigerSquad.xp`, Firestore REST `runAggregationQuery` COUNT, cached 10 min — `_squadRank`) in My profile, the support menu and the requests card.
- **XP achievements (server, `achievementParts` / `storeTotals`)** — state-based (lost again when removed): friend 10 each, social link 5 each, profile photo 10, public profile 5, connected printer 20 each, owning a TigerScale 200 / a TigerSpool 75; one-time discoveries kept in `users/{uid}.xpUnlocked`: phone app 100, TigerPOD (two readers) 50, first RFID reader 20, first TD1S 30, first rack 10, first list 5, and **+5 per sidebar community link opened** (wiki, Discord, GitHub, MakerWorld, shop, support — read from `telemetry/studio.<k>ClickedAt` or `users/{uid}.<k>Seen`, so earlier clicks count). Current split in `users/{uid}.xpBreakdown`; every change logged in `users/{uid}/xpHistory` (server-only, owner-readable); triggers `xpFriends`, `xpScales`, `xpTigerSpools`, `xpRacks`, `xpLists`, `xpTelemetry`, `xpTd1s`, `xpPrinters`, `xpProfile`.
- **XP and level-up notifications** — server-written: `storeTotals` merges the day's gains into `users/{uid}/notifications/xp-<YYYY-MM-DD>` (`type: xp_gain`, `data.gained` increment, `data.parts` arrayUnion; unread again and moved to the top; skipped on an account's first computation; losses stay in the history); `publishSupporter` adds `type: squad_level` (`data.tier`, `data.from`) when the tier rises (not on joining). Rendered in the notification centre (orange star / the new badge art), click → rankings History / Achievements.
- **TigerSquad sound and celebration** (Web Audio, no asset files) — a three-note sparkle when the invite appears (`_soundSquadInvite`); joining plays a "legendary" fanfare (falling-sine impact, sawtooth C–E–G–C arpeggio, held chord through a `DynamicsCompressor`, high sparkles — `_soundSquadLegendary`) over a ~3 s celebration (`_squadCelebrate`: badge rising out of a light burst with spinning rays, 28 sparks, "+50 XP", welcome line, level name; static under reduced motion); glassy hover ticks on the perk cards, coffee card and level badges (pitch climbing Bronze → Platinum — `_soundHoverTick`); hovering "Join" plays a rising shimmer (`_soundJoinHover`) and runs a light round the button's edge (conic sweep on `@property --tsq-join-ang`, masked to a 3 px ring).
- **Level-up window** — climbing a level (Bronze → Silver → Gold → Platinum) opens a window with the celebration and fanfare, old badge → new badge and a line per level, "See the rankings" / "Let's go"; live when it happens, otherwise at the next launch: the last seen level is kept per account in `localStorage` `tigertag.squadSeenTier.<uid>` and compared on the first server-confirmed profile snapshot (`_checkSquadLevelUp`, `_openSquadLevelUp`); never on a downgrade.
- **TigerSquad flag / Countries League pick** — My profile → TigerSquad: searchable country picker (flag + name in the app language, detected country by default) writing `users/{uid}.squadCountry` (ISO alpha-2, cleared = detected); the server publishes `userProfiles/{uid}.tigerSquad.country` (pick → telemetry country → mobile locale → language) — `_fillSquadCountry`, `_setSquadCountry`, `renderer/tz-country.js` (`COUNTRY_CODES`).
- **TD1S recorded on the account** — `users/{uid}/td1s/{USB serial}` = { manufacturer, vendorId, productId, firstSeenAt (once), lastSeenAt }; the main process reads the USB identity in `td1sFind` and sends it with "Sensor connected" (`td1s-device` → `td1s.onDevice`).

### Changed
- TigerSquad members wear their **level badge** (Bronze → Platinum art) on the avatar corner instead of the heart — sidebar, banners, friends' lists (`avatarMarkup`, `renderSupporterBadge`); the sidebar's own avatar shows its level ring (the inline account-colour glow from `paintAvatar` is skipped for `.av-squad`); Platinum ring in its crystals' blue (#6CB8FF → #2F6FE0), softer Silver grey.
- Friends list in the sidebar: inner room (8 px padding cancelled by a negative margin) so members' rings and badges are not clipped by `overflow-x: hidden`, badge 22 px like the own avatar, and 10 px between friends (was 3 px) so a ring or badge no longer overlaps the friend below.
- TigerSquad invite timing (`_maybeInviteSquad`): accounts older than 3 days (was 7), 10 s after start-up or after switching account (once per account per session), shown anywhere (even over another window or in a friend's stock); "Maybe later" / ✕ / Esc snooze it to the next local midnight (was 30 days).
- TigerSquad windows wear a white breathing halo so they stand out from the app.
- Printers empty screens (no printer yet; a friend's printers) show a six-brand printer line-up (Anycubic Kobra S1, Elegoo CC2, Creality K2 Plus, Bambu Lab X1C, FlashForge AD5X, Snapmaker U1) rising in one after another instead of a lone icon (`_printersLineupHTML`); the first bullet now lists all seven supported brands (Anycubic and Prusa were missing).

### Fixed
- Sidebar fold / unfold no longer moves anything: the name + XP block does not wrap while it grows from 0 px (was 44 → 58 px, pushing the avatar and friends down), the profile row keeps one height, the rail padding is 12.5 px in both states and the action buttons stay left-anchored while narrowing.
- The level-up check now listens with `includeMetadataChanges`, so a profile identical to the cached copy still delivers the server-confirmed snapshot it waits for.

### i18n
- Added: `squadCountryLabel`, `squadRankLine`, `squadBoardMakers`, `squadBoardYourCountry`, `squadBoardEmpty`, `squadBoardOpen`, `acctSquadRanking`, `squadLevelUpKicker`, `squadLevelUpTitle`, `squadLevelUpText_{bronze,silver,gold,platinum}`, `squadLevelUpOk`, `notifXpTitle`, `xpTotalLine`, `xpPerItem`, `xpKeptForGood`, `xpAchievements`, `xpHistory`, `xpHistoryEmpty`, `xpNextTitle`, `xpNextCount`, `xpNextJoin`, `xpNextSilverHow`, `xpNextOrGold`, `xpNextGoldHow`, `xpNextPlatinumHow`, `xpNextTop`, `xpPart_chips`, `xpPart_squad`, `xpChipsHint`, `xpAch_*` / `xpAchHint_*` (14 achievements); changed `printersEmptyBullet1` — 11 locales.

## v2.37.0 — 2026-10-11

### Added
- **Refill pictogram** — a spool flagged as a refill (`info1`, the same flag as the "Refill" chip) wears a green "Refill" pill (recycle icon + the word; a plain round icon on small thumbnails) with the twin / backup badges: on grid cards, list and grouped-spool thumbnails, and in the detail panel's top-right group (`_cardTlBadges`, `buildPanelHTML`, card signature); the catalogue product card shows it too once the product detail has arrived (`_productHeroParts`) — the catalogue list itself waits for `info1` in the export (TigerTag-Manager#35) — `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css`
- **A reply on one of My requests raises a notification** ("Request #55 got a reply" → opens My requests; the bell counts it) — merged from PR #58 (`renderer/report/replies.js`, `renderer/report/index.js`, `renderer/inventory.js`, i18n `notifReqReply`, `reportMineHint`). Only **team** replies count: the backend's `myReports` now returns `replies` = comments by the repo's owner / members / collaborators, never the app's own account (its "closed by the reporter" note) — so the user's own comments on GitHub are not announced; the client compares `replies` (falls back to `comments` on an older server) and keeps its seen counts under a new key (`tigertag.reportSeen.v2.<uid>`) so nobody's baseline masks a reply.
- **Supporter badge (Ko-fi, Buy Me a Coffee, PayPal).** A payment on any of the three tied to an account gives it a golden-heart **Supporter** badge on the sidebar avatar and a thank-you notification — a thank-you, never a feature. Tied by the account's fixed **supporter code** `TS-XXXX-XXXX` the support menu shows (copy chip: "add your code to your payment message"; derived from the uid so it never changes, unlike the friend code — which still works as a fallback), else by the payer's email; anyone else claims it from the menu ("Already gave? Get your badge": payer email or transaction id). A supporter's menu thanks them instead of pitching.
  - Backend (`TigerTag_Firebase_Backend`): `functions/supportWebhooks.js` — `kofiWebhook` (Ko-fi `verification_token` vs secret `KOFI_VERIFICATION_TOKEN`), `bmcWebhook` (HMAC-SHA256 `x-signature-sha256` vs secret `BMC_WEBHOOK_SECRET`; both BMC payload shapes), `paypalIpn` (PayPal IPN, verified by post-back; completed incoming payments only, note in `memo`) — all record `supportDonations/{source}_{transactionId}` idempotently — and `claimSupport` (ID token; matches an unclaimed payment by lower-cased email or transaction id → `granted`/`not_found`/`taken`); `claimSupport { action: "code" }` returns the supporter code and registers it in `supporterCodes/{code}` (server-only); all set the server-only `users/{uid}.supporter = { since, lastAt, sources, monthly, count }` and, the first time, a `supporter_thanks` notification. `firestore.rules`: `supporter` joins `roles` / `tier` as refused from clients; `supportDonations` has no client rule. README documents both.
  - **TigerSquad** — the Hub's wall of fame, open to everyone, with a level the server computes: **Bronze** (joined for free), **Gold** (gave), **Silver** (20+ TigerTag chips of any kind — `tigerTagCount`) and **Platinum** (gave + 50+ certified) — certified chips are verified server-side with the TigerTag SDK (`certifyChip`, backend), one account per chip, plus **XP points** per physical chip (certified TigerTag+ 10, TigerTag+ 2, TigerTag 1 — twins count twice, TigerData 0) + 50 XP while in the TigerSquad (taken back on leaving), server-counted from the chip census AND the inventory spools (doc id = chip UID — so spools added before the census, imported or scanned on the phone earn too), once per UID and kept for good (a deleted spool keeps its points, a chip re-programmed on the same UID earns nothing new, only a verified certified backup lifts it to 10); Studio asks for a recount when the account's `xpCountVersion` is behind the current rules (`XP_COUNT_VERSION`), published as `tigerSquad.certified` / `tigerSquad.xp` for the Hub's maker ranking and shown under the name in the sidebar as an XP chip for every account ("★ 407 XP", live via a listener on the account doc — `_subscribeOwnSquad`) and in My profile ("407 XP · 19 certified TigerTag+", `_renderSquadXp`; a never-counted account asks `claimSupport { action: "xp" }` once). Join from the **TigerSquad invite** (accounts older than 7 days that haven't joined; 20 s after start-up, once a session; "Maybe later" / ✕ / Esc snooze 30 days per account; leaving the squad lifts the snooze). Redesigned "dark premium": title with a gradient "TigerSquad", three informative perk cards (+50 XP, badges Bronze → Platinum, back the project), each glowing in its own colour (radial light, tinted rim, twinkling sparkles) with a light idle animation (gift hops, badge sways, heart beats; livelier on hover, off under reduced motion), the four levels as large badges on an open strip (no boxed card; big arrows that light up one after the other, left to right, on a loop) with a short motto each, and "Discover the levels →" opening an info bubble anchored to it with the real rules + the XP scale (and a link to the wall of fame; closes on outside click / Esc), an optional coffee card (orange rim + glow, bigger cup, gradient button with a cup icon) that opens the support chooser anchored to its button without leaving the window (Esc / outside click close the chooser first; its "join" row is hidden there), a full-width saturated-orange free-join button (community icon, soft halo) that waits for the real write (loading → "Welcome… +50 XP" or an error), reassurance ticks, "Maybe later", a footer with the live TigerSquad member count bottom-left (`_squadMemberCount`: Firestore REST COUNT over `userProfiles` ordered on `tigerSquad.since`, cached 10 min, hidden when 0 or on error); 700 px / 90 vh with inner scrolling, focus trap + focus restore, 240/160 ms open/close, reduced-motion safe. Artwork slots `SQUAD_ASSETS` → `assets/img/tigersquad/` (README lists the expected WebP files; placeholders until then — in: the coffee cup `tigersquad-coffee.webp` (135×180, 10 KB) and the four level badges `tigersquad-bronze/silver/gold/platinum.webp` (160², 6–12 KB, cut from the founder's sheet, centred on a common scale) — masters in `assets-src/img/tigersquad/`) — `_openSquadInvite`, `tigerSquadRankBadgeHTML`, `tigerSquadCoffeeAssetHTML`; icons `community`, `coffee` + a colour trophy (`assets/img/tigersquad/trophy.svg`); perk cards use the founder's colour SVGs (`assets/img/tigersquad/perk-gift.svg`, `perk-badges.svg`, `perk-heart.svg`), a "Join the TigerSquad" row in the support menu (a member sees their badge, level and the head count instead, linking to the wall; swapped live while the menu is open) and a TigerSquad card under the coffee card of the requests panel (both open the invite — never a silent join; shown only to accounts not in the squad — a member sees instead their level badge, a "you're in" note, the squad's head count and a link to the wall; the card swaps live when the account joins, leaves or changes level) or My profile → TigerSquad switch (`eacSquadToggle`) — all write `users/{uid}.supportPublicly`. The level comes from the public profile (`userProfiles/{uid}.tigerSquad = { since, tier }`, live via `_subscribeOwnSquad`); members wear a ring and a corner heart in their level's colour, plus a "TigerSquad · <Level>" pill on the banners (not in the sidebar) (`.sq-bronze/silver/gold/platinum` CSS variables), on their own avatar and banner, in friends' lists and on the friend-view banner (`avatarMarkup` wraps the avatar for the heart; `squadTier` carried through the friends refresh, the sidebar list signature and `state.friendView`). Switching to an account with no cached profile clears the previous account's badges at once. My own banner shows the level pill too, and my avatar keeps its size inside the heart wrapper.
  - Studio: `state.supporter` (user doc + local-first cache, reset on sign-out), `renderSupporterBadge()`, supporter-code chip (`_supporterCode`, cached per account in `tigertag.supporterCode.<uid>`) + claim form in `_openCoffee` (`_claimSupport` → `claimSupport`), `supporter_thanks` row in the notification center — `renderer/inventory.js`, `renderer/css/10-settings.css`, `docs/firestore-schema.md`
- **Support the project: Buy Me a Coffee, Ko-fi or PayPal.** Every coffee button (sidebar, About, What's New, the requests card's coffee cards) opens a small chooser anchored next to it — Buy Me a Coffee (card, Apple / Google Pay), Ko-fi (`ko-fi.com/tigersystemio`, card or PayPal, one-off or monthly) or PayPal (`paypal.me/tigersystemio`) — for supporters who can't or won't use BMC; closes on pick, outside click or Esc. The repo's GitHub **Sponsor** button lists all three (`.github/FUNDING.yml`) — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_openCoffee`, `PAYPAL_URL`, `KOFI_URL`), `renderer/report/index.js`, `renderer/css/10-settings.css`, `assets/svg/logos/logo_paypal.svg`, `assets/svg/logos/logo_kofi.svg`
  - Reassures before it asks: it says the app is free and open source for good, built after hours by one maker with the community, a footer notes that payment happens on the platform's site (the card never reaches us) and offers a free alternative, a GitHub star. The About and What's New buttons are a neutral cup + "Support" pill instead of the Buy Me a Coffee wordmark (they open the three-platform chooser); support buttons get localised aria-labels; the requests card's coffee line drops the repeated "coffee" — `renderer/inventory.js` (`_openCoffee`, `GITHUB_REPO_URL`), `renderer/inventory.html`, `renderer/css/10-settings.css`, `renderer/css/70-detail-misc.css` (`.support-btn` replaces `.bmc-btn`), `renderer/css/60-modals.css`
  - Popover per the redesign mockup: title with a red heart, a short reassuring intro (free and open source for good), a caret pointing at the button that opened it, smart placement (right of the button when there is room, otherwise above, then below), the opener lit with a yellow ring while open, a ↗ hint on the hovered platform, lift-on-hover, 150 ms open / 120 ms close (off under reduced motion), options left-aligned (a global button rule was centring them); opened from the requests side card it sits to the right of the whole card (the topic list stays visible), or becomes a compact centred modal over a dimmed backdrop when there is no room

## v2.36.0 — 2026-10-10

### Added

- **In-app requests → public GitHub issues ("Improvements & suggestions").** A sidebar entry opens a left side card (`renderer/report/`) that slides out from behind the sidebar with the right-hand cards' motion (.25s translate, mirrored, inside the click-through clipping window `.rep-clip`). Not a modal: the draft (topic, text, attachments) survives close / reopen; once a topic is picked the request is *in progress* and the card's tab stays parked on the sidebar edge as **»** (and the sidebar entry stays lit) until it is sent, cancelled or abandoned (back to the topic list).
  - **Topics** (`KINDS`, same ids as the backend): Report a problem (`bug`), Suggest an idea (`idea`), Suggest an improvement (`enhancement`), Fix a product sheet (`catalog` — the material is picked with the Catalogue search: same index and words, exact barcode / SKU / id first; the issue carries link, id, brand, material, SKU, EAN), Suggest a brand (`new-brand` — name + validated website), Ask a question (`question`). Each GitHub issue gets its label + `from-app` and a `[Bug]` / `[Idea]`… title tag.
  - **Form**: per-topic title / lead / placeholders / send label, required marks (description required except for a brand), a Markdown toolbar (bold, italic, list, link, emoji picker), up to 5 attachments — images, window captures (`report:capture` → `webContents.capturePage()` with the card hidden) and PDFs (≤ 5 MB, max 2, `%PDF`-checked server-side) — by drop anywhere in the window (the `.ttag` import drop zone stands down while the card is open, with a full "Drop to attach" overlay), paste or file picker; images re-encoded client-side to JPEG ≤ 1920 px. A collapsible **Automatic information** panel shows exactly what travels with it: app version, current page ("Catalogue · Grid"), OS (`app:info.systemVersion`), window size, printer brands; opt-in recent error log; a mandatory **public** checkbox; no uid, name or email in the issue.
  - **Screenshot editor** (`renderer/report/annotator.js`, opens straight after a capture): every mark stays an object until Done (select, move, resize by handles, recolour, resize, delete, duplicate); move, pen (smoothed), eraser (cuts pen strokes, removes other objects), arrow, line, box, circle (⇧ square / circle / 45° snap), highlighter, inline text (double-click to edit, contrasting halo), auto-numbered steps, **blur** (pixelates private details); 7 colours, S/M/L/XL, undo / redo (80), shortcuts (V P E A L R O H T N B, 1–7, ⌘Z / ⇧⌘Z, ⌫, ⌘D, arrow nudges), contextual tips; exported at the image's own size.
  - **Screens** after the founder's mockup: hero + *Send feedback* / *My requests* tabs + six colour-tiled topic cards (hover: icon zoom, coloured border, chevron slide) + coffee card → form → *Sending…* (spinning ring, four steps ticked in turn, ≥ 300 ms each) → confirmation (drawn check, confetti that blur out, tracking `#issue` with copy, *See my requests* / *Send another one*, thank-you and coffee cards); screens slide horizontally (200 ms).
  - **My requests**: the account's requests with topic, title, `#issue`, age, reply count and status — Sent / In progress (label `in progress`) / Done / Not planned / Closed by you — a GitHub link and **close my own request** (two-step; comments and closes the issue as not planned). Statuses are read from GitHub on demand (sign-in + 8 s, and when the tab opens — no webhook); a change writes a `request_status` notification (`data: { reportId, number, status, title }`) worded by the app in the user's language (`notifReq_*`), opening *My requests* on click; reports without a known status are recorded silently.
  - **Backend** (TigerTag_Firebase_Backend `functions/reportIssue.js`): `reportIssue` (Firebase ID token in `X-Firebase-Id-Token` — a Bearer `Authorization` is inspected by Cloud Run itself and was once refused upstream — one retry with a forced refresh; 5 requests / account / 24 h; images re-encoded with sharp, EXIF dropped, stored at `bugReports/{id}/n.jpg` / `doc-n.pdf` with download tokens; issue opened with the fine-grained secret `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`; private trace in `bugReports/{id}`, quota in `bugReportQuota/{uid}`, both Admin-SDK only) and `myReports` (`list` with sync / owner-checked `close`).
- **Downloads** in the sidebar Resources — `tigersystem.io/<lang>/download` in the user's language when the site has it (fr, en, de, es, it, zh, pt, pl), else English.
- Creality printer settings: optional **Camera address** (`cameraUrl`, http/https) to use an MJPEG stream directly (rooted / custom setups).

### Changed

- **Sidebar footer** redesigned after the founder's mockup and palette: labelled **Resources** (Downloads, Wiki, Shop, GitHub, 3D Files, Discord) and **Support the project** (Buy me a coffee, Improvements & suggestions) groups of quiet rows — brand colour on the icon only (`#FF8A32`, `#FFC83D`, `#7ABF43`, `#E2E8F0`, `#00D4C7`, `#5865F2`), hover = lighter row + 2 px nudge + icon ×1.06 with a halo in its own colour (180 ms), the suggestions row warm-outlined with a little 8° sparkle; dark sidebar gradient `#101018 → #15111D → #1A1020`; **Settings** moved from the account dropdown to the footer, right of the version.
- One look for the spool card, the catalogue card and the product card (PR #47 by @Ptitlouis6012): print settings as icon tiles (density a tile, TD still editable), an icon on every Details row, a tag icon on price and SKU / EAN, the friend-view shop button fills its row.
- Product card: the colour HEX moves to a **Hex code** row under Colour in Product details (PR #46 by @Ptitlouis6012; `.icon-pipette` declared); the catalogue card's HEX row uses the pipette too.
- View switcher order: Inventory / Favorites / Lists / Catalogue / Printers (PR #45 by @Ptitlouis6012).
- View-switcher tooltips show at once (no 1 s dwell) and stay above the search field's orange + while fading out.

### Fixed

- **Creality K1 / K1C / K1 Max camera stayed black** (issue #38): no WebRTC on :8000 there — the camera is mjpg-streamer on :8080. K1-series models (catalog ids 6–9) probe `:8080/?action=snapshot` first and show `/?action=stream` (an `<img class="cre-cam-mjpeg">` beside each hidden `<video>`, remembered per IP); other models fall back to it when WebRTC signaling fails; side card, board, camera wall and detached window all follow.
- **Cloud Anycubic camera from the printers board** (PR #48 by @Ptitlouis6012, tested on a real Kobra 3 Max): play key offered for an online cloud Anycubic, loading dots while order 1001 + the Agora join run, closing the side panel no longer releases a camera the board plays, leaving the board releases the cloud cameras it started, one order-1001 request at a time (10 s pause after a failure), Agora clears its loading overlays; the camera wall rebuilds when a placed card flips offline ↔ live (all brands).

### Removed

- The sidebar's "TigerTag Mobile Apps" QR code and title (Downloads now leads to the apps).

### i18n

- Added the `report*` set (topics, hints, leads, labels, placeholders, send labels, editor tools and tips, attachments, automatic information, sending steps, confirmation, My requests, statuses), `notifReq_*`, `sbResources`, `sbSupport`, `sbDownloadBtn`, `roDetHex`, `printerLblCameraUrl`, `printerHintCrealityCameraUrl` — 11 locales. Removed `mobileApp`, `mobileAppShort`.

## v2.35.0 — 2026-10-09

### Added

- **Prusa printers (beta).** Every Prusa running PrusaLink — MK4/MK4S, MK3.9/MK3.9S, Core One, XL (1/2/5 tools), MINI/MINI+, MK3S+ / MK2.5S via Raspberry Pi — with or without MMU, with or without camera. LAN only (Prusa Connect has no public API). First shipped as `v2.35.0-beta.1`.
  - Transport in main: `prusa:http` (HTTP Digest auth with a cached challenge, optional `X-Api-Key`, tight path allowlist, GET/PUT/DELETE/HEAD, binary for thumbnails/snapshots) + `prusa:probe` (one unauthenticated `GET /api/version`: 401 Digest "Printer API" / PrusaLink server header / version body); `window.prusa` in `preload.js`.
  - Driver `renderer/printers/prusa/index.js`: 2 s `/api/v1/status` poll (state map incl. `ATTENTION`, nozzle/bed/chamber temps, progress, time left, fans, speed/flow), `/api/v1/job` on a new job + every 10 s (name, thumbnail → data URL, G-code metadata), `/api/v1/info` + `/api/version` once (MMU, serial, camera, firmware); reconnect backoff + re-find (`refind.js`, ARP MAC); auth / old-API / command errors as banners + one toast.
  - Slots: MMU → 5 bays (one "MMU" board unit), XL → T1…Tn from the model catalog `tools`, else E1; filled from the job's `filament_type` / `extruder_colour` metadata while it runs (PrusaLink reports no loaded filament).
  - Pause / resume / stop on the side card and the printer board (`controlJob`, hold-to-confirm 1.2 s / 1.5 s).
  - Camera: Buddy3D over local RTSP (`rtsp://<ip>/live`, ffmpeg → JPEG, `prusa:cam-start-rtsp`) or PrusaLink `/api/v1/cameras/snap` every 2 s; one feed per printer shared by side card, board and camera wall (not in the detached camera window yet).
  - Add flow: HTTP `/api/version` sweep of the local /24s + extra subnets, or manual `host[:port]`, then PrusaLink password, optional user, optional Buddy3D address. Model catalog `data/printers/prusa_printer_models.json`; placeholder brand mark + model image.
  - Marked beta: "Beta" tag in the add-printer picker (`meta.beta`), beta notice at the top of the live block.
- **Elegoo Centauri Carbon 1 (beta) — SDCP v3** as a second Elegoo transport (issue #41): `M99999` discovery on UDP 3000 (`elegoo:sdcp-discover` sweep + `elegoo:sdcp-probe`, in parallel with the CC2's MQTT probe), WebSocket `:3030/websocket` without auth from the renderer (`renderer/printers/elegoo/sdcp.js`): status (Cmd 0 + pushes) → state / progress / layers / time left / temps / fans / speed; attributes (Cmd 1, MAC kept for the IP re-find); camera (Cmd 386 → MJPEG :3031); pause / resume / stop (129 / 131 / 130 — not yet confirmed on firmware 1.4.x). Every exported entry point of `elegoo/index.js` hands over when `printer.protocol === "sdcp"`. CC1 form asks only the IP (`sdcpSchema`, writes `protocol: "sdcp"`); catalog entry "Centauri Carbon (Beta)"; `PROTOCOL.md` §25.
- **Per-printer settings schemas within a brand**: `schemaFor(printer | prefill)` on the brand + `fixed` values written with the doc; used by the form, the save, the scan multi-select (`scan-pick.js`, required fields asked per candidate) and multi-add (`_printerSchemaFor`, `printerRequiredFields(brand, prefill)`).
- **Catalogue product card.** A product opened from the Catalogue views gets its own side card (`#productCardPanel`, catalogue mode): illustration, identity + chips, "Already N in your inventory", a **Product sheet** button (opens the full product card beside it; a second press closes it), then a 2 × 2 grid of calls to action — **+ Inventory** (`_catalogCreate`), **+ Wishlist** (list picker), **+ Cart**, **+ Favorite** (✓ states) — then description, print settings, documents and details; no weight bar. ★ / cart / lists use the row "+ Inventory" would create (`catalogRow`), so they land on the same product record as the spools. Picking another product while the card is open slides the new card over a ghost of the old one (`_ghostSwapPanel`, ghost one z-slot under panels at z ≤ 100) and closes a product sheet left on the old product.
- **Catalogue — nothing found.** A search / filter set with no match shows an empty state with **Create it by hand**, opening the Add-product card (`[data-cv-create]` → `openAddProductPanel`).
- **Full catalogue data on catalogue spools.** Spools created from the catalogue, converted to TigerTag+ or refreshed from the API store `catalogProduct`: the local `id_catalog.json` entry enriched field by field with what `product/get` adds (description, documents, images, filament block, nozzle / dryer / bed, fan speeds, slicer profiles in `metadata`, brand logo / site, dates); the chip still gets only protocol fields (`_catalogFullRecord`, `_productApiFields`, `_refreshApiData`, `normalizeRow`). Documented in `docs/firestore-schema.md`, the backend `README.md` and the MCP data guide.
- **Favourite MasterSpools.** A ☆ on every MasterSpool in the container picker (`favorite: true` on `users/{uid}/containerOverrides/{id}`, beside the weight correction — resetting one keeps the other). Favourites get their own group at the top of the picker; a refill with favourites always asks, the picker narrowed to the favourites (any brand) + the brand's own MasterSpools, skipped only when the favourite is the brand's only MasterSpool (`containerIsFavorite`, `_cpToggleFavorite`, `_containerChoice`).
- **Container picker filters**: Cardboard / Plastic / MasterSpool chips under the search (one at a time, press again for all; reset on open; combined with the search and a narrowed list) — `_cpSetKind`, `CONTAINER_KIND`.

### Changed

- **One product card instead of two** (PR #39 by @Ptitlouis6012, then redesigned): "Product info" and "Product card" merged into one wide card — hero (illustration flush top-left, 236 px with a 20 px colour frame; identity; aspect / weight chips; HEX colour badges; 2-line description), two figures (**In stock** — "N spools" or "stock/minimum" — and **Estimated value**, stock × unit price HT/TTC), buy-link / price buttons, actions (+, list, to-order, ★) in a row beside the ✕ that scrolls with the content, then three tabs kept across re-renders: **Info** (print settings as 2 × 3 tiles; **Product details** — brand linked to its site with a globe, range, material, colour, net weight, product id → catalogue, type, diameter, fan speed; **Stock** — minimum, current, value; **Slicer profiles** — Bambu Studio / Creality Print, when known; note with a 0/500 counter; personal links), **Documents** (documents + video, orange count bubble on the tab) and **Notes** (tags, SKU / EAN, raw JSON in debug). Friend products keep the read-only card. i18n `reorderPanelTitle`, `reorderOpenProductCard` removed.
- **Container on a new spool** (`_containerChoice`, keyed on `brandId` == `id_brand`): not a refill → the brand's only container silently, several → picker narrowed to the brand's, none → narrowed to the generic ones ("Without MasterSpool" never offered); refill (`info1`) → the brand's only MasterSpool, several → narrowed to them, none → every MasterSpool. The narrowed picker says why and has "Show all"; questions are queued one at a time and only for spools added during the session. Replaces the old fallback to the first generic entry ("Without MasterSpool", 0 g).
- **Inventory reads like the catalogue** (issue #43): grid cards titled with the product name ("PETG HF - Blue" — the catalogue title for a catalogue spool, else "<series> - <colour>") over "Brand · Material" (`spoolCardTitle`, `spoolCardSub`); the table and grouped spools show the material with its series (`materialWithSeriesAspect`).
- Elegoo: the Centauri Carbon 2 is named as such everywhere (catalog typo "Centaury" fixed, tutorial), a Centauri found over MQTT is no longer mistaken for another model, the empty scan names the supported models.
- `docs:check` accepts prerelease versions.

### Fixed

- **Inventory search finds what the catalogue finds** (issue #43): one word-based search shared by the inventory, the Products views and the catalogue — every word must appear among brand, series, product name, material, aspects, colour, note, SKU / EAN, UID, tags, capacity — instead of the whole phrase in one field ("Bambu PETG HF" found nothing). Exact-code shortcut unchanged (`_searchTokens`, `_hayHasAll`, `_rowSearchHay`).
- **Product card showed "In stock: 0" for a catalogue product you own**, and a minimum / ★ set there landed on another record: the preview was built on a "PRODUCT_" row instead of as the TigerData+ the catalogue creates ("tt:<id>"). Now built on a chipless id, keyed by `_spoolProductKey`; rows synthesised from a product record carry `_productHash` and stock / row lookups match on it. A one-time `_healGhostProducts` merges the ghost records the bug left (flags OR-ed, larger minimum, empty fields filled, tags united) and deletes them.
- Printer board: dropping a printer group onto another no longer pushes the other printer's widgets down (PR #40 by @Ptitlouis6012; follow-up issue #42) — `_planSettleColumns`.

### i18n

- Added the Prusa set (`printerHintPrusa*`, `printerLblPrusa*`, `printerHelperPrusa*`, `prusa*`), `printerHintElegooSdcpIP`, `elgSdcpCmdRefused`, `betaTag`, `printerBetaNote`, `cpHint*`, `cpShowAll`, `cpFavorites`, `cpFavAdd`, `cpFavRemove`, `cpKindCardboard`, `cpKindPlastic`, `pcCta*`, `pcCatOwned`, `pcOpenSheet`, `det*` (fan, profiles, brand site, …), `sectionDescription`, the `ro*` product-card set, `roPanelSlicer`, `catEmptyTitle`, `catEmptySub`, `catEmptyCta` — 11 locales.
